`README.md`:

```md
# EAC/FN Spoofer Meme

Join discord for full files: https://discord.gg/GSFE2xpRtU

## What It Spoofs

SMBIOS
- System UUID, serial numbers (system, board, chassis, battery)
- BIOS vendor, version, release date
- Processor socket, manufacturer, CPUID values
- RAM size, type, speed, manufacturer, serial, part number

Disk
- Drive serial numbers (HDD/SSD/NVMe)
- GPT Disk GUID and partition GUIDs
- MBR disk signature
- Volume serials (all drives C–Z)
- SMART power-on hours and power cycle count
- NVMe NGUID/EUI64 identifiers
- USN journal IDs

Network
- MAC address (registry, kernel NDIS, CMOS,
k profile history (router MAC, saved SSIDs)

GPU
- NVIDIA GPU UUID
- AMD GPU serial
- PCI Device ID and Subsystem ID (all devices)
- GPU driver description in registry
- NVIDIA registry UUIDs (ClientUUID, PersistenceIdentifier)

CPU
- Vendor string, brand string, family/model/
- Hypervisor presence bit
- x2APIC topology ID
- Processor frequency info
- Microcode revision (MSR 0x8B)

EFI / ACPI
- EFI OfflineUniqueIDRandomSeed variable
- ACPI MSDM product key
- Boot identifier GUID
- ACPI table OEM IDs (DSDT, FACP, SSDT, APIC, HPET)

OS / Registry
- MachineGuid, HwProfileGuid, ComputerName
- Windows Product ID, Install date/time
- Advertising ID, DiagTrack DeviceId/ClientId
- Windows Defender MachineGuid
- SoftwareProtectionPlatform ClientMachineId
- Windows Update SusClientId, SQM MachineId
- Terminal Server and WER MachineIDs
- BIOS/CPU info in HARDWARE\DESCRIPTION registry

USB
- USB device serial numbers

```

```h
#ifndef _KERNEL_MODE
#include <time.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>

static ULONG g_SpoofSeed = 0;

static __inline void InitSpoofSeed(void) {
    if (g_SpoofSeed == 0) {
        LARGE_INTEGER counter;
        QueryPerformanceCounter(&counter);
        g_SpoofSeed = (ULONG)(counter.QuadPart ^ GetCurrentProcessId() ^ (ULONG)time(NULL));
    }
}

static __inline ULONG SpoofRand(ULONG* state) {
    ULONG x = *state;
    x ^= x << 13;
    x ^= x >> 17;
    x ^= x << 5;
    *state = x;
    return x;
}

static __inline void GenerateRandomSerial(char* out, int len, ULONG* seed) {
    const char charset[] = "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        out[i] = charset[SpoofRand(seed) % (sizeof(charset) - 1)];
    }
    out[len] = '\0';
}

static __inline void GenerateRandomMAC(UCHAR* out, ULONG* seed) {
    static const UCHAR ouiTable[][3] = {
        {0x00, 0x1B, 0x21},
        {0x8C, 0xEC, 0x4B},
        {0xF4, 0x4D, 0x30},
        {0xA0, 0x36, 0x9F},
        {0x48, 0x51, 0xB7},
        {0x00, 0xE0, 0x4C},
        {0x10, 0x02, 0xB5},
        {0x90, 0x2B, 0x34},
        {0xE0, 0x4F, 0x43},
        {0x40, 0xB0, 0x76},
        {0x18, 0xE7, 0x28},
        {0x60, 0x67, 0x20},
        {0x00, 0x90, 0x4C},
        {0xB8, 0x27, 0xEB},
        {0x00, 0x50, 0x43},
        {0x58, 0x47, 0xCA},
        {0x34, 0x02, 0x86},
        {0xAC, 0x17, 0x02},
        {0x00, 0x21, 0x6A},
        {0x5C, 0xE0, 0xC5},
        {0x20, 0xCF, 0x30},
        {0x74, 0xD4, 0x35},
        {0x28, 0xD2, 0x44},
        {0x00, 0x26, 0xB9},
    };
    ULONG idx = SpoofRand(seed) % (sizeof(ouiTable) / sizeof(ouiTable[0]));
    out[0] = ouiTable[idx][0];
    out[1] = ouiTable[idx][1];
    out[2] = ouiTable[idx][2];
    out[3] = (UCHAR)(SpoofRand(seed) & 0xFF);
    out[4] = (UCHAR)(SpoofRand(seed) & 0xFF);
    out[5] = (UCHAR)(SpoofRand(seed) & 0xFF);
}

static __inline void GenerateRandomUUID(UCHAR* out, ULONG* seed) {
    int i;
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
        out[i] = (UCHAR)(SpoofRand(seed) & 0xFF);
    }
    out[6] = (out[6] & 0x0F) | 0x40;
    out[8] = (out[8] & 0x3F) | 0x80;
}

static __inline void GenerateRandomGUID(WCHAR* out, ULONG* seed) {
    UCHAR uuid[16];
    GenerateRandomUUID(uuid, seed);
    swprintf_s(out, 64, L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
        uuid[0], uuid[1], uuid[2], uuid[3],
        uuid[4], uuid[5], uuid[6], uuid[7],
        uuid[8], uuid[9], uuid[10], uuid[11],
        uuid[12], uuid[13], uuid[14], uuid[15]);
}
#endif

#endif
```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_cpuid.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

BOOLEAN      g_VmxLaunched[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
VMX_CPU_CTX  g_VmxCpus[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
BOOLEAN      g_VmxGlobalEnabled = FALSE;
ULONG64 g_VmxonPhys[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
ULONG64 g_VmcsPhys[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
ULONG64 g_VmxMsrBitmapPhys[VMX_MAX_CPUS];

BOOLEAN         g_SvmLaunched[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
SVM_CPU_CTX     g_SvmCpus[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
BOOLEAN         g_SvmGlobalEnabled = FALSE;
ULONG64 g_SvmVmcbPhys[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
ULONG64 g_SvmHsavePhys[VMX_MAX_CPUS];
ULONG64 g_SvmMsrpmPhys[VMX_MAX_CPUS];

CPUID_SPOOF_CFG g_CpuidSpoof;

static ULONG VmxAdjustControls(ULONG desired, ULONG msrNum) {
    ULONG64 msr = __readmsr(msrNum);
    desired |= (ULONG)(msr & 0xFFFFFFFF);   /* force required-1 bits */
    desired &= (ULONG)(msr >> 32);           /* force required-0 bits */
    return desired;
}

#pragma pack(push, 1)
typedef struct { USHORT Limit; ULONG64 Base; } PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR;
#pragma pack(pop)

static ULONG VmxGetSegmentAR(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR entry;
    if (selector == 0) return 0x10000; /* unusable */
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    entry = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    return (ULONG)(entry[5]) | (((ULONG)(entry[6]) & 0xF0) << 8);
}

static ULONG64 VmxGetSegmentBase(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR e;
    ULONG64 base;
    if (selector == 0) return 0;
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    e = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    base = ((ULONG64)e[2]) | ((ULONG64)e[3] << 8) | ((ULONG64)e[4] << 16) | ((ULONG64)e[7] << 24);
    if ((e[5] & 0x10) == 0) /* system descriptor, 16-byte entry */
        base |= (ULONG64)(*(PULONG)(e + 8)) << 32;
    return base;
}

static ULONG VmxGetSegmentLimit(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR e;
    ULONG limit;
    if (selector == 0) return 0;
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    e = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    limit = (ULONG)(e[0]) | ((ULONG)e[1] << 8) | ((ULONG)(e[6] & 0x0F) << 16);
    if (e[6] & 0x80) limit = (limit << 12) | 0xFFF; /* G bit */
    return limit;
}

/* ============================================================
 * VT-x CPUID INTERCEPTION
 * ============================================================ */

VOID VmxHandleExit(PVOID pRegs) {
    typedef struct {
        ULONG64 Rax, Rcx, Rdx, Rbx, Rbp, Rsi, Rdi;
        ULONG64 R8, R9, R10, R11, R12, R13, R14, R15;
    } GUEST_REGS;

    GUEST_REGS* regs = (GUEST_REGS*)pRegs;
    ULONG64 exitReason = 0;
    __vmx_vmread(VMCS_EXIT_REASON, &exitReason);
    exitReason &= 0xFFFF;

    if (exitReason == EXIT_REASON_CPUID) {
        ULONG leaf    = (ULONG)regs->Rax;
        ULONG subleaf = (ULONG)regs->Rcx;
        int   info[4];

        __cpuidex(info, (int)leaf, (int)subleaf);
        regs->Rax = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[0];
        regs->Rbx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[1];
        regs->Rcx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[2];
        regs->Rdx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[3];

        if (g_CpuidSpoof.Active) {
            switch (leaf) {
            case 0x00000000:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEBX) regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEBX;
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEDX) regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEDX;
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.VendorECX) regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.VendorECX;
                break;
            case 0x00000001:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.HideHypervisor) regs->Rcx &= ~(1ULL << 31);
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.FakeLeaf1EAX)   regs->Rax = (ULONG64)g_CpuidSpoof.FakeLeaf1EAX;
                break;
            case 0x00000003:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.ZeroLeaf3) { regs->Rcx = 0; regs->Rdx = 0; }
                break;
            case 0x80000002:
                regs->Rax = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][0]; regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][1];
                regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][2]; regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][3];
                break;
            case 0x80000003:
                regs->Rax = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][0]; regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][1];
                regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][2]; regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][3];
                break;
            case 0x80000004:
                regs->Rax = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][0]; regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][1];
                regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][2]; regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][3];
                break;
            case 0x0000000B:
                /* Extended topology / x2APIC — zero EDX (per-processor x2APIC ID)
                 * to eliminate topology-based fingerprinting.                       */
                regs->Rdx = 0;
                break;
            case 0x00000016:
                /* Processor frequency info — zero all fields to prevent a mismatch
                 * against the spoofed brand string frequency (e.g. "@ 3.60GHz").   */
                regs->Rax = 0; regs->Rbx = 0; regs->Rcx = 0;
                break;
            case 0x40000000:
            case 0x40000001:
            case 0x40000002:
            case 0x40000003:
                /* Hypervisor identity leaves — return all zeros when hiding hypervisor
                 * so EAC's kernel component cannot detect our VT-x presence via these
                 * leaves (compliant guests skip them when bit 31 of leaf 1 ECX is clear,
                 * but EAC may enumerate them unconditionally).                          */
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.HideHypervisor)
                    { regs->Rax = 0; regs->Rbx = 0; regs->Rcx = 0; regs->Rdx = 0; }
                break;
            }
        }

        /* Advance guest RIP past CPUID (2 bytes) */
        {
            ULONG64 rip = 0;
            __vmx_vmread(VMCS_GUEST_RIP, &rip);
            __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_RIP, rip + 2);
        }
    } else if (exitReason == EXIT_REASON_RDMSR) {
        /* MSR bitmap triggered for MSR 0x8B (IA32_BIOS_SIGN_ID).
         * Return 0 (no microcode revision) when CPUID spoofing is active — this
         * prevents EAC's kernel component from detecting a model/microcode mismatch
         * when FakeLeaf1EAX changes the reported CPU family/model.                   */
        ULONG   msrIndex = (ULONG)regs->Rcx;
        ULONG64 msrValue = 0;
        if (msrIndex == 0x8BUL) {
            /* IA32_BIOS_SIGN_ID: return 0 (no microcode revision) when spoofing is
             * active to avoid model/microcode mismatch; pass-through otherwise. */
            msrValue = g_CpuidSpoof.Active ? 0 : __readmsr(0x8B);
        }
        /* Any other index: bitmap should only intercept 0x8B; return 0 for safety
         * rather than calling __readmsr on an unexpected index (avoids #GP in host). */
        regs->Rax = (ULONG64)(ULONG)(msrValue & 0xFFFFFFFFULL);
        regs->Rdx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)(msrValue >> 32);
        {
            ULONG64 rip = 0;
            __vmx_vmread(VMCS_GUEST_RIP, &rip);
            __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_RIP, rip + 2); /* RDMSR = 0F 32 = 2 bytes */
        }
    }
}

static NTSTATUS VmxEnableCpu(ULONG cpuIndex) {
    VMX_CPU_CTX* ctx = &g_VmxCpus[cpuIndex];
    ULONG64 vmxBasic, revId;
    ULONG64 cr0, cr4, cr0fixed0, cr0fixed1, cr4fixed0, cr4fixed1;
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr, idtr;
    USHORT trSel;
    ULONG pinCtls, cpuCtls, exitCtls, entryCtls;
    PHYSICAL_ADDRESS physAddr;
    PVOID hostStackTop;

    /* Check VMX support — AMD CPUs will fail here; CPUID spoof gracefully skipped */
    {
        int cpuinfo[4];
        __cpuid(cpuinfo, 0);
        /* Leaf 0 EBX/EDX/ECX spell "GenuineIntel" or "AuthenticAMD" */
        __cpuid(cpuinfo, 1);
        if (!(cpuinfo[2] & (1 << 5))) {
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] VmxEnableCpu[%u]: VMX not available (AMD or disabled in BIOS) — CPUID spoof skipped\n",
                cpuIndex);
            return STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
        }
    }

    vmxBasic = __readmsr(MSR_IA32_VMX_BASIC);
    revId    = vmxBasic & 0x7FFFFFFF;

    /* Enable VMX in CR4 */
    cr4 = __readcr4();
    if (!(cr4 & (1ULL << 13))) __writecr4(cr4 | (1ULL << 13));

    /* Fix CR0 required bits */
    cr0       = __readcr0();
    cr0fixed0 = __readmsr(MSR_IA32_VMX_CR0_FIXED0);
    cr0fixed1 = __readmsr(MSR_IA32_VMX_CR0_FIXED1);
    cr0 = (cr0 | cr0fixed0) & cr0fixed1;
    __writecr0(cr0);

    cr4fixed0 = __readmsr(MSR_IA32_VMX_CR4_FIXED0);
    cr4fixed1 = __readmsr(MSR_IA32_VMX_CR4_FIXED1);
    cr4 = __readcr4();
    cr4 = (cr4 | cr4fixed0) & cr4fixed1;
    __writecr4(cr4);

    /* Check/set IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL */
    {
        ULONG64 fc = __readmsr(MSR_IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL);
        if (!(fc & IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL_LOCKED)) {
            __writemsr(MSR_IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL,
                       fc | IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL_VMX_OUTSIDE_SMX |
                       IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL_LOCKED);
        } else if (!(fc & IA32_FEATURE_CONTROL_VMX_OUTSIDE_SMX)) {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] VMX disabled by BIOS/firmware on CPU %u\n", cpuIndex);
            return STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
        }
    }

    /* All four regions pre-allocated at PASSIVE_LEVEL by VmxPreallocateResources()
     * before KeIpiGenericCall — ExAllocatePool is illegal above DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (!ctx->VmxonVa || !ctx->VmcsVa || !ctx->HostStackBase || !ctx->MsrBitmapVa) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] VmxEnableCpu[%u]: resources not pre-allocated\n", cpuIndex);
        return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
    }

    RtlZeroMemory(ctx->VmxonVa, PAGE_SIZE);
    *(PULONG)ctx->VmxonVa = (ULONG)revId;

    physAddr.QuadPart = g_VmxonPhys[cpuIndex];
    if (__vmx_on((PULONG64)&physAddr.QuadPart) != 0) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] VMXON failed on CPU %u\n", cpuIndex);
        return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
    }

    RtlZeroMemory(ctx->VmcsVa, PAGE_SIZE);
    *(PULONG)ctx->VmcsVa = (ULONG)revId;

    physAddr.QuadPart = g_VmcsPhys[cpuIndex];
    __vmx_vmclear((PULONG64)&physAddr.QuadPart);
    __vmx_vmptrld((PULONG64)&physAddr.QuadPart);
    /* HOST_RSP = top of stack, 16-byte aligned (VMEXIT stores no retaddr, but
     * our handler does sub rsp,78h so we need some headroom above HOST_RSP). */
    hostStackTop = (PVOID)((ULONG_PTR)ctx->HostStackBase +
                            VMX_STACK_PAGES * PAGE_SIZE - 0x10);

    /* ---- Compute control values ---- */
    {
        BOOLEAN useTrueCtls = (vmxBasic >> 55) & 1;
        ULONG pinMsr  = useTrueCtls ? 0x48D : MSR_IA32_VMX_PINBASED_CTLS;
        ULONG cpuMsr  = useTrueCtls ? 0x48E : MSR_IA32_VMX_PROCBASED_CTLS;
        ULONG exitMsr = useTrueCtls ? 0x48F : MSR_IA32_VMX_EXIT_CTLS;
        ULONG entMsr  = useTrueCtls ? 0x490 : MSR_IA32_VMX_ENTRY_CTLS;

        pinCtls   = VmxAdjustControls(0, pinMsr);
        cpuCtls   = VmxAdjustControls(CPU_BASED_CPUID_EXITING | CPU_BASED_USE_MSR_BITMAPS, cpuMsr);
        exitCtls  = VmxAdjustControls(VM_EXIT_HOST_64BIT, exitMsr);
        entryCtls = VmxAdjustControls(VM_ENTRY_IA32E_GUEST, entMsr);
    }

    /* ---- Read current CPU state ---- */
    cr0   = __readcr0();
    cr4   = __readcr4();
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    __sidt(&idtr);
    trSel = VmxReadTR();

    /* ---- Write VMCS control fields ---- */
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_PIN_CTLS,   pinCtls);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_CPU_CTLS,   cpuCtls);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_EXC_BITMAP, 0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_EXIT_CTLS,  exitCtls);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_EXIT_MSR_ST, 0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_EXIT_MSR_LD, 0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_ENTRY_CTLS, entryCtls);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_ENTRY_MSR_LD, 0);
    /* Configure MSR bitmap — zero = pass-through, set bit to intercept specific RDMSR.
     * Read-low bitmap covers MSRs 0x00000000-0x00001FFF at offset 0 of the 4KB page.
     * MSR 0x8B (IA32_BIOS_SIGN_ID, microcode revision): byte 0x11, bit 3.
     * Intercepting this prevents a microcode/brand-string mismatch when FakeLeaf1EAX
     * changes the reported CPU model. */
    RtlZeroMemory(ctx->MsrBitmapVa, PAGE_SIZE);
    ((PUCHAR)ctx->MsrBitmapVa)[0x8B / 8] |= (UCHAR)(1U << (0x8B & 7));
    physAddr.QuadPart = g_VmxMsrBitmapPhys[cpuIndex];
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_MSR_BITMAP, physAddr.QuadPart);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_CR0_MASK,   0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_CR4_MASK,   0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_CR0_SHADOW, cr0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_CR4_SHADOW, cr4);

    /* ---- Guest state ---- */
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CR0,   cr0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CR3,   __readcr3());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CR4,   cr4);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_DR7,   0x400);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_RFLAGS, __readeflags());

    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CS_SEL,   VmxReadCS());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SS_SEL,   VmxReadSS());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_DS_SEL,   VmxReadDS());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_ES_SEL,   VmxReadES());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_FS_SEL,   VmxReadFS());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_GS_SEL,   VmxReadGS());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_TR_SEL,   trSel);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_LDTR_SEL, 0);

    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CS_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(VmxReadCS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SS_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(VmxReadSS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_DS_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(VmxReadDS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_ES_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(VmxReadES()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_FS_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(VmxReadFS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_GS_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(VmxReadGS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_TR_LIM,   VmxGetSegmentLimit(trSel));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_LDTR_LIM, 0xFFFF);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_GDTR_LIM, gdtr.Limit);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_IDTR_LIM, idtr.Limit);

    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CS_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(VmxReadCS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SS_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(VmxReadSS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_DS_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(VmxReadDS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_ES_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(VmxReadES()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_FS_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(VmxReadFS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_GS_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(VmxReadGS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_TR_AR,   VmxGetSegmentAR(trSel));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_LDTR_AR, 0x10000);  /* unusable */

    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_CS_BASE,   VmxGetSegmentBase(VmxReadCS()));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SS_BASE,   0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_DS_BASE,   0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_ES_BASE,   0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_FS_BASE,   __readmsr(MSR_IA32_FS_BASE));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_GS_BASE,   __readmsr(MSR_IA32_GS_BASE));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_TR_BASE,   VmxGetSegmentBase(trSel));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_LDTR_BASE, 0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_GDTR_BASE, gdtr.Base);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_IDTR_BASE, idtr.Base);

    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SYSEN_CS, __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_CS));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SYSEN_SP, __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_ESP));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_SYSEN_IP, __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_EIP));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_DEBUGCTL, __readmsr(MSR_IA32_DEBUGCTL));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_VMCS_LINK, (ULONG64)-1);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_INTR_ST,  0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_ACTIVITY, 0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_GUEST_PEND_DBG, 0);

    /* ---- Host state ---- */
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_CR0, cr0);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_CR3, __readcr3());
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_CR4, cr4);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_CS_SEL, VmxReadCS() & ~3U);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_SS_SEL, VmxReadSS() & ~3U);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_DS_SEL, VmxReadDS() & ~3U);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_ES_SEL, VmxReadES() & ~3U);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_FS_SEL, VmxReadFS() & ~3U);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_GS_SEL, VmxReadGS() & ~3U);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_TR_SEL, trSel & ~3);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_FS_BASE,   __readmsr(MSR_IA32_FS_BASE));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_GS_BASE,   __readmsr(MSR_IA32_GS_BASE));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_TR_BASE,   VmxGetSegmentBase(trSel));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_GDTR_BASE, gdtr.Base);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_IDTR_BASE, idtr.Base);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_SYSEN_CS,  __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_CS));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_SYSEN_SP,  __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_ESP));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_SYSEN_IP,  __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_EIP));
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_RSP,       (ULONG64)hostStackTop);
    __vmx_vmwrite(VMCS_HOST_RIP,       (ULONG64)(ULONG_PTR)VmxExitHandler);

    /* GUEST_RSP and GUEST_RIP are written by VmxLaunchContext (assembly) */
    g_VmxLaunched[cpuIndex] = FALSE;
    VmxLaunchContext(cpuIndex);

    if (g_VmxLaunched[cpuIndex]) {
        ctx->Launched = TRUE;
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] VT-x enabled on CPU %u\n", cpuIndex);
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }
    {
        ULONG64 errCode = 0;
        __vmx_vmread(0x4400 /* VM_INSTRUCTION_ERROR */, &errCode);
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] VMLAUNCH failed on CPU %u — VM_INSTRUCTION_ERROR=%llu"
            " (check Intel SDM Vol.3 Table C-1)\n", cpuIndex, errCode);
    }
    __vmx_off();
    return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
}

static ULONG_PTR VmxIpiCallback(ULONG_PTR Context) {
    ULONG cpu = KeGetCurrentProcessorNumber();
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (cpu < VMX_MAX_CPUS) VmxEnableCpu(cpu);
    return 0;
}

static NTSTATUS VmxPreallocateResources(VOID) {
    ULONG i;
    ULONG cpuCount = KeQueryActiveProcessorCount(NULL);
    if (cpuCount > VMX_MAX_CPUS) cpuCount = VMX_MAX_CPUS;
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] VmxPreallocateResources: %u active CPU(s)\n", cpuCount);
    for (i = 0; i < cpuCount; i++) {
        VMX_CPU_CTX* ctx = &g_VmxCpus[i];
        if (!ctx->VmxonVa) {
            ctx->VmxonVa = SafeAllocatePool(PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->VmxonVa) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        if (!ctx->VmcsVa) {
            ctx->VmcsVa = SafeAllocatePool(PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->VmcsVa) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        if (!ctx->HostStackBase) {
            ctx->HostStackBase = SafeAllocatePool(VMX_STACK_PAGES * PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->HostStackBase) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        if (!ctx->MsrBitmapVa) {
            ctx->MsrBitmapVa = SafeAllocatePool(PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->MsrBitmapVa) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        /* Cache physical addresses now at PASSIVE_LEVEL — MmGetPhysicalAddress
         * dereferences MiVisibleState which is invalid at IPI_LEVEL → 0xD1. */
        g_VmxonPhys[i]       = MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx->VmxonVa).QuadPart;
        g_VmcsPhys[i]        = MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx->VmcsVa).QuadPart;
        g_VmxMsrBitmapPhys[i] = MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx->MsrBitmapVa).QuadPart;
    }
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

static NTSTATUS VmxEnableAll(VOID) {
    ULONG i, launched = 0, total;
    NTSTATUS st = VmxPreallocateResources();
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] VmxPreallocateResources failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return st;
    }
    KeIpiGenericCall(VmxIpiCallback, 0);
    total = KeQueryActiveProcessorCount(NULL);
    if (total > VMX_MAX_CPUS) total = VMX_MAX_CPUS;
    for (i = 0; i < total; i++) if (g_VmxCpus[i].Launched) launched++;
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] VmxEnableAll: %u/%u CPUs launched VT-x successfully\n", launched, total);
    if (!launched) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] VmxEnableAll: no CPUs launched — CPUID spoof inactive"
            " (check BIOS Virtualization Technology setting)\n");
    }
    return launched ? STATUS_SUCCESS : STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
}

static VOID VmxDisableCpu(ULONG cpuIndex) {
    VMX_CPU_CTX* ctx = &g_VmxCpus[cpuIndex];
    if (!ctx->Launched) return;
    __vmx_off();
    ctx->Launched = FALSE;
}

static ULONG_PTR VmxDisableIpiCallback(ULONG_PTR Context) {
    ULONG cpu = KeGetCurrentProcessorNumber();
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (cpu < VMX_MAX_CPUS) VmxDisableCpu(cpu);
    return 0;
}

static VOID VmxDisableAll(VOID) {
    ULONG i;
    if (!g_VmxGlobalEnabled) return;
    KeIpiGenericCall(VmxDisableIpiCallback, 0);
    for (i = 0; i < VMX_MAX_CPUS; i++) {
        if (g_VmxCpus[i].VmxonVa)     { ExFreePoolWithTag(g_VmxCpus[i].VmxonVa,     POOL_TAG_SPOOF); g_VmxCpus[i].VmxonVa     = NULL; }
        if (g_VmxCpus[i].VmcsVa)      { ExFreePoolWithTag(g_VmxCpus[i].VmcsVa,      POOL_TAG_SPOOF); g_VmxCpus[i].VmcsVa      = NULL; }
        if (g_VmxCpus[i].HostStackBase){ ExFreePoolWithTag(g_VmxCpus[i].HostStackBase,POOL_TAG_SPOOF); g_VmxCpus[i].HostStackBase= NULL; }
    }
    g_VmxGlobalEnabled = FALSE;
}

/* ============================================================
 * AMD SVM CPUID INTERCEPTION
 * ============================================================ */

/* Assembly stub — defined in spoofer_vmx.asm */
VOID SvmLaunchContext(ULONG cpuIndex, ULONG64 vmcbPhys, ULONG64 hostRsp);

/* VMCB helpers — write field at byte offset within the 4 KB VMCB page */
static VOID VmcbWrite8 (PVOID vmcb, ULONG off, UCHAR  v) { *(PUCHAR) ((PUCHAR)vmcb + off) = v; }
static VOID VmcbWrite16(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off, USHORT v) { *(PUSHORT)((PUCHAR)vmcb + off) = v; }
static VOID VmcbWrite32(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off, ULONG  v) { *(PULONG) ((PUCHAR)vmcb + off) = v; }
static VOID VmcbWrite64(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off, ULONG64 v){ *(PULONG64)((PUCHAR)vmcb + off) = v; }
static ULONG   VmcbRead32(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off)         { return *(PULONG)  ((PUCHAR)vmcb + off); }
static ULONG64 VmcbRead64(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off)         { return *(PULONG64)((PUCHAR)vmcb + off); }

/* VmxGetSegmentAR returns Intel VMX format: {G,D/B,L,AVL} in bits [15:12].
 * AMD VMCB wants those same flags in bits [11:8] (bits [15:12] reserved/zero).
 * Without this conversion the guest CS has L=0 → runs in 32-bit compat mode
 * → SvmGuestContinue executes 64-bit code as 32-bit → immediate triple fault
 * → hardware system reset (no BSOD) if SHUTDOWN is not intercepted. */
static USHORT SvmGetSegmentAR(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR entry;
    if (selector == 0) return 0; /* P=0 = unusable in AMD VMCB */
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    entry = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    /* byte5 = {P,DPL,S,Type} → bits[7:0]; byte6 upper nibble = {G,D/B,L,AVL} → bits[11:8] */
    return (USHORT)((USHORT)(entry[5]) | (((USHORT)(entry[6]) & 0xF0U) << 4));
}

/* Write a segment descriptor into the VMCB at the given base offset.
 * Each segment slot in the VMCB is 16 bytes:
 *   +0: USHORT selector
 *   +2: USHORT attrib (AMD packed AR: {G,D/B,L,AVL} at bits [11:8])
 *   +4: ULONG  limit
 *   +8: ULONG64 base
 */
static VOID VmcbWriteSeg(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off, USHORT sel) {
    USHORT ar;
    ULONG  lim;
    ULONG64 base;
    if (sel == 0) {
        VmcbWrite16(vmcb, off + 0, 0);
        VmcbWrite16(vmcb, off + 2, 0);
        VmcbWrite32(vmcb, off + 4, 0);
        VmcbWrite64(vmcb, off + 8, 0);
        return;
    }
    ar   = SvmGetSegmentAR(sel);
    lim  = VmxGetSegmentLimit(sel);
    base = VmxGetSegmentBase(sel);
    VmcbWrite16(vmcb, off + 0, sel);
    VmcbWrite16(vmcb, off + 2, ar);
    VmcbWrite32(vmcb, off + 4, lim);
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, off + 8, base);
}

static VOID VmcbWriteDescriptor(PVOID vmcb, ULONG off, ULONG64 base, ULONG limit) {
    VmcbWrite16(vmcb, off + 0, 0);
    VmcbWrite16(vmcb, off + 2, 0);
    VmcbWrite32(vmcb, off + 4, limit);
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, off + 8, base);
}

/* Called from SvmExitHandler (assembly).
 * regs points at the GPR save area on the host stack (same layout as GUEST_REGS).
 * vmcbVa is the guest VMCB virtual address. */
/* Returns 0 = re-enter guest, 1 = bail out (SvmExitLoop must NOT re-issue vmrun). */
ULONG SvmHandleExit(PVOID pRegs, PVOID vmcbVa) {
    typedef struct {
        ULONG64 Rax, Rcx, Rdx, Rbx, Rbp, Rsi, Rdi;
        ULONG64 R8, R9, R10, R11, R12, R13, R14, R15;
    } GUEST_REGS;

    GUEST_REGS* regs = (GUEST_REGS*)pRegs;
    ULONG64 exitCode = VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_EXITCODE);

    /* VMEXIT_INVALID (-1) means VMCB consistency checks failed.  Signal bail-out
     * so SvmExitLoop restores the original stack and returns instead of re-issuing
     * vmrun with SVME cleared (which gives #UD → 0x139 on the SVM host stack). */
    if (exitCode == (ULONG64)-1) {
        ULONG64 efer = __readmsr(MSR_EFER);
        __writemsr(MSR_EFER, efer & ~EFER_SVME);
        return 1;
    }

    if (exitCode == SVM_VMEXIT_CPUID) {
        ULONG leaf    = (ULONG)VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RAX); /* host RAX undefined after VMEXIT; guest RAX is in VMCB */
        ULONG subleaf = (ULONG)regs->Rcx;
        int   info[4];

        __cpuidex(info, (int)leaf, (int)subleaf);
        regs->Rax = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[0];
        regs->Rbx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[1];
        regs->Rcx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[2];
        regs->Rdx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)info[3];

        if (g_CpuidSpoof.Active) {
            switch (leaf) {
            case 0x00000000:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEBX) regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEBX;
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEDX) regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.VendorEDX;
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.VendorECX) regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.VendorECX;
                break;
            case 0x00000001:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.HideHypervisor) regs->Rcx &= ~(1ULL << 31);
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.FakeLeaf1EAX)   regs->Rax = (ULONG64)g_CpuidSpoof.FakeLeaf1EAX;
                break;
            case 0x00000003:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.ZeroLeaf3) { regs->Rcx = 0; regs->Rdx = 0; }
                break;
            case 0x80000002:
                regs->Rax = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][0]; regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][1];
                regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][2]; regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[0][3];
                break;
            case 0x80000003:
                regs->Rax = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][0]; regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][1];
                regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][2]; regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[1][3];
                break;
            case 0x80000004:
                regs->Rax = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][0]; regs->Rbx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][1];
                regs->Rcx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][2]; regs->Rdx = g_CpuidSpoof.Brand[2][3];
                break;
            case 0x0000000B:
                regs->Rdx = 0;
                break;
            case 0x00000016:
                regs->Rax = 0; regs->Rbx = 0; regs->Rcx = 0;
                break;
            case 0x40000000:
            case 0x40000001:
            case 0x40000002:
            case 0x40000003:
                if (g_CpuidSpoof.HideHypervisor)
                    { regs->Rax = 0; regs->Rbx = 0; regs->Rcx = 0; regs->Rdx = 0; }
                break;
            }
        }

        /* Advance guest RIP past CPUID (2 bytes) */
        {
            ULONG64 rip = VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RIP);
            VmcbWrite64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RIP, rip + 2);
        }
        /* Write back RAX (other GPRs are in the regs frame; RAX is also in VMCB) */
        VmcbWrite64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RAX, regs->Rax);
        /* Mark VMCB clean bits as dirty so hardware re-reads guest state */
        VmcbWrite32(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_CLEAN_BITS, 0);
    } else if (exitCode == SVM_VMEXIT_MSR) {
        /* MSRPM triggered for MSR 0x8B (IA32_BIOS_SIGN_ID).
         * EXITINFO1 = 0 for RDMSR, 1 for WRMSR — we only set the read intercept bit. */
        ULONG64 exitInfo1 = VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_EXITINFO1);
        if (exitInfo1 == 0) { /* RDMSR */
            ULONG   msrIndex = (ULONG)regs->Rcx;
            ULONG64 msrValue = 0;
            if (msrIndex == 0x8BUL) {
                msrValue = g_CpuidSpoof.Active ? 0 : __readmsr(0x8B);
            }
            /* Any other index: MSRPM should only intercept 0x8B; return 0 safely. */
            regs->Rax = (ULONG64)(ULONG)(msrValue & 0xFFFFFFFFULL);
            regs->Rdx = (ULONG64)(ULONG)(msrValue >> 32);
            VmcbWrite64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RAX, regs->Rax);
        }
        /* Advance RIP past RDMSR/WRMSR (2 bytes) for both read and write cases. */
        {
            ULONG64 rip = VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RIP);
            VmcbWrite64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RIP, rip + 2);
        }
        VmcbWrite32(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_CLEAN_BITS, 0);
        return 0;
    } else {
        /* Unhandled exit — fire a diagnostic BSOD so we can read the exit code
         * from the minidump instead of hanging silently.
         * BugCheck params: [0]=exitCode [1]=cpuIndex [2]=EXITINFO1 [3]=EXITINFO2
         * If you see 0xDEAD0SVM in the dump, look at param 0 for the VMEXIT code:
         *   0x12 = VMEXIT_WRITE_CR2  (wrong intercept offset — still 0x000)
         *   0x62 = VMEXIT_INIT       (unexpected INIT IPI)
         *   0x7F = VMEXIT_SHUTDOWN   (triple fault in guest)
         *   other = check AMD APM Table 15-10 */
        ULONG64 efer     = __readmsr(MSR_EFER);
        ULONG64 info1    = VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_EXITINFO1);
        ULONG64 info2    = VmcbRead64(vmcbVa, VMCB_OFF_EXITINFO2);
        __writemsr(MSR_EFER, efer & ~EFER_SVME);
        KeBugCheckEx(0xDEAD05BF,
                     (ULONG_PTR)exitCode,
                     (ULONG_PTR)(ULONG)regs->Rcx,  /* cpuIndex slot / MSR index */
                     (ULONG_PTR)info1,
                     (ULONG_PTR)info2);
        return 1; /* unreachable */
    }
    return 0;
}

static NTSTATUS SvmEnableCpu(ULONG cpuIndex) {
    SVM_CPU_CTX* ctx = &g_SvmCpus[cpuIndex];
    PVOID vmcb, hsave;
    PHYSICAL_ADDRESS physVmcb, physHsave;
    ULONG64 efer, vmCr;
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr, idtr;
    USHORT trSel;
    PVOID hostStackTop;

    /* Check SVM support: CPUID 0x80000001 ECX bit 2 */
    {
        int cpuinfo[4];
        __cpuid(cpuinfo, (int)0x80000001);
        if (!(cpuinfo[2] & (1 << 2))) {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmEnableCpu[%u]: SVM not supported by CPU\n", cpuIndex);
            return STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
        }
    }

    /* Check VM_CR.SVMDIS — BIOS may have disabled SVM */
    vmCr = __readmsr(MSR_VM_CR);
    if (vmCr & VM_CR_SVMDIS) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmEnableCpu[%u]: SVM disabled by BIOS (VM_CR.SVMDIS=1)\n", cpuIndex);
        return STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
    }

    if (!ctx->VmcbVa || !ctx->HostSaveVa || !ctx->HostStackBase || !ctx->MsrpmVa) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmEnableCpu[%u]: resources not pre-allocated\n", cpuIndex);
        return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
    }

    vmcb   = ctx->VmcbVa;
    hsave  = ctx->HostSaveVa;
    physVmcb.QuadPart  = g_SvmVmcbPhys[cpuIndex];
    physHsave.QuadPart = g_SvmHsavePhys[cpuIndex];

    /* Enable SVME in EFER */
    efer = __readmsr(MSR_EFER);
    __writemsr(MSR_EFER, efer | EFER_SVME);

    /* Write host save area PA */
    __writemsr(MSR_VM_HSAVE_PA, physHsave.QuadPart);

    /* Zero both regions */
    RtlZeroMemory(vmcb,  PAGE_SIZE);
    RtlZeroMemory(hsave, PAGE_SIZE);

    /* ---- VMCB control area ---- */
    /* Intercept CPUID and MSR accesses via MSRPM.
     * Both bits live in the same MISC1 DWORD (offset 0x00):
     *   bit 18 = CPUID, bit 28 = MSR_PROT (consult MSRPM for RDMSR/WRMSR). */
    VmcbWrite32(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_INTERCEPT_MISC1, SVM_INTERCEPT_CPUID | SVM_INTERCEPT_MSR_PROT | SVM_INTERCEPT_SHUTDOWN);
    VmcbWrite32(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_INTERCEPT_MISC2, SVM_INTERCEPT_VMRUN); /* mandatory per AMD APM */

    /* Configure MSRPM (8KB, zero = pass-through, set bit for RDMSR intercept).
     * Each MSR has 2 bits (bit 0 = RDMSR intercept, bit 1 = WRMSR intercept).
     * MSR 0x8B (IA32_BIOS_SIGN_ID): bit index = 0x8B*2 = 278 → byte 34, bit 6. */
    {
        PHYSICAL_ADDRESS physMsrpm;
        RtlZeroMemory(ctx->MsrpmVa, PAGE_SIZE * 2);
        ((PUCHAR)ctx->MsrpmVa)[(0x8B * 2) / 8] |= (UCHAR)(1U << ((0x8B * 2) & 7));
        physMsrpm.QuadPart = g_SvmMsrpmPhys[cpuIndex];
        VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_MSRPM_BASE, physMsrpm.QuadPart);
    }

    /* Guest ASID must be non-zero; use cpuIndex+1 */
    VmcbWrite32(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_ASID, cpuIndex + 1);
    /* TLB control: flush this guest's TLB on VMRUN (value 1) */
    VmcbWrite32(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_TLB_CTRL, 1);

    /* No nested paging, no AVIC */
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_NP_ENABLE, 0);

    /* ---- VMCB guest state area ---- */
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    __sidt(&idtr);
    trSel = VmxReadTR();

    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_CS_SEL, VmxReadCS());
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_SS_SEL, VmxReadSS());
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_DS_SEL, VmxReadDS());
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_ES_SEL, VmxReadES());
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_FS_SEL, VmxReadFS());
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_GS_SEL, VmxReadGS());
    /* GDT descriptors for FS/GS have base=0 in x64; real bases live in MSRs.
     * Override the base fields written by VmcbWriteSeg with the MSR values. */
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_FS_SEL + 8, __readmsr(0xC0000100)); /* IA32_FS_BASE */
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_GS_SEL + 8, __readmsr(0xC0000101)); /* IA32_GS_BASE */
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_TR,     trSel);
    VmcbWriteSeg(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_LDTR,   0);
    VmcbWriteDescriptor(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_GDTR, gdtr.Base, gdtr.Limit);
    VmcbWriteDescriptor(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_IDTR, idtr.Base, idtr.Limit);

    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_EFER,   __readmsr(MSR_EFER));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_CR0,    __readcr0());
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_CR3,    __readcr3());
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_CR4,    __readcr4());
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_DR7,    0x400);
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_DR6,    0xFFFF0FF0);
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_RFLAGS, __readeflags());

    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_STAR,       __readmsr(0xC0000081));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_LSTAR,      __readmsr(0xC0000082));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_CSTAR,      __readmsr(0xC0000083));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_SFMASK,     __readmsr(0xC0000084));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_KERNEL_GS,  __readmsr(0xC0000102));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_SYSENTER_CS,  __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_CS));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_SYSENTER_SP,  __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_ESP));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_SYSENTER_IP,  __readmsr(MSR_IA32_SYSENTER_EIP));
    VmcbWrite64(vmcb, VMCB_OFF_GUEST_CR2, 0);

    /* GUEST_RIP / GUEST_RSP / GUEST_RAX are written by SvmLaunchContext (assembly).
     * Pass vmcbPhys and a pointer to the host stack top for the exit handler. */
    /* Pass raw top of the host stack; ASM stores two QWORDs just below it */
    hostStackTop = (PVOID)((ULONG_PTR)ctx->HostStackBase + VMX_STACK_PAGES * PAGE_SIZE);

    g_SvmLaunched[cpuIndex] = FALSE;
    SvmLaunchContext(cpuIndex, physVmcb.QuadPart, (ULONG64)hostStackTop);

    if (g_SvmLaunched[cpuIndex]) {
        ctx->Launched = TRUE;
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SVM enabled on CPU %u\n", cpuIndex);
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }
    /* If launch failed, clear SVME so the CPU is left in a clean state */
    efer = __readmsr(MSR_EFER);
    __writemsr(MSR_EFER, efer & ~EFER_SVME);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmLaunchContext failed on CPU %u\n", cpuIndex);
    return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
}

typedef struct _SVM_INIT_DPC {
    KDPC   Dpc;
    KEVENT Event;
} SVM_INIT_DPC;

static VOID SvmInitDpcCallback(PKDPC Dpc, PVOID Context, PVOID Arg1, PVOID Arg2) {
    ULONG cpu = KeGetCurrentProcessorNumber();
    SVM_INIT_DPC* d = (SVM_INIT_DPC*)Context;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Dpc);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Arg1);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Arg2);
    if (cpu < VMX_MAX_CPUS) SvmEnableCpu(cpu);
    KeSetEvent(&d->Event, 0, FALSE);
}

static NTSTATUS SvmPreallocateResources(VOID) {
    ULONG i;
    ULONG cpuCount = KeQueryActiveProcessorCount(NULL);
    if (cpuCount > VMX_MAX_CPUS) cpuCount = VMX_MAX_CPUS;
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmPreallocateResources: %u active CPU(s)\n", cpuCount);
    for (i = 0; i < cpuCount; i++) {
        SVM_CPU_CTX* ctx = &g_SvmCpus[i];
        if (!ctx->VmcbVa) {
            ctx->VmcbVa = SafeAllocatePool(PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->VmcbVa) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        if (!ctx->HostSaveVa) {
            ctx->HostSaveVa = SafeAllocatePool(PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->HostSaveVa) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        if (!ctx->HostStackBase) {
            ctx->HostStackBase = SafeAllocatePool(VMX_STACK_PAGES * PAGE_SIZE, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!ctx->HostStackBase) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        if (!ctx->MsrpmVa) {
            ctx->MsrpmVa = SafeAllocatePool(PAGE_SIZE * 2, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); /* 8KB MSRPM */
            if (!ctx->MsrpmVa) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
        }
        /* Cache physical addresses at PASSIVE_LEVEL — MmGetPhysicalAddress is
         * unsafe at IPI_LEVEL (accesses MiVisibleState → 0xD1 if paged). */
        g_SvmVmcbPhys[i]  = MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx->VmcbVa).QuadPart;
        g_SvmHsavePhys[i] = MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx->HostSaveVa).QuadPart;
        g_SvmMsrpmPhys[i] = MmGetPhysicalAddress(ctx->MsrpmVa).QuadPart;
    }
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

static NTSTATUS SvmEnableAll(VOID) {
    ULONG i, launched = 0, total;
    SVM_INIT_DPC* dpcs;
    LARGE_INTEGER timeout;
    NTSTATUS st = SvmPreallocateResources();
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmPreallocateResources failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return st;
    }

    total = KeQueryActiveProcessorCount(NULL);
    if (total > VMX_MAX_CPUS) total = VMX_MAX_CPUS;

    /* Queue one DPC per CPU at DISPATCH_LEVEL rather than KeIpiGenericCall at
     * IPI_LEVEL.  At IPI_LEVEL all CPUs are simultaneously frozen; any CPUID
     * the Windows IPI-synchronisation loop issues after vmrun triggers a
     * VMEXIT on every core at once, starving the system permanently.
     * DPCs fire one-at-a-time at DISPATCH_LEVEL so unaffected CPUs remain
     * schedulable and CPUID VMEXITs drain normally. */
    dpcs = (SVM_INIT_DPC*)SafeAllocatePool(total * sizeof(SVM_INIT_DPC), POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!dpcs) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
    RtlZeroMemory(dpcs, total * sizeof(SVM_INIT_DPC));

    for (i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        KeInitializeEvent(&dpcs[i].Event, NotificationEvent, FALSE);
        KeInitializeDpc(&dpcs[i].Dpc, SvmInitDpcCallback, &dpcs[i]);
        KeSetTargetProcessorDpc(&dpcs[i].Dpc, (CCHAR)i);
        KeSetImportanceDpc(&dpcs[i].Dpc, HighImportance);
        KeInsertQueueDpc(&dpcs[i].Dpc, NULL, NULL);
    }

    timeout.QuadPart = -10000000LL * 10; /* 10 s per CPU */
    for (i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        KeWaitForSingleObject(&dpcs[i].Event, Executive, KernelMode, FALSE, &timeout);
    }

    ExFreePoolWithTag(dpcs, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

    for (i = 0; i < total; i++) if (g_SvmCpus[i].Launched) launched++;
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmEnableAll: %u/%u CPUs launched SVM successfully\n", launched, total);
    if (!launched)
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SvmEnableAll: no CPUs launched — CPUID spoof inactive\n");
    return launched ? STATUS_SUCCESS : STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
}

static VOID SvmDisableCpu(ULONG cpuIndex) {
    SVM_CPU_CTX* ctx = &g_SvmCpus[cpuIndex];
    ULONG64 efer;
    if (!ctx->Launched) return;
    /* vmsave is not needed on disable — just clear SVME */
    efer = __readmsr(MSR_EFER);
    __writemsr(MSR_EFER, efer & ~EFER_SVME);
    ctx->Launched = FALSE;
}

static ULONG_PTR SvmDisableIpiCallback(ULONG_PTR Context) {
    ULONG cpu = KeGetCurrentProcessorNumber();
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (cpu < VMX_MAX_CPUS) SvmDisableCpu(cpu);
    return 0;
}

static VOID SvmDisableAll(VOID) {
    ULONG i;
    if (!g_SvmGlobalEnabled) return;
    KeIpiGenericCall(SvmDisableIpiCallback, 0);
    for (i = 0; i < VMX_MAX_CPUS; i++) {
        if (g_SvmCpus[i].VmcbVa)      { ExFreePoolWithTag(g_SvmCpus[i].VmcbVa,      POOL_TAG_SPOOF); g_SvmCpus[i].VmcbVa      = NULL; }
        if (g_SvmCpus[i].HostSaveVa)  { ExFreePoolWithTag(g_SvmCpus[i].HostSaveVa,  POOL_TAG_SPOOF); g_SvmCpus[i].HostSaveVa  = NULL; }
        if (g_SvmCpus[i].HostStackBase){ ExFreePoolWithTag(g_SvmCpus[i].HostStackBase,POOL_TAG_SPOOF); g_SvmCpus[i].HostStackBase= NULL; }
    }
    g_SvmGlobalEnabled = FALSE;
}

/* Detect CPU vendor: returns TRUE for AMD, FALSE for Intel/unknown */
BOOLEAN CpuIsAmd(VOID) {
    int info[4];
    __cpuid(info, 0);
    /* "AuthenticAMD": EBX=0x68747541 EDX=0x69746E65 ECX=0x444D4163 */
    return (info[1] == 0x68747541 && info[3] == 0x69746E65 && info[2] == 0x444D4163);
}

/* Unified enable — dispatches to SVM or VT-x based on vendor.
 * Returns STATUS_SUCCESS if at least one CPU launched. */
NTSTATUS CpuidHypervisorEnableAll(VOID) {
    int cpuinfo[4];
    /* CPUID leaf 1, ECX bit 31: hypervisor present.  If set, we are already a
     * guest (Hyper-V, WSL2 Windows Hypervisor Platform, VMware, etc.).  Nested
     * VT-x/SVM is unreliable in this config — VMXON/VMRUN at IPI_LEVEL inside a
     * guest either freezes all CPUs (VMEXIT_INVALID loop) or causes a machine
     * check with no dump. */
    __cpuid(cpuinfo, 1);
    if (cpuinfo[2] & (1 << 31)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] CpuidHypervisorEnableAll: hypervisor present (CPUID.1:ECX[31]=1)"
                  " — nested VT-x/SVM skipped to avoid freeze\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
    }
    if (CpuIsAmd()) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] CPU vendor: AMD — using SVM hypervisor\n");
        return SvmEnableAll();
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] CPU vendor: Intel — using VT-x hypervisor\n");
    return VmxEnableAll();
}

VOID CpuidHypervisorDisableAll(VOID) {
    if (g_SvmGlobalEnabled) SvmDisableAll();
    if (g_VmxGlobalEnabled) VmxDisableAll();
}


```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_disk.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

GPT_GUID_ENTRY g_GptGuids[MAX_GPT_SPOOFS];
ULONG          g_GptGuidCount = 0;

NTSTATUS DiskIoCompletion(
    PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject,
    PIRP Irp,
    PVOID Context
) {
    PSTORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR_DEF desc;
    ULONG i;
    KIRQL oldIrql;
    PCHAR serialLocation;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DeviceObject);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);

    /* Cannot safely access buffers or call MmIsAddressValid at DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) {
        if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) {
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    if (Irp->IoStatus.Information < sizeof(STORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR_DEF)) {
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }
    desc = (PSTORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR_DEF)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!desc || desc->SerialNumberOffset == 0 ||
        desc->SerialNumberOffset >= desc->Size ||
        desc->SerialNumberOffset >= (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information) {
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    serialLocation = (PCHAR)desc + desc->SerialNumberOffset;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);

    for (i = 0; i < g_DiskSpoofCount; i++) {
        if (g_DiskSpoofs[i].Active) {
            /* Use the length precomputed at IOCTL time (PASSIVE_LEVEL) to
             * avoid calling strnlen at DISPATCH_LEVEL inside this spinlock. */
            SIZE_T origLen = g_DiskSpoofs[i].OriginalSerialLength;
            if (origLen > 0 &&
                RtlCompareMemory(serialLocation, g_DiskSpoofs[i].OriginalSerial, origLen) == origLen) {
                ULONG spoofLen = g_DiskSpoofs[i].SerialLength;
                ULONG bufRemain = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;

                /* Guard: don't write past the IRP system buffer.          */
                if (desc->SerialNumberOffset + spoofLen + 1 > bufRemain) {
                    SPOOF_LOG(
                        "[Spoofer] DiskIoCompletion: disk[%lu] spoofLen %lu overflows buf %lu — skipped\n",
                        g_DiskSpoofs[i].DriveIndex, spoofLen, bufRemain);
                    break;
                }

                __try {
                    RtlCopyMemory(serialLocation, g_DiskSpoofs[i].SpoofedSerial, spoofLen);
                    serialLocation[spoofLen] = '\0';
                    SPOOF_LOG(
                        "[Spoofer] DiskIoCompletion: disk[%lu] serial replaced -> \"%.63s\"\n",
                        g_DiskSpoofs[i].DriveIndex, g_DiskSpoofs[i].SpoofedSerial);
                } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
                    SPOOF_LOG(
                        "[Spoofer] DiskIoCompletion: exception 0x%08X patching disk[%lu]\n",
                        GetExceptionCode(), g_DiskSpoofs[i].DriveIndex);
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);

    /* Blank VendorId and ProductId — EAC cross-checks these against the spoofed
     * serial to detect mismatches.  Replace with generic strings of equal length. */
    __try {
        ULONG infoLen = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
        /* VendorId: overwrite with spaces (HDD convention for absent vendor) */
        if (desc->VendorIdOffset && desc->VendorIdOffset < infoLen) {
            PCHAR v = (PCHAR)desc + desc->VendorIdOffset;
            while ((ULONG_PTR)(v - (PCHAR)desc) < infoLen && *v)
                *v++ = ' ';
        }
        /* ProductId: overwrite in-place with a plausible generic string,
         * preserving original byte count so downstream parsers don't break. */
        if (desc->ProductIdOffset && desc->ProductIdOffset < infoLen) {
            static const CHAR genericProd[] = "STORAGE DEVICE          "; /* 24 chars */
            PCHAR p    = (PCHAR)desc + desc->ProductIdOffset;
            ULONG orig = 0;
            while (desc->ProductIdOffset + orig < infoLen && p[orig]) orig++;
            if (orig) {
                ULONG cpy = (orig < sizeof(genericProd) - 1) ? orig : sizeof(genericProd) - 1;
                RtlCopyMemory(p, genericProd, cpy);
                while (cpy < orig) p[cpy++] = ' ';
            }
        }
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}

    if (Irp->PendingReturned) {
        IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    }
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* NVMe StorageDeviceIdProperty completion */
NTSTATUS NvmeIoCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DeviceObject);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) {
        if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }
    if (NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) {
        PatchNvmeIdentifiers(Irp);
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] NvmeIoCompletion: NVMe identifiers randomized (info=%Iu)\n",
            Irp->IoStatus.Information);
    }
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* StorageDeviceUniqueIdProperty (PropertyId=8) completion.
 * The returned STORAGE_DEVICE_UNIQUE_IDENTIFIER embeds a full
 * STORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR at StorageDeviceOffset; patch its serial
 * with the same spoof table used by DiskIoCompletion.
 * Structure layout (all ULONGs, little-endian):
 *   [0] Version  [4] Size  [8] StorageDeviceIdOffset  [12] StorageDeviceOffset */
NTSTATUS UniqueIdIoCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PUCHAR                       buf;
    ULONG                        totalLen, storageDevOff;
    PSTORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR_DEF desc;
    PCHAR                        serialLocation;
    KIRQL                        oldIrql;
    ULONG                        i;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) {
        if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) return STATUS_SUCCESS;

    totalLen = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    if (totalLen < 16) return STATUS_SUCCESS;

    buf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf) return STATUS_SUCCESS;

    /* StorageDeviceOffset is at dword offset 3 (byte offset 12) */
    storageDevOff = ((PULONG)buf)[3];
    if (storageDevOff == 0 ||
        storageDevOff + sizeof(STORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR_DEF) > totalLen)
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;

    desc = (PSTORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR_DEF)(buf + storageDevOff);
    if (desc->SerialNumberOffset == 0 ||
        storageDevOff + desc->SerialNumberOffset >= totalLen)
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;

    serialLocation = (PCHAR)desc + desc->SerialNumberOffset;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);
    for (i = 0; i < g_DiskSpoofCount; i++) {
        if (g_DiskSpoofs[i].Active) {
            SIZE_T origLen = g_DiskSpoofs[i].OriginalSerialLength;
            if (origLen > 0 &&
                RtlCompareMemory(serialLocation, g_DiskSpoofs[i].OriginalSerial, origLen) == origLen) {
                ULONG spoofLen = g_DiskSpoofs[i].SerialLength;
                ULONG remain   = totalLen - storageDevOff;
                if (desc->SerialNumberOffset + spoofLen + 1 <= remain) {
                    __try {
                        RtlCopyMemory(serialLocation, g_DiskSpoofs[i].SpoofedSerial, spoofLen);
                        serialLocation[spoofLen] = '\0';
                    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

NTSTATUS FilterDeviceControl(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)DeviceObject->DeviceExtension;
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack;
    PSTORAGE_PROPERTY_QUERY_DEF query;

    /* Not one of our filter devices — forward to the carrier's original handler.
     * Guard: paged carrier code cannot be called at DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (!ext || !MmIsAddressValid(ext) || ext->Magic != FILTER_MAGIC || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        if (KeGetCurrentIrql() <= APC_LEVEL && g_CarrierDispatch[IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL])
            return g_CarrierDispatch[IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL](DeviceObject, Irp);
        Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_REQUEST;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_REQUEST;
    }

    stack = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    query = NULL; /* suppress may-be-uninitialised warning */

    if (!ext || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_STORAGE_QUERY_PROPERTY) {

        query = (PSTORAGE_PROPERTY_QUERY_DEF)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
        if (query && query->QueryType == (ULONG)PropertyStandardQueryType) {
            if (query->PropertyId == (ULONG)StorageDevicePropertyId) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, DiskIoCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
            if (query->PropertyId == 1) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, NvmeIoCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
            if (query->PropertyId == 8) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, UniqueIdIoCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
        }
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == SMART_RECV_DRIVE_DATA_CODE) {
        PUCHAR inBuf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
        ULONG  inLen = stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.InputBufferLength;
        if (inBuf && inLen >= 11 && MmIsAddressValid(inBuf + 10)) {
            if (inBuf[10] == ATA_IDENTIFY_CMD || inBuf[10] == ATAPI_IDENTIFY_CMD) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, SmartCompletion, (PVOID)(ULONG_PTR)FALSE, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
            if (inBuf[10] == 0xB0 && inLen >= 5 && MmIsAddressValid(inBuf + 4) && inBuf[4] == 0xD0) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, SmartAttrCompletion, (PVOID)(ULONG_PTR)FALSE, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
        }
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_SCSI_MINIPORT_CODE) {
        PUCHAR inBuf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
        ULONG  inLen = stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.InputBufferLength;
        if (inBuf && inLen >= 39 && MmIsAddressValid(inBuf + 38)) {
            if (inBuf[38] == ATA_IDENTIFY_CMD || inBuf[38] == ATAPI_IDENTIFY_CMD) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, SmartCompletion, (PVOID)(ULONG_PTR)TRUE, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
            if (inBuf[38] == 0xB0 && inLen >= 33 && MmIsAddressValid(inBuf + 32) && inBuf[32] == 0xD0) {
                IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
                IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, SmartAttrCompletion, (PVOID)(ULONG_PTR)TRUE, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
                return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
            }
        }
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        (stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_ATA_PASSTHROUGH_CODE ||
         stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_ATA_PASSTHROUGH_DIR)) {
        PUCHAR inBuf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
        ULONG  inLen = stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.InputBufferLength;
        if (inBuf && inLen >= 0x30 && MmIsAddressValid(inBuf + 0x2E) &&
            (inBuf[0x2E] == ATA_IDENTIFY_CMD || inBuf[0x2E] == ATAPI_IDENTIFY_CMD)) {
            IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
            IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, AtaPassThroughCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
            return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
        }
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        (stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_SCSI_PASS_THROUGH_CODE ||
         stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_SCSI_PASS_THROUGH_DIR)) {
        PUCHAR inBuf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
        ULONG  inLen = stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.InputBufferLength;
        if (inBuf && inLen >= 56 && MmIsAddressValid(inBuf + 40) && inBuf[40] == 0x12) {
            BOOLEAN isDirect =
                (stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_SCSI_PASS_THROUGH_DIR);
            IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
            IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, ScsiPassThroughCompletion,
                                   (PVOID)(ULONG_PTR)(isDirect ? 1 : 0),
                                   TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
            return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
        }
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_DISK_PART_INFO_EX) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, PartitionInfoExCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }

    if (g_SpoofActive &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_DISK_DRIVE_LAYOUT_EX) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, DriveLayoutExCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }

    IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
}

/* GPT sector read interception for filter devices */
NTSTATUS FilterReadDispatch(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)DeviceObject->DeviceExtension;
    UCHAR majorFn;
    if (!ext || !MmIsAddressValid(ext) || ext->Magic != FILTER_MAGIC || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        /* Carrier's own device. g_CarrierDispatch points into paged carrier code —
         * cannot call it at DISPATCH_LEVEL or we fault on the pageable text. */
        majorFn = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp)->MajorFunction;
        if (KeGetCurrentIrql() <= APC_LEVEL &&
            majorFn <= IRP_MJ_MAXIMUM_FUNCTION && g_CarrierDispatch[majorFn])
            return g_CarrierDispatch[majorFn](DeviceObject, Irp);
        Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_REQUEST;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_REQUEST;
    }
    if (g_SpoofActive && ext->Type == FILTER_TYPE_DISK) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, GptReadCompletion, NULL, TRUE, FALSE, FALSE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }
    IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
}

/* Passthrough for all non-intercepted IRP types on filter devices.
 * For the control device, complete with an error — it only handles
 * IRP_MJ_CREATE, IRP_MJ_CLOSE, and IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL.         */

NTSTATUS AttachFilterToDiskDevices(PDRIVER_OBJECT DriverObject) {
    NTSTATUS status;
    UNICODE_STRING diskDriverName;
    PDRIVER_OBJECT diskDriver = NULL;
    PDEVICE_OBJECT diskDevice;

    if (!g_ObReferenceObjectByName || !g_IoDriverObjectType) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Undocumented APIs not resolved — skipping disk filter\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_IMPLEMENTED;
    }

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&diskDriverName, L"\\Driver\\Disk");
    status = g_ObReferenceObjectByName(
        &diskDriverName,
        OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE,
        NULL, 0,
        *g_IoDriverObjectType,
        KernelMode, NULL,
        (PVOID*)&diskDriver
    );
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Cannot find \\Driver\\Disk: 0x%08X\n", status);
        return status;
    }

    /* Take an initial reference on the first device before entering the loop.
     * Inside the loop we grab a reference on nextDevice before releasing the
     * current one, so the pointer is always safe to dereference.            */
    diskDevice = diskDriver->DeviceObject;
    if (diskDevice) ObReferenceObject(diskDevice);

    while (diskDevice && g_FilterCount < MAX_FILTERS) {
        PDEVICE_OBJECT filterDevice = NULL;
        PDEVICE_OBJECT nextDevice;

        /* Pre-grab the next pointer's reference BEFORE doing any work so a
         * concurrent hot-remove cannot free it while we hold only diskDevice. */
        nextDevice = diskDevice->NextDevice;
        if (nextDevice) ObReferenceObject(nextDevice);

        status = IoCreateDevice(
            DriverObject,
            sizeof(FILTER_EXTENSION),
            NULL,
            diskDevice->DeviceType,
            0,
            FALSE,
            &filterDevice
        );
        if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
            PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)filterDevice->DeviceExtension;

            /* Stamp magic and type BEFORE attaching so MasterDispatch can identify
             * this device the instant the first IRP arrives after IoAttachDevice. */
            ext->Magic = FILTER_MAGIC;
            ext->Type  = FILTER_TYPE_DISK;

            /* Copy I/O method flags BEFORE attaching — the filter is live the
             * instant IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack returns, so any IRP that
             * arrives before the flags are set will be processed with the wrong
             * transfer type, causing misaligned MDLs and a fault.            */
            filterDevice->Flags |= diskDevice->Flags & (DO_BUFFERED_IO | DO_DIRECT_IO);
            filterDevice->Characteristics |= diskDevice->Characteristics;

            ext->LowerDevice = IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack(filterDevice, diskDevice);
            if (ext->LowerDevice) {
                /* Update alignment from the actual lower device (may differ from
                 * diskDevice if other filters were already in the stack).     */
                filterDevice->AlignmentRequirement = ext->LowerDevice->AlignmentRequirement;
                filterDevice->Flags &= ~DO_DEVICE_INITIALIZING;

                g_FilterDevices[g_FilterCount++] = filterDevice;
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Filter attached to disk device %p (lower=%p)\n",
                         diskDevice, ext->LowerDevice);
            } else {
                IoDeleteDevice(filterDevice);
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack failed for %p\n", diskDevice);
            }
        } else {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] IoCreateDevice for filter failed: 0x%08X (diskDev=%p)\n",
                     status, diskDevice);
        }

        ObDereferenceObject(diskDevice);
        diskDevice = nextDevice;
    }

    /* If the loop exited because MAX_FILTERS was hit, we still hold a
     * reference on the current device — release it.                  */
    if (diskDevice) ObDereferenceObject(diskDevice);

    ObDereferenceObject(diskDriver);
    return (g_FilterCount > 0) ? STATUS_SUCCESS : STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
}

void DetachAllFilters(void) {
    ULONG i;
    for (i = 0; i < g_FilterCount; i++) {
        if (g_FilterDevices[i]) {
            PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)g_FilterDevices[i]->DeviceExtension;
            if (ext && ext->LowerDevice) {
                IoDetachDevice(ext->LowerDevice);
            }
            IoDeleteDevice(g_FilterDevices[i]);
            g_FilterDevices[i] = NULL;
        }
    }
    g_FilterCount = 0;
}

void DetachGpuFilters(void) {
    ULONG i;
    for (i = 0; i < g_GpuFilterCount; i++) {
        if (g_GpuFilterDevices[i]) {
            PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)g_GpuFilterDevices[i]->DeviceExtension;
            if (ext && ext->LowerDevice) {
                IoDetachDevice(ext->LowerDevice);
            }
            IoDeleteDevice(g_GpuFilterDevices[i]);
            g_GpuFilterDevices[i] = NULL;
        }
    }
    g_GpuFilterCount = 0;
}


ULONG Crc32Table[256];
BOOLEAN g_Crc32Inited = FALSE;

VOID InitCrc32(VOID) {
    ULONG i, j, crc;
    for (i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
        crc = i;
        for (j = 0; j < 8; j++) crc = (crc & 1) ? (crc >> 1) ^ 0xEDB88320 : (crc >> 1);
        Crc32Table[i] = crc;
    }
    g_Crc32Inited = TRUE;
}

ULONG ComputeCrc32(const UCHAR* data, ULONG len) {
    ULONG crc = 0xFFFFFFFF, i;
    for (i = 0; i < len; i++) crc = (crc >> 8) ^ Crc32Table[(crc ^ data[i]) & 0xFF];
    return crc ^ 0xFFFFFFFF;
}

NTSTATUS GptReadCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack;
    LARGE_INTEGER offset;
    PUCHAR buf;
    ULONG len;
    KIRQL oldIrql;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DeviceObject);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);

    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    if (Irp->IoStatus.Information < 512) goto done;

    stack  = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    offset = stack->Parameters.Read.ByteOffset;
    len    = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;

    buf = (PUCHAR)MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);
    if (!buf && Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer) buf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf) goto done;

    /* LBA 0 — MBR sector: patch disk signature at offset 0x1B8 (EAC reads as MBR:%08x) */
    if (offset.QuadPart == 0 && len >= 512 && MmIsAddressValid(buf + 0x1FF)) {
        if (buf[0x1FE] == 0x55 && buf[0x1FF] == 0xAA) {
            KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);
            if (g_SpoofedMBRSig == 0)
                g_SpoofedMBRSig = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) | 0x00000001U;
            *(PULONG)(buf + 0x1B8) = g_SpoofedMBRSig;
            KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);
        }
        goto done;
    }

    /* LBA 1 — GPT header: check for both 512-byte and 4K sector sizes */
    if (offset.QuadPart != 512 && offset.QuadPart != 4096) goto done;
    if (len < 0x60) goto done;

    /* "EFI PART" signature at offset 0 of the GPT header */
    if (buf[0] != 'E' || buf[1] != 'F' || buf[2] != 'I' || buf[3] != ' ' ||
        buf[4] != 'P' || buf[5] != 'A' || buf[6] != 'R' || buf[7] != 'T') goto done;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);
    {
        PULONG headerCrc = (PULONG)(buf + 0x10);
        ULONG  headerSize;
        ULONG  i;

        if (!g_Crc32Inited) InitCrc32();

        headerSize = *(PULONG)(buf + 0x0C);
        if (headerSize < 0x5C || headerSize > len) { KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql); goto done; }

        /* Randomise Disk GUID at offset 0x38 */
        __try {
            for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) buf[0x38 + i] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xFF);

            /* Recalculate header CRC32 */
            *headerCrc = 0;
            *headerCrc = ComputeCrc32(buf, headerSize);
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] GptReadCompletion: GPT DiskGUID randomized at LBA1 (offset=%I64u"
                " guid=%02X%02X%02X%02X...)\n",
                offset.QuadPart, buf[0x38], buf[0x39], buf[0x3A], buf[0x3B]);
        } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] GptReadCompletion: exception 0x%08X patching GPT header\n",
                GetExceptionCode());
        }
    }
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);

done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * VT-x HELPERS
 * ============================================================ */

static ULONG VmxAdjustControls(ULONG desired, ULONG msrNum) {
    ULONG64 msr = __readmsr(msrNum);
    desired |= (ULONG)(msr & 0xFFFFFFFF);   /* force required-1 bits */
    desired &= (ULONG)(msr >> 32);           /* force required-0 bits */
    return desired;
}

/* GDTR/IDTR pseudo-descriptor — must be packed to match the hardware format:
 * [0..1]=Limit, [2..9]=Base (no padding). Without pack(1) MSVC places Base at
 * offset 8 (aligned), but _sgdt/_sidt write Base at offset 2 (hardware spec),
 * causing gdtr.Base to read the wrong stack bytes. */
#pragma pack(push, 1)
typedef struct { USHORT Limit; ULONG64 Base; } PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR;
#pragma pack(pop)

static ULONG VmxGetSegmentAR(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR entry;
    if (selector == 0) return 0x10000; /* unusable */
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    entry = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    return (ULONG)(entry[5]) | (((ULONG)(entry[6]) & 0xF0) << 8);
}

static ULONG64 VmxGetSegmentBase(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR e;
    ULONG64 base;
    if (selector == 0) return 0;
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    e = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    base = ((ULONG64)e[2]) | ((ULONG64)e[3] << 8) | ((ULONG64)e[4] << 16) | ((ULONG64)e[7] << 24);
    if ((e[5] & 0x10) == 0) /* system descriptor, 16-byte entry */
        base |= (ULONG64)(*(PULONG)(e + 8)) << 32;
    return base;
}

static ULONG VmxGetSegmentLimit(USHORT selector) {
    PSEUDO_DESCRIPTOR gdtr;
    PUCHAR e;
    ULONG limit;
    if (selector == 0) return 0;
    _sgdt(&gdtr);
    e = (PUCHAR)gdtr.Base + (selector & (USHORT)~0x7);
    limit = (ULONG)(e[0]) | ((ULONG)e[1] << 8) | ((ULONG)(e[6] & 0x0F) << 16);
    if (e[6] & 0x80) limit = (limit << 12) | 0xFFF; /* G bit */
    return limit;
}

/* ============================================================
 * SMART / ATA PASS-THROUGH SERIAL INTERCEPTION
 * Covers SMART_RCV_DRIVE_DATA, IOCTL_SCSI_MINIPORT (IDENTIFY),
 * IOCTL_ATA_PASS_THROUGH, IOCTL_ATA_PASS_THROUGH_DIRECT.
 * ATA IDENTIFY DEVICE serial is at words 10-19 (bytes 20-39),
 * each word byte-swapped (big-endian per word in ASCII).
 * WWN is at words 108-111 (bytes 216-223).
 * ============================================================ */

static VOID PatchIdentifySerial(PUCHAR id, ULONG len, ULONG* seed) {
    CHAR s[20];
    ULONG i;
    if (len < 40) return;
    KernelGenSerial(s, 20, seed);
    /* byte-swap per word: word N high byte = char i+1, low byte = char i */
    for (i = 0; i < 20; i += 2) {
        id[20 + i + 0] = (UCHAR)s[i + 1];
        id[20 + i + 1] = (UCHAR)s[i + 0];
    }
    /* Randomise WWN (World Wide Name) at words 108-111 */
    if (len >= 224)
        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++)
            id[216 + i] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(seed) & 0xFF);
}

/* Completion for SMART_RCV_DRIVE_DATA and IOCTL_SCSI_MINIPORT.
 * Context = (PVOID)(ULONG_PTR)isScsiMiniport (0 or 1, not a pointer). */
NTSTATUS SmartCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    BOOLEAN isMiniport = (BOOLEAN)(ULONG_PTR)Context;
    PUCHAR out;
    ULONG  len;
    KIRQL  irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    out = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!out || !MmIsAddressValid(out)) goto done;
    __try {
        PUCHAR id;
        ULONG  idLen;
        /* SENDCMDOUTPARAMS layout: cBufferSize(4) + DRIVERSTATUS(12) + bBuffer */
        if (isMiniport) {
            /* SRB_IO_CONTROL (28) + SENDCMDOUTPARAMS header (16) + bBuffer */
            if (len < 28 + 16 + 40) goto done;
            id    = out + 28 + 16;
            idLen = len - 28 - 16;
        } else {
            if (len < 16 + 40) goto done;
            id    = out + 16;
            idLen = len - 16;
        }
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(id + 39)) goto done;
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        PatchIdentifySerial(id, idLen, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* Patch SMART attribute table (SMART READ DATA, cmd 0xB0 feat 0xD0).
 * Attribute layout: 2-byte version, then 30 * 12-byte records.
 * Attr 0x09 = Power-On Hours, Attr 0x0C = Power Cycle Count.
 * Raw values are little-endian 6-byte fields at record+5. */
static void PatchSmartAttributes(PUCHAR tbl, ULONG len) {
    ULONG pohHours, pccCount, i;
    if (len < 2 + 12) return;
    pohHours = (g_SpoofSeed ^ 0x9A3B7C2DUL) % 6001 + 1000;  /* 1000-7000 hours */
    pccCount = (g_SpoofSeed ^ 0x4F2E8A1BUL) % 801  + 200;   /* 200-1000 cycles  */
    for (i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
        ULONG off = 2 + i * 12;
        ULONG val;
        if (off + 11 >= len || tbl[off] == 0) break;
        val = 0;
        if      (tbl[off] == 0x09) val = pohHours;
        else if (tbl[off] == 0x0C) val = pccCount;
        else continue;
        tbl[off+5] = (UCHAR)( val        & 0xFF);
        tbl[off+6] = (UCHAR)((val >>  8) & 0xFF);
        tbl[off+7] = (UCHAR)((val >> 16) & 0xFF);
        tbl[off+8] = (UCHAR)((val >> 24) & 0xFF);
        tbl[off+9] = 0; tbl[off+10] = 0;
    }
}

/* Completion for SMART_RCV_DRIVE_DATA with SMART READ DATA command (0xB0/0xD0).
 * Patches power-on hours and power cycle count attributes. */
NTSTATUS SmartAttrCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    BOOLEAN isMiniport = (BOOLEAN)(ULONG_PTR)Context;
    PUCHAR out;
    ULONG  len;
    KIRQL  irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    out = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!out || !MmIsAddressValid(out)) goto done;
    __try {
        PUCHAR tbl;
        ULONG  tblLen;
        if (isMiniport) {
            if (len < 28 + 16 + 14) goto done;
            tbl    = out + 28 + 16;
            tblLen = len - 28 - 16;
        } else {
            if (len < 16 + 14) goto done;
            tbl    = out + 16;
            tblLen = len - 16;
        }
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(tbl + 13)) goto done;
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        PatchSmartAttributes(tbl, tblLen);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* Completion for IOCTL_ATA_PASS_THROUGH / DIRECT.
 * ATA_PASS_THROUGH_EX (x64): DataBufferOffset at 0x18 (ULONG64),
 * CurrentTaskFile at 0x28 (cmd byte at [6] => offset 0x2E). */
NTSTATUS AtaPassThroughCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PUCHAR  buf;
    ULONG   len;
    ULONG64 off;
    KIRQL   irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    buf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf || !MmIsAddressValid(buf) || len < 0x30 + 40) goto done;
    __try {
        off = *(PULONG64)(buf + 0x18);
        if (off < 0x30 || off + 40 > len) goto done;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(buf + off + 39)) goto done;
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        PatchIdentifySerial(buf + (ULONG)off, len - (ULONG)off, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * SCSI PASS-THROUGH COMPLETION
 * Intercepts IOCTL_SCSI_PASS_THROUGH (buffered) and
 * IOCTL_SCSI_PASS_THROUGH_DIRECT (METHOD_IN_DIRECT).
 * SCSI_PASS_THROUGH on x64:
 *   +0  Length(USHORT), +2 ScsiStatus, +6 CdbLength, +8 DataIn
 *   +12 DataTransferLength(ULONG)
 *   +24 DataBufferOffset(ULONG_PTR) — offset from buf start for BUFFERED
 *   +40 Cdb[16]
 * Patches:
 *   INQUIRY (0x12) VPD page 0x80 — Unit Serial Number
 *   INQUIRY (0x12) standard       — vendor/product/revision
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS ScsiPassThroughCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    BOOLEAN isDirect = ((ULONG_PTR)Context != 0);
    PUCHAR  spt;
    ULONG   len;
    PUCHAR  cdb;
    PUCHAR  dataBuf;
    ULONG   dataLen;
    KIRQL   irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);

    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    spt = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!spt || !MmIsAddressValid(spt) || len < 56) goto done;

    __try {
        cdb = spt + 40;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(cdb + 2) || cdb[0] != 0x12) goto done;

        if (!isDirect) {
            /* Buffered: data at spt + DataBufferOffset */
            ULONG64 dbo = *(PULONG64)(spt + 24);
            if (dbo == 0 || dbo >= (ULONG64)len) goto done;
            dataBuf = spt + (ULONG)dbo;
            dataLen = len - (ULONG)dbo;
        } else {
            /* DIRECT: data in MDL; length from DataTransferLength */
            ULONG xferLen = *(PULONG)(spt + 12);
            if (!Irp->MdlAddress) goto done;
            dataBuf = (PUCHAR)MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(
                          Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);
            if (!dataBuf) goto done;
            dataLen = (xferLen > 0) ? xferLen : MmGetMdlByteCount(Irp->MdlAddress);
        }

        if (!MmIsAddressValid(dataBuf) || dataLen < 5) goto done;

        if (cdb[1] & 0x01) {
            /* EVPD set — check VPD page code */
            if (cdb[2] == 0x80) {
                /* Page 0x80: Unit Serial Number
```
