```c
                 * Layout: [devtype][0x80][rsvd][length][ASCII serial...] */
                ULONG serialLen = dataBuf[3];
                if (serialLen > 0 && (ULONG)(4 + serialLen) <= dataLen) {
                    CHAR  s[20];
                    ULONG copy;
                    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
                    KernelGenSerial(s, 20, &g_SpoofSeed);
                    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
                    copy = (serialLen < 20) ? serialLen : 20;
                    RtlCopyMemory(dataBuf + 4, s, copy);
                    while (copy < serialLen) dataBuf[4 + copy++] = ' ';
                }
            }
        } else if (dataLen >= 36) {
            /* Standard INQUIRY: vendor[8-15], product[16-31], revision[32-35] */
            static const CHAR vendor[] = "ATA     ";
            static const CHAR prodId[] = "STORAGE DEVICE  ";
            static const CHAR revStr[] = "0001";
            RtlCopyMemory(dataBuf +  8, vendor, 8);
            RtlCopyMemory(dataBuf + 16, prodId, 16);
            RtlCopyMemory(dataBuf + 32, revStr,  4);
        }
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}

done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * PARTITION GUID INTERCEPTION
 * IOCTL_DISK_GET_PARTITION_INFO_EX: randomise PartitionId GUID.
 * IOCTL_DISK_GET_DRIVE_LAYOUT_EX: randomise disk DiskId and all
 * partition PartitionId GUIDs.
 * PARTITION_INFORMATION_EX layout (x64):
 *   +0  PartitionStyle (DWORD)
 *   +8  StartingOffset (LARGE_INTEGER)
 *   +16 PartitionLength
 *   +24 PartitionNumber (DWORD)
 *   +28 RewritePartition (BOOLEAN)
 *   +32 union { Gpt: PartitionType[16], PartitionId[16], ... }
 * => PartitionId for GPT at offset 48.
 * DRIVE_LAYOUT_INFORMATION_EX:
 *   +0  PartitionStyle, +4 PartitionCount
 *   +8  union { Gpt.DiskId at +8 }
 *   +48 PartitionEntry[N] each 144 bytes (32 pre-union + 112 GPT union)
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS PartitionInfoExCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PUCHAR buf;
    KIRQL  irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    buf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf || !MmIsAddressValid(buf) || Irp->IoStatus.Information < 64) goto done;
    __try {
        if (*(PULONG)buf == PARTITION_STYLE_GPT_SPOOF &&
            MmIsAddressValid(buf + 63)) {
            KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
            KernelGenUUID(buf + 48, &g_SpoofSeed); /* PartitionId */
            KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        }
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

NTSTATUS DriveLayoutExCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PUCHAR buf;
    ULONG  len, cnt, i;
    KIRQL  irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    buf = (PUCHAR)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf || !MmIsAddressValid(buf) || len < 64) goto done;
    __try {
        if (*(PULONG)buf != PARTITION_STYLE_GPT_SPOOF) goto done;
        cnt = *(PULONG)(buf + 4);
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        /* DiskId GUID at offset 8 in GPT layout union */
        if (MmIsAddressValid(buf + 23))
            KernelGenUUID(buf + 8, &g_SpoofSeed);
        /* Partition entries at offset 48, each 144 bytes */
        for (i = 0; i < cnt && i < 128; i++) {
            ULONG eoff = 48 + i * 144;
            PUCHAR e;
            if (eoff + 64 > len) break;
            e = buf + eoff;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(e + 63)) break;
            if (*(PULONG)e == PARTITION_STYLE_GPT_SPOOF)
                KernelGenUUID(e + 48, &g_SpoofSeed); /* PartitionId */
        }
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * NVIDIA GPU UUID HOOK
 * Hooks nvlddmkm.sys IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL. For IOCTL 0x08DE0008
 * (GPU enumeration) the output buffer contains "GPU-XXXXXXXX-..."
 * at offset 0x1AC. We randomise the hex digits in place.
 * ============================================================ */



```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_efi.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

typedef NTSTATUS (NTAPI *PFN_ExSetFirmwareEnv)(PUNICODE_STRING, LPGUID, PVOID, ULONG, ULONG);

NTSTATUS SpoofEFIVars(VOID) {
    static const GUID offlineGuid = {
        0xDDBC5D3, 0xE959, 0x4EBF,
        { 0xB4, 0xE6, 0x5F, 0x4C, 0x13, 0xE3, 0x6F, 0x49 }
    };
    UNICODE_STRING fnName, varName;
    PFN_ExSetFirmwareEnv pSetVar;
    UCHAR seed[64];
    ULONG i;
    KIRQL oldIrql;
    NTSTATUS st;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&fnName, L"ExSetFirmwareEnvironmentVariable");
    pSetVar = (PFN_ExSetFirmwareEnv)MmGetSystemRoutineAddress(&fnName);
    if (!pSetVar) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] ExSetFirmwareEnvironmentVariable not available\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_IMPLEMENTED;
    }

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) seed[i] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xFF);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&varName, L"OfflineUniqueIDRandomSeed");
    st = pSetVar(&varName, (LPGUID)&offlineGuid, seed, sizeof(seed), 7 /* NV|BS|RT */);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st))
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofEFIVars: SetVariable failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
    else
        g_EfiVarsSpoofed = TRUE;

    return st;
}

/* ============================================================
 * ACPI MSDM TABLE SPOOFING
 * Locates the kernel's ACPI table cache and patches the product key.
 * ============================================================ */

#define ACPI_PROVIDER    0x49504341  /* 'ACPI' */
#define MSDM_TABLE_ID    0x4D44534D  /* 'MSDM' */

NTSTATUS PatchAcpiMsdm(VOID) {
    typedef struct { ULONG Provider; ULONG Action; ULONG TableID;
                     ULONG BufLen; UCHAR Buf[1]; } SFTI2;
    SFTI2 probe = {0};
    ULONG needed = 0;
    SFTI2* sfti;
    ULONG allocSz;
    NTSTATUS st;
    PUCHAR anchor, base, pat;
    ULONG patLen;
    LONG_PTR delta;

    probe.Provider = ACPI_PROVIDER;
    probe.Action   = 1;
    probe.TableID  = MSDM_TABLE_ID;
    ZwQuerySystemInformation(SFTI_CLASS, &probe, sizeof(probe), &needed);
    if (needed < sizeof(SFTI2) + 36) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] MSDM: size probe returned %u — table absent?\n", needed);
        return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
    }

    allocSz = needed + 0x100;
    sfti = (SFTI2*)SafeAllocatePool(allocSz, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!sfti) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
    RtlZeroMemory(sfti, allocSz);
    sfti->Provider = ACPI_PROVIDER;
    sfti->Action   = 1;
    sfti->TableID  = MSDM_TABLE_ID;
    sfti->BufLen   = allocSz - FIELD_OFFSET(SFTI2, Buf);

    st = ZwQuerySystemInformation(SFTI_CLASS, sfti, allocSz, &needed);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st) || sfti->BufLen < 36) {
        ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        return st;
    }

    /* sfti->Buf now contains the raw MSDM table.  Use first 48 bytes as pattern. */
    patLen = min(48, sfti->BufLen);
    pat = (PUCHAR)SafeAllocatePool(patLen, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!pat) { ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; }
    RtlCopyMemory(pat, sfti->Buf, patLen);
    RtlZeroMemory(sfti->Buf, patLen);   /* prevent freed sfti from matching its own data in the backward scan */
    ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

    /* Scan NonPagedPool backwards 512 MB for the live MSDM buffer */
    anchor = (PUCHAR)SafeAllocatePool(16, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!anchor) { ExFreePoolWithTag(pat, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; }
    base = (PUCHAR)((ULONG_PTR)anchor & ~(ULONG_PTR)0xFFF);
    ExFreePoolWithTag(anchor, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

    for (delta = 0; delta > -(LONG_PTR)(512ULL << 20); delta -= 0x1000) {
        PUCHAR page = base + delta;
        ULONG off;
        if ((ULONG_PTR)page < 0xFFFF800000000000ULL) break;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(page)) continue;
        for (off = 0; off + patLen <= 0x1000; off += 16) {
            PUCHAR cand = page + off;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(cand + patLen - 1)) break;
            if (RtlCompareMemory(cand, pat, patLen) != patLen) continue;
            /* Found the live MSDM.  Product key is at byte offset 36+16 = 52
             * (ACPI header 36 bytes, then version/reserved/type/reserved/length = 5*4 bytes,
             * then the key string).  Overwrite with a placeholder key. */
            {
                PUCHAR keyField = cand + 36 + 20; /* header + 5 ULONG fields */
                const char fakeKey[] = "VKXTG-7CNMQ-G27XF-HFJXB-D34HH";
                ULONG klen = sizeof(fakeKey) - 1;
                __try { RtlCopyMemory(keyField, fakeKey, klen); } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] MSDM patched @ %p\n", cand);
                g_MsdmSpoofed = TRUE;
                ExFreePoolWithTag(pat, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                return STATUS_SUCCESS;
            }
        }
    }

    ExFreePoolWithTag(pat, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] MSDM: kernel buffer not found\n");
    return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
}

/* ============================================================
 * BOOT IDENTIFIER GUID SPOOFING
 * NtQuerySystemInformation(0x5A) returns a per-boot GUID that EAC
 * uses as a session correlation key.  Locate the kernel's live copy
 * via backward pool scan and overwrite with a random UUID.
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS PatchBootIdentifier(VOID) {
    BOOT_ENV_INFO_SPOOF bei;
    ULONG  needed = 0;
    NTSTATUS st;
    UCHAR  newGuid[16];
    KIRQL  irql;
    PUCHAR anchor, base;
    LONG_PTR delta;
    ULONG  i;
    BOOLEAN allZero;

    RtlZeroMemory(&bei, sizeof(bei));
    st = ZwQuerySystemInformation(SYS_BOOT_ENV_INFO_CLASS, &bei, sizeof(bei), &needed);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchBootIdentifier: ZwQuerySystemInformation(0x5A) failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return st;
    }

    allZero = TRUE;
    for (i = 0; i < 16; i++)
        if (((PUCHAR)&bei.BootIdentifier)[i]) { allZero = FALSE; break; }
    if (allZero) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchBootIdentifier: BIOS mode (zero GUID) — skip\n");
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    KernelGenUUID(newGuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);

    anchor = (PUCHAR)SafeAllocatePool(16, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!anchor) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
    base = (PUCHAR)((ULONG_PTR)anchor & ~(ULONG_PTR)0xFFF);
    ExFreePoolWithTag(anchor, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

    for (delta = 0; delta > -(LONG_PTR)(512ULL << 20); delta -= 0x1000) {
        PUCHAR page = base + delta;
        ULONG  off;
        if ((ULONG_PTR)page < 0xFFFF800000000000ULL) break;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(page)) continue;
        for (off = 0; off + 16 <= 0x1000; off += 8) {
            PUCHAR cand = page + off;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(cand + 15)) break;
            if (RtlCompareMemory(cand, &bei.BootIdentifier, 16) == 16) {
                __try { RtlCopyMemory(cand, newGuid, 16); }
                __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] BootIdentifier patched @ %p\n", cand);
                g_BootUuidSpoofed = TRUE;
                return STATUS_SUCCESS;
            }
        }
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchBootIdentifier: GUID not found in 512 MB pool scan\n");
    return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
}


VOID PatchAcpiOemIds(VOID) {
    /* Table IDs to patch */
    static const ULONG aclTableIds[] = {
        0x54445344u, /* 'DSDT' */
        0x50434146u, /* 'FACP' */
        0x54445353u, /* 'SSDT' */
        0x43495041u, /* 'APIC' */
        0x54455048u, /* 'HPET' */
        0u
    };
    /* Plausible OEM ID / OEM Table ID pairs (6-byte / 8-byte, no null term) */
    static const char acpiOemIdChoices[][7] = {
        "LENOVO", "ASUS  ", "DELL  ", "HPQOEM", "MSRSI ", "GIGABY", "BOCHS "
    };
    static const char acpiOemTblChoices[][9] = {
        "CB-01   ", "CM6870  ", "DELL0001", "HP 30A  ", "MS-7C37 ", "B550M   ", "BXPC    "
    };
    ULONG tblIdx;
    ULONG oemChoice;
    KIRQL irql;
    ULONG rnd;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    rnd = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    oemChoice = rnd % 7;

    {
    typedef struct { ULONG Provider; ULONG Action; ULONG TableID; ULONG BufLen; UCHAR Buf[1]; } SFTI3;
    for (tblIdx = 0; aclTableIds[tblIdx]; tblIdx++) {
        SFTI3  probe = {0};
        ULONG  needed = 0;
        SFTI3* sfti;
        ULONG  allocSz;
        NTSTATUS st;
        ULONG  patLen;
        PUCHAR pat;
        PUCHAR anchor, base;
        LONG_PTR delta;
        BOOLEAN found;

        probe.Provider = ACPI_PROVIDER;
        probe.Action   = 1;
        probe.TableID  = aclTableIds[tblIdx];
        ZwQuerySystemInformation(SFTI_CLASS, &probe, sizeof(probe), &needed);
        if (needed < sizeof(SFTI3) + 36) {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchAcpiOemIds: table %.4s not present\n",
                     (char*)&aclTableIds[tblIdx]);
            continue;
        }

        allocSz = needed + 0x100;
        sfti = (SFTI3*)SafeAllocatePool(allocSz, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!sfti) continue;
        RtlZeroMemory(sfti, allocSz);
        sfti->Provider = ACPI_PROVIDER;
        sfti->Action   = 1;
        sfti->TableID  = aclTableIds[tblIdx];
        sfti->BufLen   = allocSz - FIELD_OFFSET(SFTI3, Buf);

        st = ZwQuerySystemInformation(SFTI_CLASS, sfti, allocSz, &needed);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st) || sfti->BufLen < 36) {
            ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            continue;
        }

        patLen = min(36u, sfti->BufLen); /* full ACPI header for unique match */
        pat = (PUCHAR)SafeAllocatePool(patLen, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!pat) { ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); continue; }
        RtlCopyMemory(pat, sfti->Buf, patLen);
        RtlZeroMemory(sfti->Buf, sfti->BufLen); /* prevent freed sfti from self-matching */
        ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

        anchor = (PUCHAR)SafeAllocatePool(16, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!anchor) { ExFreePoolWithTag(pat, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); continue; }
        base = (PUCHAR)((ULONG_PTR)anchor & ~(ULONG_PTR)0xFFF);
        ExFreePoolWithTag(anchor, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

        found = FALSE;
        for (delta = 0; delta > -(LONG_PTR)(512ULL << 20) && !found; delta -= 0x1000) {
            PUCHAR page = base + delta;
            ULONG  off;
            if ((ULONG_PTR)page < 0xFFFF800000000000ULL) break;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(page)) continue;
            for (off = 0; off + patLen <= 0x1000; off += 8) {
                PUCHAR cand = page + off;
                if (!MmIsAddressValid(cand + patLen - 1)) break;
                if (RtlCompareMemory(cand, pat, patLen) != patLen) continue;
                __try {
                    RtlCopyMemory(cand + 10, acpiOemIdChoices[oemChoice], 6);
                    RtlCopyMemory(cand + 16, acpiOemTblChoices[oemChoice], 8);
                } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] ACPI %.4s OEM ID patched @ %p -> %.6s/%.8s\n",
                    (char*)&aclTableIds[tblIdx], cand,
                    acpiOemIdChoices[oemChoice], acpiOemTblChoices[oemChoice]);
                found = TRUE;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!found)
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchAcpiOemIds: %.4s not found in pool scan\n",
                     (char*)&aclTableIds[tblIdx]);

        ExFreePoolWithTag(pat, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    }
    } /* SFTI3 scope */
}

/* ============================================================
 * USB DEVICE SERIAL SPOOFING
 * Enumerates non-synthetic USB device instance keys (real hardware
 * serial numbers, not "5&..." topology IDs) and stores old→new
 * mappings so the CM callback can alias them during PnP enumeration.
 * Risk: Zero — CM callback intercepts in-memory reads only; the
 * actual registry subkey names are never renamed.
 * ============================================================ */


NTSTATUS CleanAllArtifacts(VOID) {
    NTSTATUS st = STATUS_SUCCESS;

    if (g_ArtifactsCleaned) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: already run — skipping\n");
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: starting artifact cleanup pass\n");

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [1/17] NVIDIA GPU UUID\n");
    SpoofNvidiaGpuUuid();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [2/17] SMBIOS registry cache\n");
    PatchSmbiosRegistryCache();

    if (g_SMBIOSSpoofActive) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [3/17] motherboard UUID keys (uuid=%02X%02X%02X%02X...)\n",
            g_SpoofedUUID[0], g_SpoofedUUID[1], g_SpoofedUUID[2], g_SpoofedUUID[3]);
        PatchMotherboardUuidKeys(g_SpoofedUUID);
    } else {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [3/17] skipping motherboard UUID (SMBIOS not spoofed yet)\n");
    }

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [4/17] NVIDIA registry\n");
    SpoofNvidiaRegistry();
    SpoofAmdGpuRegistry();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [5/17] extra registry 2 (SystemInformation, WU, SQM)\n");
    SpoofExtraRegistry2();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [6/17] GPU PCI HardwareIDs + DriverDesc\n");
    PatchGpuPciHardwareIds();
    PatchGpuDriverDesc();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [7/17] volume serials\n");
    AutoSpoofVolumeSerials();
    CleanVolumeGuidRegistry();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [8/17] USN journal reset\n");
    ResetUsnJournals();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [9/17] Boot identifier GUID\n");
    {
        NTSTATUS bst = PatchBootIdentifier();
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(bst))
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: BootIdentifier patch returned 0x%08X\n", bst);
    }

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [10/17] NIC interface GUIDs\n");
    SpoofNicInterfaceGuids();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [11/17] ACPI DSDT/FACP OEM IDs\n");
    PatchAcpiOemIds();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [12/17] USB device serials\n");
    {
        NTSTATUS ust = SpoofUsbDeviceSerials();
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(ust))
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: USB serial spoof returned 0x%08X\n", ust);
    }

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [13/17] NIC driver description\n");
    PatchNicDriverDesc();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [14/17] HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\n");
    SpoofHardwareDescRegistry();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [15/17] DHCP lease data\n");
    SpoofDhcpLeaseData();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [16/17] network profile signatures (DefaultGatewayMac)\n");
    ClearNetworkProfiles();

    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: [17/17] NVMe \\Enum\\NVMe serial aliasing\n");
    {
        NTSTATUS nst = SpoofNvmeEnumKeys();
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(nst))
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: NVMe enum spoof returned 0x%08X\n", nst);
    }

    g_ArtifactsCleaned = TRUE;
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] CleanAllArtifacts: complete\n");
    return st;
}



```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_gpu.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

static ULONG SubsysWcharToUlong(const WCHAR* s) {
    ULONG val = 0, i;
    for (i = 0; i < 8 && s[i]; i++) {
        WCHAR c = s[i]; ULONG n;
        if (c >= L'0' && c <= L'9') n = (ULONG)(c - L'0');
        else if (c >= L'A' && c <= L'F') n = (ULONG)(c - L'A') + 10;
        else if (c >= L'a' && c <= L'f') n = (ULONG)(c - L'a') + 10;
        else break;
        val = (val << 4) | n;
    }
    return val;
}

static ULONG PciReadConfigDword(UCHAR Bus, UCHAR Dev, UCHAR Func, UCHAR Offset) {
    ULONG addr = 0x80000000UL | ((ULONG)Bus  << 16) |
                                ((ULONG)Dev  << 11) |
                                ((ULONG)Func <<  8) |
                                (Offset & 0xFC);
    WRITE_PORT_ULONG((PULONG)(ULONG_PTR)0xCF8, addr);
    return READ_PORT_ULONG((PULONG)(ULONG_PTR)0xCFC);
}

VOID PciWriteConfigDword(UCHAR Bus, UCHAR Dev, UCHAR Func, UCHAR Offset, ULONG Value) {
    ULONG addr = 0x80000000UL | ((ULONG)Bus  << 16) |
                                ((ULONG)Dev  << 11) |
                                ((ULONG)Func <<  8) |
                                (Offset & 0xFC);
    WRITE_PORT_ULONG((PULONG)(ULONG_PTR)0xCF8, addr);
    WRITE_PORT_ULONG((PULONG)(ULONG_PTR)0xCFC, Value);
}

NTSTATUS SpoofGPUIds(PSPOOF_GPU_REQUEST req) {
    UCHAR    bus, dev;
    ULONG    patchCount = 0;
    ULONG    origVendorDevice = 0;
    ULONG    origSubSys = 0;
    ULONG    newVendorDevice = 0;

    /* Scan PCI buses 0-15 for ALL display controllers (class 0x03).
     * Patch every GPU found — covers multi-GPU, PCIe-switch configs. */
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] SpoofGPUIds: scanning PCI buses 0-15 for display adapters\n");
    for (bus = 0; bus < 16; bus++) {
        for (dev = 0; dev < 32; dev++) {
            ULONG vd       = PciReadConfigDword(bus, dev, 0, 0x00);
            ULONG classRev = PciReadConfigDword(bus, dev, 0, 0x08);

            if ((vd & 0xFFFF) == 0xFFFF) continue;       /* empty slot */
            if ((classRev >> 24) != 0x03) continue;       /* not Display */
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] SpoofGPUIds: display adapter at bus %u dev %u"
                " vendor=%04X device=%04X\n",
                bus, dev, vd & 0xFFFF, vd >> 16);

            origVendorDevice = vd;
            origSubSys       = PciReadConfigDword(bus, dev, 0, 0x2C);

            /* If caller left VendorId == 0 keep the real vendor so the
             * bound display driver doesn't lose its device match.        */
            if (req->VendorId == 0) {
                newVendorDevice = (origVendorDevice & 0x0000FFFF) |
                                  ((req->DeviceId & 0xFFFF) << 16);
            } else {
                newVendorDevice = (req->VendorId & 0xFFFF) |
                                  ((req->DeviceId & 0xFFFF) << 16);
            }

            /* Writing PCI VID/DID to hardware via CF8/CFC causes NVIDIA's background
             * health-check thread to read back a mismatched DeviceID ~seconds later,
             * triggering TDR escalation and a hardware reset with no BSOD.
             * EAC reads GPU IDs from the registry (patched by PatchGpuPciHardwareIds),
             * not from raw PCI config space, so these writes are unnecessary. */
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] GPU [%02X:%02X.0] PCI %08X -> %08X (reg-only) SubSys %08X -> %08X (reg-only)\n",
                     bus, dev, origVendorDevice, newVendorDevice, origSubSys, req->SubSysId);
            patchCount++;
        }
    }

    if (patchCount == 0) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofGPUIds: no display adapter found in PCI scan\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofGPUIds: patched %u display adapter(s)\n", patchCount);

    g_OrigGPUVendorDevice = origVendorDevice;
    g_NewGPUVendorDevice  = newVendorDevice;
    g_GPUSpoofActive      = TRUE;
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}


NTSTATUS NvlddmkmCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PVOID  buf = NULL;
    ULONG  len;
    PUCHAR uuid;
    KIRQL  irql;
    ULONG  i;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    /* UserBuffer is user-mode memory — unsafe at DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    if (len < NVIDIA_UUID_BUF_OFFSET + 40) goto done;
    if (Irp->MdlAddress)
        buf = MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);
    if (!buf && Irp->UserBuffer && MmIsAddressValid(Irp->UserBuffer))
        buf = Irp->UserBuffer;
    if (!buf && Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer)
        buf = Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf) goto done;
    __try {
        /* Locate "GPU-" marker starting at the expected offset */
        uuid = (PUCHAR)buf + NVIDIA_UUID_BUF_OFFSET;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(uuid + 39)) goto done;
        if (!(uuid[0]=='G'&&uuid[1]=='P'&&uuid[2]=='U'&&uuid[3]=='-')) {
            ULONG off;
            BOOLEAN found = FALSE;
            for (off = 0; off + 40 <= len; off++) {
                PUCHAR c = (PUCHAR)buf + off;
                if (!MmIsAddressValid(c + 39)) break;
                if (c[0]=='G'&&c[1]=='P'&&c[2]=='U'&&c[3]=='-') {
                    uuid = c; found = TRUE; break;
                }
            }
            if (!found) goto done;
        }
        /* Randomise hex digits after "GPU-" */
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        uuid += 4;
        for (i = 0; i < 36; i++) {
            if (uuid[i] == '-') continue;
            uuid[i] = "0123456789abcdef"[KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xF];
        }
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * NVIDIA GPU UUID — filter device dispatch
 *
 * Sits above nvlddmkm in the device stack (attached at init time).
 * nvlddmkm's own MajorFunction table is NEVER touched, so EAC's
 * dispatch-pointer scan sees only legitimate nvlddmkm.sys addresses.
 * IRP flow:  caller → [our GPU filter] → [nvlddmkm device]
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS NvlddmkmFilterDeviceControl(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION  ext   = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)DeviceObject->DeviceExtension;
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);

    if (!ext || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
    }

    if (g_NvidiaUuidSpoofed &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == NVIDIA_GPU_UUID_IOCTL) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, NvlddmkmCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }

    IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
}

NTSTATUS AttachFilterToNvidiaDevices(PDRIVER_OBJECT DriverObject) {
    NTSTATUS status;
    UNICODE_STRING nvName;
    PDRIVER_OBJECT nvDriver = NULL;
    PDEVICE_OBJECT nvDevice;

    if (!g_ObReferenceObjectByName || !g_IoDriverObjectType) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Undocumented APIs not resolved — skipping nvlddmkm filter\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_IMPLEMENTED;
    }

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&nvName, L"\\Driver\\nvlddmkm");
    status = g_ObReferenceObjectByName(
        &nvName,
        OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE,
        NULL, 0,
        *g_IoDriverObjectType,
        KernelMode, NULL,
        (PVOID*)&nvDriver
    );
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || !nvDriver) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] nvlddmkm not found (AMD/no GPU?): 0x%08X\n", status);
        return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
    }

    nvDevice = nvDriver->DeviceObject;
    if (nvDevice) ObReferenceObject(nvDevice);

    while (nvDevice && g_GpuFilterCount < MAX_GPU_FILTERS) {
        PDEVICE_OBJECT filterDevice = NULL;
        PDEVICE_OBJECT nextDevice;

        nextDevice = nvDevice->NextDevice;
        if (nextDevice) ObReferenceObject(nextDevice);

        status = IoCreateDevice(
            DriverObject,
            sizeof(FILTER_EXTENSION),
            NULL,
            nvDevice->DeviceType,
            0,
            FALSE,
            &filterDevice
        );
        if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
            PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)filterDevice->DeviceExtension;

            ext->Magic = FILTER_MAGIC;
            ext->Type  = FILTER_TYPE_GPU;

            filterDevice->Flags |= nvDevice->Flags & (DO_BUFFERED_IO | DO_DIRECT_IO);
            filterDevice->Characteristics |= nvDevice->Characteristics;

            ext->LowerDevice = IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack(filterDevice, nvDevice);
            if (ext->LowerDevice) {
                filterDevice->AlignmentRequirement = ext->LowerDevice->AlignmentRequirement;
                filterDevice->Flags &= ~DO_DEVICE_INITIALIZING;
                g_GpuFilterDevices[g_GpuFilterCount++] = filterDevice;
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] GPU filter attached to nvlddmkm dev %p (lower=%p)\n",
                         nvDevice, ext->LowerDevice);
            } else {
                IoDeleteDevice(filterDevice);
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack failed for nvlddmkm %p\n",
                         nvDevice);
            }
        } else {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] IoCreateDevice for GPU filter failed: 0x%08X\n", status);
        }

        ObDereferenceObject(nvDevice);
        nvDevice = nextDevice;
    }

    if (nvDevice) ObDereferenceObject(nvDevice);
    ObDereferenceObject(nvDriver);

    return (g_GpuFilterCount > 0) ? STATUS_SUCCESS : STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
}

/* ============================================================
 * AMD GPU FILTER — dispatch + completion
 *
 * Attaches above amdkmdag (primary) or atikmpag (legacy).
 * Intercepts AMD_IOCTL_QUERY_INFO responses and randomises any
 * 32-hex-char serial-like run in the output buffer.
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS AmdkmdagCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PVOID  buf = NULL;
    ULONG  len, i;
    KIRQL  irql;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto amd_done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto amd_done;
    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    if (len < 32) goto amd_done;

    if (Irp->MdlAddress)
        buf = MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);
    if (!buf && Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer)
        buf = Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!buf) goto amd_done;

    __try {
        /* Scan for a 32-char run of hex digits — AMD GPU serial format */
        PUCHAR p = (PUCHAR)buf;
        ULONG  hexRun = 0;
        for (i = 0; i < len && MmIsAddressValid(p + i); i++) {
            UCHAR c = p[i];
            if ((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F')) {
                hexRun++;
                if (hexRun >= 32) {
                    /* Found a 32+ hex-char run — randomise it */
                    ULONG startIdx = i - hexRun + 1;
                    ULONG j;
                    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
                    for (j = startIdx; j <= i; j++)
                        p[j] = "0123456789abcdef"[KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xF];
                    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
                    hexRun = 0;
                }
            } else {
                hexRun = 0;
            }
        }
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}

amd_done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

static BOOLEAN g_AmdUuidSpoofed = FALSE;

NTSTATUS AmdkmdagFilterDeviceControl(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION  ext   = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)DeviceObject->DeviceExtension;
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);

    if (!ext || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
    }

    if (g_AmdUuidSpoofed &&
        stack->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == AMD_IOCTL_QUERY_INFO) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, AmdkmdagCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }

    IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
}

NTSTATUS AttachFilterToAmdDevices(PDRIVER_OBJECT DriverObject) {
    static const LPCWSTR amdDriverNames[] = {
        L"\\Driver\\amdkmdag",
        L"\\Driver\\atikmpag",
        NULL
    };
    int nameIdx;
    NTSTATUS status = STATUS_NOT_FOUND;

    if (!g_ObReferenceObjectByName || !g_IoDriverObjectType) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Undocumented APIs not resolved — skipping amdkmdag filter\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_IMPLEMENTED;
    }

    for (nameIdx = 0; amdDriverNames[nameIdx]; nameIdx++) {
        UNICODE_STRING amdName;
        PDRIVER_OBJECT amdDriver = NULL;
        PDEVICE_OBJECT amdDevice;

        RtlInitUnicodeString(&amdName, amdDriverNames[nameIdx]);
        status = g_ObReferenceObjectByName(
            &amdName, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE, NULL, 0,
            *g_IoDriverObjectType, KernelMode, NULL, (PVOID*)&amdDriver);

        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || !amdDriver) {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] %S not found: 0x%08X\n", amdDriverNames[nameIdx], status);
            continue;
        }

        amdDevice = amdDriver->DeviceObject;
        if (amdDevice) ObReferenceObject(amdDevice);

        while (amdDevice && g_GpuFilterCount < MAX_GPU_FILTERS) {
            PDEVICE_OBJECT filterDevice = NULL;
            PDEVICE_OBJECT nextDevice   = amdDevice->NextDevice;
            if (nextDevice) ObReferenceObject(nextDevice);

            status = IoCreateDevice(DriverObject, sizeof(FILTER_EXTENSION), NULL,
                                    amdDevice->DeviceType, 0, FALSE, &filterDevice);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
                PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)filterDevice->DeviceExtension;
                ext->Magic = FILTER_MAGIC;
                ext->Type  = FILTER_TYPE_GPU_AMD;
                filterDevice->Flags |= amdDevice->Flags & (DO_BUFFERED_IO | DO_DIRECT_IO);
                filterDevice->Characteristics |= amdDevice->Characteristics;
                ext->LowerDevice = IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack(filterDevice, amdDevice);
                if (ext->LowerDevice) {
                    filterDevice->AlignmentRequirement = ext->LowerDevice->AlignmentRequirement;
                    filterDevice->Flags &= ~DO_DEVICE_INITIALIZING;
                    g_GpuFilterDevices[g_GpuFilterCount++] = filterDevice;
                    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] AMD GPU filter attached to %S dev %p\n",
                             amdDriverNames[nameIdx], amdDevice);
                } else {
                    IoDeleteDevice(filterDevice);
                    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] AMD IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack failed for %p\n", amdDevice);
                }
            }
            ObDereferenceObject(amdDevice);
            amdDevice = nextDevice;
        }
        if (amdDevice) ObDereferenceObject(amdDevice);
        ObDereferenceObject(amdDriver);

        if (g_GpuFilterCount > 0) {
            g_AmdUuidSpoofed = TRUE;
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] AMD GPU filter active via %S\n", amdDriverNames[nameIdx]);
            return STATUS_SUCCESS;
        }
    }
    return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
}


NTSTATUS SpoofNvidiaGpuUuid(VOID) {
    if (g_NvidiaUuidSpoofed) return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    if (g_GpuFilterCount == 0) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] GPU filters not attached — no NVIDIA device\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
    }
    g_NvidiaUuidSpoofed = TRUE;
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] nvlddmkm GPU UUID spoofing active\n");
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * SMBIOS REGISTRY CACHE SYNC
 * After patching the in-memory SMBIOS buffer, write it to
 * HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Services\mssmbios\Data\SMBiosData
 * so WMI queries (Win32_BaseBoard etc.) return spoofed data.
 * ============================================================ */


NTSTATUS SpoofNvidiaRegistry(VOID) {
    UCHAR  uuid[16];
    WCHAR  guidStr[48];
    KIRQL  irql;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    KernelGenUUID(uuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidStr, sizeof(guidStr),
        L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
        uuid[8],uuid[9], uuid[10],uuid[11],
        uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);

    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\NVIDIA Corporation\\Global",
        L"ClientUUID", guidStr);

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    KernelGenUUID(uuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidStr, sizeof(guidStr),
        L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
        uuid[8],uuid[9], uuid[10],uuid[11],
        uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\NVIDIA Corporation\\Global",
        L"PersistenceIdentifier", guidStr);

    /* ChipsetMatchID — random hex string */
    {
        CHAR chipId[17];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenSerial(chipId, 16, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RegSetStringA(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\NVIDIA Corporation\\Global\\CoProcManager",
            L"ChipsetMatchID", chipId);
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NVIDIA registry UUIDs randomised\n");
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================ */

VOID SubsysUlongToWchar(ULONG val, PWCHAR hex8) {
    LONG i;
    for (i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
        ULONG nibble = val & 0xF;
        hex8[i] = (nibble < 10) ? (L'0' + (WCHAR)nibble) : (L'A' + (WCHAR)(nibble - 10));
        val >>= 4;
    }
}

static ULONG SubsysMapGetOrCreate(ULONG orig) {
    ULONG i;
    KIRQL irql;
    ULONG newVal;
    for (i = 0; i < g_SubsysMapCount; i++)
        if (g_SubsysMap[i].Orig == orig) return g_SubsysMap[i].New;
    if (g_SubsysMapCount >= MAX_SUBSYS_MAP) return 0;
    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    newVal = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
    g_SubsysMap[g_SubsysMapCount].Orig = orig;
    g_SubsysMap[g_SubsysMapCount].New  = newVal;
    g_SubsysMapCount++;
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    return newVal;
}

/* Reverse lookup: given a new value, return the original (0 = not found). */
static ULONG SubsysMapFindOrig(ULONG newVal) {
    ULONG i;
    for (i = 0; i < g_SubsysMapCount; i++)
        if (g_SubsysMap[i].New == newVal) return g_SubsysMap[i].Orig;
    return 0;
}

/* --- DEV_ (4 hex char) helpers ---- */
static USHORT DevWcharToUshort(PWCHAR hex4) {
    USHORT val = 0; ULONG i;
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        WCHAR c = hex4[i]; ULONG n;
        if (c >= L'0' && c <= L'9') n = c - L'0';
        else if (c >= L'A' && c <= L'F') n = c - L'A' + 10;
        else if (c >= L'a' && c <= L'f') n = c - L'a' + 10;
        else return 0;
        val = (USHORT)((val << 4) | n);
    }
    return val;
}

VOID DevUshortToWchar(USHORT val, PWCHAR hex4) {
    LONG i;
    for (i = 3; i >= 0; i--) {
        ULONG nibble = val & 0xF;
        hex4[i] = (nibble < 10) ? (L'0' + (WCHAR)nibble) : (L'A' + (WCHAR)(nibble - 10));
        val >>= 4;
    }
}

static USHORT DevMapGetOrCreate(USHORT orig) {
    ULONG i; KIRQL irql; USHORT newVal;
    for (i = 0; i < g_DevMapCount; i++)
        if (g_DevMap[i].Orig == orig) return g_DevMap[i].New;
    if (g_DevMapCount >= MAX_DEV_MAP) return 0;
    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    newVal = (USHORT)(KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xFFFF);
    if (newVal == 0) newVal = 0x1234;
    g_DevMap[g_DevMapCount].Orig = orig;
    g_DevMap[g_DevMapCount].New  = newVal;
    g_DevMapCount++;
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    return newVal;
}

static USHORT DevMapFindOrig(USHORT newVal) {
    ULONG i;
    for (i = 0; i < g_DevMapCount; i++)
        if (g_DevMap[i].New == newVal) return g_DevMap[i].Orig;
    return 0;
}

VOID PatchGpuPciHardwareIds(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING enumPciPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hPci = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG devIdx = 0;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&enumPciPath,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\PCI");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &enumPciPath,
        OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hPci, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchGpuPciHardwareIds: can't open PCI Enum: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return;
    }

    for (;;) {
        ULONG needed = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION kbi;
        HANDLE hDev = NULL;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaDev;
        UNICODE_STRING devNameUs;
        WCHAR devName[128];
        USHORT copyLen;

        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hPci, devIdx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (needed == 0 || needed > 0x1000) { devIdx++; continue; }

        kbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(needed + 8, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!kbi) { devIdx++; continue; }
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hPci, devIdx, KeyBasicInformation, kbi, needed, &needed);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { ExFreePoolWithTag(kbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); devIdx++; continue; }

        /* Process any VEN_XXXX key that contains SUBSYS_ — covers GPU, NIC, and all other
         * PCI devices.  Skip keys that don't start with VEN_ (e.g. instance IDs like
         * "4&2283F625&0&0019") by checking the first 4 chars.                            */
        if (kbi->NameLength >= 8 * sizeof(WCHAR) &&
            (kbi->Name[0] == L'V' || kbi->Name[0] == L'v') &&
            (kbi->Name[1] == L'E' || kbi->Name[1] == L'e') &&
            (kbi->Name[2] == L'N' || kbi->Name[2] == L'n') &&
             kbi->Name[3] == L'_') {

            copyLen = (USHORT)min(kbi->NameLength, sizeof(devName) - sizeof(WCHAR));
            RtlCopyMemory(devName, kbi->Name, copyLen);
            devName[copyLen / sizeof(WCHAR)] = L'\0';
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&devNameUs, devName);
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaDev, &devNameUs,
                OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hPci, NULL);

            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hDev, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oaDev))) {
                ULONG instIdx = 0;
                for (;;) {
                    ULONG instNeeded = 0;
                    PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION instKbi;
                    HANDLE hInst = NULL;
                    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaInst;
                    UNICODE_STRING instUs;
                    WCHAR instName[64];
                    USHORT iCopyLen;
                    ULONG vi;
                    static const WCHAR* patchVals[] = { L"HardwareID", L"CompatibleIDs" };

                    st = ZwEnumerateKey(hDev, instIdx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &instNeeded);
                    if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
                    if (instNeeded == 0 || instNeeded > 0x800) { instIdx++; continue; }

                    instKbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(instNeeded + 8, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                    if (!instKbi) { instIdx++; continue; }
                    st = ZwEnumerateKey(hDev, instIdx, KeyBasicInformation, instKbi, instNeeded, &instNeeded);
                    if (NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
                        iCopyLen = (USHORT)min(instKbi->NameLength, sizeof(instName) - sizeof(WCHAR));
                        RtlCopyMemory(instName, instKbi->Name, iCopyLen);
                        instName[iCopyLen / sizeof(WCHAR)] = L'\0';
                        RtlInitUnicodeString(&instUs, instName);
                        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaInst, &instUs,
                            OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hDev, NULL);

                        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaInst))) {
                            for (vi = 0; vi < 2; vi++) {
                                UCHAR valBuf[1024];
                                ULONG valLen = sizeof(valBuf);
                                UNICODE_STRING vname;
                                PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION vpi =
                                    (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)valBuf;
                                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, patchVals[vi]);
                                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hInst, &vname,
                                        KeyValuePartialInformation, vpi, valLen, &valLen)) &&
                                    vpi->Type == REG_MULTI_SZ && vpi->DataLength > 4) {
                                    PWCHAR p = (PWCHAR)vpi->Data;
                                    ULONG rem = vpi->DataLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
                                    BOOLEAN modified = FALSE;
                                    while (rem > 0 && *p) {
                                        ULONG slen = 0;
                                        PWCHAR q = p;
                                        ULONG si;
                                        while (slen < rem && *q) { q++; slen++; }
                                        /* Replace the 8 hex chars that follow SUBSYS_ */
                                        for (si = 0; si + 14 <= slen; si++) {
                                            if ((p[si]   == L'S' || p[si]   == L's') &&
                                                (p[si+1] == L'U' || p[si+1] == L'u') &&
                                                (p[si+2] == L'B' || p[si+2] == L'b') &&
                                                (p[si+3] == L'S' || p[si+3] == L's') &&
                                                (p[si+4] == L'Y' || p[si+4] == L'y') &&
                                                (p[si+5] == L'S' || p[si+5] == L's') &&
                                                p[si+6] == L'_') {
                                                ULONG origVal = SubsysWcharToUlong(p + si + 7);
                                                ULONG newVal  = SubsysMapGetOrCreate(origVal);
                                                WCHAR newHex[8];
                                                SubsysUlongToWchar(newVal, newHex);
                                                RtlCopyMemory(p + si + 7, newHex,
                                                              8 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                                modified = TRUE;
                                                break;
                                            }
                                        }
                                        /* Also replace the 4 hex chars that follow DEV_ */
                                        for (si = 0; si + 8 <= slen; si++) {
                                            if ((p[si]   == L'D' || p[si]   == L'd') &&
                                                (p[si+1] == L'E' || p[si+1] == L'e') &&
                                                (p[si+2] == L'V' || p[si+2] == L'v') &&
                                                 p[si+3] == L'_') {
                                                USHORT origDev = DevWcharToUshort(p + si + 4);
                                                USHORT newDev  = DevMapGetOrCreate(origDev);
                                                WCHAR newHex4[4];
                                                if (newDev) {
                                                    DevUshortToWchar(newDev, newHex4);
                                                    RtlCopyMemory(p + si + 4, newHex4,
                                                                  4 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                                    modified = TRUE;
                                                }
                                                break;
                                            }
                                        }
                                        p   += slen + 1;
                                        rem -= slen + 1;
                                    }
                                    if (modified)
                                        ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &vname, 0, REG_MULTI_SZ,
                                                      vpi->Data, vpi->DataLength);
                                }
                            }
                            ZwClose(hInst);
                        }
                    }
                    ExFreePoolWithTag(instKbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                    instIdx++;
                }
                ZwClose(hDev);
            }
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(kbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        devIdx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hPci);

    /* Activate the CM registry callback so WMI PNPDeviceID enumeration also
     * returns the spoofed SUBSYS (original ↔ spoofed redirect pair).       */
    if (g_SubsysMapCount > 0) {
        g_SubsysSpoofActive = TRUE;
        RegisterPciEnumCallback();
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PCI HardwareID/CompatibleIDs patched: %u device class(es)\n",
        g_SubsysMapCount);
}

/* ============================================================
 * PCI ENUM REGISTRY CALLBACK
 *
 * Intercepts two registry notification classes:
 *
 * RegNtPostEnumerateKey — fires when someone enumerates subkeys of
 *   HKLM\...\Enum\PCI.  For any returned key name that begins with
 *   VEN_10DE, the SUBSYS_XXXXXXXX portion is replaced with the
 *   spoofed value so WMI/umpnpmgr builds a patched PNPDeviceID.
 *
 * RegNtPreOpenKey/Ex — fires before any ZwOpenKey call.  When the
 *   target path contains the spoofed SUBSYS, we substitute the
 *   original SUBSYS back so the actual kernel open succeeds (the
 *   real key still has its original name on disk).
 *
 * Together these implement a transparent SUBSYS alias: callers see
 * the spoofed name, but every open that follows is silently
 * redirected to the real registry key.
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS NTAPI PciEnumRegistryCallback(PVOID Ctx, PVOID Arg1, PVOID Arg2) {
    REG_NOTIFY_CLASS cls = (REG_NOTIFY_CLASS)(ULONG_PTR)Arg1;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Ctx);
    if (!g_SubsysSpoofActive && !g_NicGuidSpoofActive &&
        !g_UsbSerialSpoofActive && !g_NvmeSpoofActive)
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;

    /* ---- post-enumerate: orig SUBSYS → spoofed in any VEN_ key name ---- */
    if (cls == RegNtPostEnumerateKey) {
        PREG_POST_OPERATION_INFORMATION post = (PREG_POST_OPERATION_INFORMATION)Arg2;
        if (NT_SUCCESS(post->Status) && post->PreInformation) {
            PREG_ENUMERATE_KEY_INFORMATION pre =
                (PREG_ENUMERATE_KEY_INFORMATION)post->PreInformation;
            if (pre->KeyInformationClass == KeyBasicInformation) {
                PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION ki = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)pre->KeyInformation;
                if (ki && MmIsAddressValid(ki) && ki->NameLength >= 8 * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                    PWCHAR n  = ki->Name;
                    ULONG  nc = ki->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
                    /* Any VEN_ key (GPU, NIC, audio, USB controller, ...) */
                    if ((n[0] == L'V' || n[0] == L'v') &&
                        (n[1] == L'E' || n[1] == L'e') &&
                        (n[2] == L'N' || n[2] == L'n') &&
                         n[3] == L'_') {
                        ULONG si;
                        __try {
                            for (si = 0; si + 14 <= nc; si++) {
                                if ((n[si]   == L'S' || n[si]   == L's') &&
                                    (n[si+1] == L'U' || n[si+1] == L'u') &&
                                    (n[si+2] == L'B' || n[si+2] == L'b') &&
                                    (n[si+3] == L'S' || n[si+3] == L's') &&
                                    (n[si+4] == L'Y' || n[si+4] == L'y') &&
                                    (n[si+5] == L'S' || n[si+5] == L's') &&
                                     n[si+6] == L'_') {
                                    ULONG origVal = SubsysWcharToUlong(n + si + 7);
                                    ULONG newVal  = SubsysMapGetOrCreate(origVal);
                                    WCHAR newHex[8];
                                    SubsysUlongToWchar(newVal, newHex);
                                    RtlCopyMemory(n + si + 7, newHex, 8 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                            /* Also patch DEV_XXXX in returned key name */
                            for (si = 0; si + 8 <= nc; si++) {
                                if ((n[si]   == L'D' || n[si]   == L'd') &&
                                    (n[si+1] == L'E' || n[si+1] == L'e') &&
                                    (n[si+2] == L'V' || n[si+2] == L'v') &&
                                     n[si+3] == L'_') {
                                    USHORT origDev = DevWcharToUshort(n + si + 4);
                                    USHORT newDev  = DevMapGetOrCreate(origDev);
                                    WCHAR newHex4[4];
                                    if (newDev) {
                                        DevUshortToWchar(newDev, newHex4);
                                        RtlCopyMemory(n + si + 4, newHex4, 4 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    }
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
                    }
                    /* ---- NIC interface GUID alias ---- */
                    /* Tcpip\Parameters\Interfaces enumerates {guid} as child key names.
                     * Replace the old GUID (stored in registry) with the spoofed one.   */
                    __try {
                        if (g_NicGuidSpoofActive && nc == 38 &&
                            n[0] == L'{' && n[9] == L'-' && n[37] == L'}') {
                            ULONG gi;
                            for (gi = 0; gi < g_NicGuidCount; gi++) {
                                if (RtlCompareMemory(n, g_NicGuids[gi].Old,
                                                     38 * sizeof(WCHAR)) == 38 * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    RtlCopyMemory(n, g_NicGuids[gi].New, 38 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        /* ---- USB device serial alias ---- */
                        if (g_UsbSerialSpoofActive && nc >= 4 && nc <= MAX_USB_SER_CHARS) {
                            ULONG ui;
                            for (ui = 0; ui < g_UsbSerialCount; ui++) {
                                if (g_UsbSerials[ui].Len == (USHORT)nc &&
                                    RtlCompareMemory(n, g_UsbSerials[ui].Old,
                                                     nc * sizeof(WCHAR)) == nc * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    RtlCopyMemory(n, g_UsbSerials[ui].New, nc * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        /* ---- NVMe enum serial alias ---- */
                        if (g_NvmeSpoofActive && nc >= 4 && nc <= MAX_USB_SER_CHARS) {
                            ULONG ni;
                            for (ni = 0; ni < g_NvmeSerialCount; ni++) {
                                if (g_NvmeSerials[ni].Len == (USHORT)nc &&
                                    RtlCompareMemory(n, g_NvmeSerials[ni].Old,
                                                     nc * sizeof(WCHAR)) == nc * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    RtlCopyMemory(n, g_NvmeSerials[ni].New, nc * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
                }
            }
        }
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    /* ---- pre-open/ex: spoofed SUBSYS/DEV → orig so ZwOpenKey finds the real key ---- */
    if (cls == RegNtPreOpenKey || cls == RegNtPreOpenKeyEx) {
        PUNICODE_STRING cn = *(PUNICODE_STRING*)Arg2;
        if (cn && MmIsAddressValid(cn) && cn->Buffer && cn->Length >= 16 * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
            PWCHAR buf  = cn->Buffer;
            ULONG  blen = cn->Length / sizeof(WCHAR);
            __try {
                ULONG si;
                for (si = 0; si + 14 <= blen; si++) {
                    if ((buf[si]   == L'S' || buf[si]   == L's') &&
                        (buf[si+1] == L'U' || buf[si+1] == L'u') &&
                        (buf[si+2] == L'B' || buf[si+2] == L'b') &&
                        (buf[si+3] == L'S' || buf[si+3] == L's') &&
                        (buf[si+4] == L'Y' || buf[si+4] == L'y') &&
                        (buf[si+5] == L'S' || buf[si+5] == L's') &&
                         buf[si+6] == L'_') {
                        ULONG newVal  = SubsysWcharToUlong(buf + si + 7);
                        ULONG origVal = SubsysMapFindOrig(newVal);
                        if (origVal) {
                            WCHAR origHex[8];
                            SubsysUlongToWchar(origVal, origHex);
                            RtlCopyMemory(buf + si + 7, origHex, 8 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
                for (si = 0; si + 8 <= blen; si++) {
                    if ((buf[si]   == L'D' || buf[si]   == L'd') &&
                        (buf[si+1] == L'E' || buf[si+1] == L'e') &&
                        (buf[si+2] == L'V' || buf[si+2] == L'v') &&
                         buf[si+3] == L'_') {
                        USHORT newDev  = DevWcharToUshort(buf + si + 4);
                        USHORT origDev = DevMapFindOrig(newDev);
                        if (origDev) {
                            WCHAR origHex4[4];
                            DevUshortToWchar(origDev, origHex4);
                            RtlCopyMemory(buf + si + 4, origHex4, 4 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
            /* ---- NIC GUID reverse alias: spoofed → orig so ZwOpenKey hits real key ---- */
            __try {
                if (g_NicGuidSpoofActive && blen >= 38) {
                    LONG bpos;
                    for (bpos = (LONG)blen - 38; bpos >= 0; bpos--) {
                        if (buf[bpos] == L'{' && buf[bpos + 9] == L'-' &&
                            buf[bpos + 37] == L'}') {
                            ULONG gi;
                            for (gi = 0; gi < g_NicGuidCount; gi++) {
                                if (RtlCompareMemory(buf + bpos, g_NicGuids[gi].New,
                                                     38 * sizeof(WCHAR)) == 38 * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    RtlCopyMemory(buf + bpos, g_NicGuids[gi].Old,
                                                  38 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    bpos = -1;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                            if (bpos == -1) break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
            /* ---- USB serial reverse alias: spoofed → orig so ZwOpenKey hits real key ---- */
            __try {
                if (g_UsbSerialSpoofActive && blen >= 4) {
                    LONG lbs = -1, bi;
                    for (bi = (LONG)blen - 1; bi >= 0; bi--) {
                        if (buf[bi] == L'\\') { lbs = bi; break; }
                    }
                    if (lbs >= 0) {
                        ULONG serStart = (ULONG)lbs + 1;
                        ULONG serLen   = blen - serStart;
                        if (serLen >= 4 && serLen <= MAX_USB_SER_CHARS) {
                            ULONG ui;
                            for (ui = 0; ui < g_UsbSerialCount; ui++) {
                                if (g_UsbSerials[ui].Len == (USHORT)serLen &&
                                    RtlCompareMemory(buf + serStart, g_UsbSerials[ui].New,
                                                     serLen * sizeof(WCHAR)) ==
                                    serLen * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    RtlCopyMemory(buf + serStart, g_UsbSerials[ui].Old,
                                                  serLen * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
            /* ---- NVMe serial reverse alias: spoofed → orig so ZwOpenKey hits real key ---- */
            __try {
                if (g_NvmeSpoofActive && blen >= 4) {
                    LONG lbs = -1, bi;
                    for (bi = (LONG)blen - 1; bi >= 0; bi--) {
                        if (buf[bi] == L'\\') { lbs = bi; break; }
                    }
                    if (lbs >= 0) {
                        ULONG serStart = (ULONG)lbs + 1;
                        ULONG serLen   = blen - serStart;
                        if (serLen >= 4 && serLen <= MAX_USB_SER_CHARS) {
                            ULONG ni;
                            for (ni = 0; ni < g_NvmeSerialCount; ni++) {
                                if (g_NvmeSerials[ni].Len == (USHORT)serLen &&
                                    RtlCompareMemory(buf + serStart, g_NvmeSerials[ni].New,
                                                     serLen * sizeof(WCHAR)) ==
                                    serLen * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    RtlCopyMemory(buf + serStart, g_NvmeSerials[ni].Old,
                                                  serLen * sizeof(WCHAR));
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
        }
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

VOID RegisterPciEnumCallback(VOID) {
    NTSTATUS st;
    if (g_PciEnumCbActive) return;
    st = CmRegisterCallback(PciEnumRegistryCallback, NULL, &g_PciEnumCbCookie);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] RegisterPciEnumCallback: CmRegisterCallback failed 0x%08X\n", st);
        return;
    }
    g_PciEnumCbActive = TRUE;
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PCI enum CM callback registered (%u SUBSYS mappings)\n",
        g_SubsysMapCount);
}

VOID UnregisterPciEnumCallback(VOID) {
    if (!g_PciEnumCbActive) return;
    CmUnRegisterCallback(g_PciEnumCbCookie);
    g_PciEnumCbActive = FALSE;
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PCI enum CM callback unregistered\n");
}


VOID PatchGpuDriverDesc(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR gpuClassPath =
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
        L"\\{4D36E97B-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}";
    static const LPCWSTR fakeGpuNames[] = {
        L"NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3060",
        L"NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3070",
        L"NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3080",
        L"NVIDIA GeForce GTX 1660 Ti",
        L"AMD Radeon RX 6600 XT",
    };
    UNICODE_STRING classPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hClass = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG idx = 0;
    ULONG nameIdx;
    KIRQL irql;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    nameIdx = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) % 5;
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&classPath, gpuClassPath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &classPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_READ, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return;

    for (;;) {
        ULONG len = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION ki;
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &len);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!len || len > 0x400) { idx++; continue; }
        ki = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(len + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!ki) { idx++; continue; }
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, ki, len, &len))) {
            UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)ki->NameLength, (USHORT)ki->NameLength, ki->Name };
            HANDLE hInst = NULL;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaI;
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaI, &sub,
                OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hClass, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaI))) {
                /* Only patch entries that already have a DriverDesc (device instance keys) */
                UNICODE_STRING vname;
                UCHAR qbuf[512];
                ULONG qlen = sizeof(qbuf);
                PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION kpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)qbuf;
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"DriverDesc");
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hInst, &vname,
                        KeyValuePartialInformation, kpi, qlen, &qlen)) &&
                    kpi->Type == REG_SZ && kpi->DataLength > 2) {
                    LPCWSTR fakeDesc = fakeGpuNames[nameIdx];
                    ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, (PVOID)fakeDesc,
                                  (ULONG)((wcslen(fakeDesc) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
                }
                ZwClose(hInst);
            }
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(ki, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        idx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hClass);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] GPU DriverDesc patched\n");
}

NTSTATUS SpoofAmdGpuRegistry(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING path;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hClass = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG idx = 0;
    WCHAR keyName[64];
    UNICODE_STRING kn;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&path,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
        L"\\{4d36e968-e325-11ce-bfc1-08002be10318}");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &path, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS | KEY_QUERY_VALUE, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return st;

    while (TRUE) {
        UCHAR kiBuf[256];
        KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION* ki = (KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION*)kiBuf;
        ULONG needed = 0;
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, ki, sizeof(kiBuf), &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { idx++; continue; }

        {
            ULONG nl = ki->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
            if (nl >= 64) { idx++; continue; }
            RtlCopyMemory(keyName, ki->Name, ki->NameLength);
            keyName[nl] = 0;
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&kn, keyName);
        }

        {
            HANDLE hInst = NULL;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES ia;
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&ia, &kn, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hClass, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &ia))) {
                UCHAR vBuf[512];
                UNICODE_STRING vname;
                ULONG rlen;
                KEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION* kv = (KEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION*)vBuf;
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"MatchingDeviceId");
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hInst, &vname, KeyValuePartialInformation,
                        vBuf, sizeof(vBuf), &rlen))) {
                    WCHAR* id = (WCHAR*)kv->Data;
                    ULONG si;
                    for (si = 0; id[si] && si + 7 < kv->DataLength / sizeof(WCHAR); si++) {
                        if (id[si]==L'S'&&id[si+1]==L'U'&&id[si+2]==L'B'&&
                            id[si+3]==L'S'&&id[si+4]==L'Y'&&id[si+5]==L'S'&&id[si+6]==L'_') {
                            ULONG orig = SubsysWcharToUlong(id + si + 7);
                            ULONG mapped = SubsysMapGetOrCreate(orig);
                            if (mapped && mapped != orig) SubsysUlongToWchar(mapped, id + si + 7);
                        }
                    }
                    ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &vname, 0, kv->Type, kv->Data, kv->DataLength);
                }
                ZwClose(hInst);
            }
        }
        idx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hClass);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] AMD GPU registry spoofed\n");
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

VOID PatchAmdVideoDeviceUids(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING path;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hPci = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG devIdx = 0;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&path,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\PCI");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &path, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hPci, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return;

    while (TRUE) {
        UCHAR kiBuf[512];
        KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION* ki = (KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION*)kiBuf;
        ULONG needed = 0;
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hPci, devIdx, KeyBasicInformation, ki, sizeof(kiBuf), &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { devIdx++; continue; }

        {
            WCHAR devKey[80]; ULONG nl = ki->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
            if (nl < 8 || nl >= 80) { devIdx++; continue; }
            RtlCopyMemory(devKey, ki->Name, ki->NameLength);
            devKey[nl] = 0;

            if ((devKey[0]==L'V'||devKey[0]==L'v') &&
                (devKey[1]==L'E'||devKey[1]==L'e') &&
                (devKey[2]==L'N'||devKey[2]==L'n') &&
                devKey[3]==L'_' &&
                devKey[4]==L'1' && devKey[5]==L'0' && devKey[6]==L'0' && devKey[7]==L'2') {

                UNICODE_STRING dn; OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES da; HANDLE hDev = NULL;
                dn.Buffer = devKey; dn.Length = (USHORT)(nl * sizeof(WCHAR));
                dn.MaximumLength = dn.Length + sizeof(WCHAR);
                InitializeObjectAttributes(&da, &dn, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hPci, NULL);
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hDev, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS | KEY_SET_VALUE, &da))) {
                    ULONG instIdx = 0;
                    while (TRUE) {
                        UCHAR iBuf[512];
                        KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION* iki = (KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION*)iBuf;
                        ULONG ineeded = 0;
                        if (ZwEnumerateKey(hDev, instIdx, KeyBasicInformation,
                                           iki, sizeof(iBuf), &ineeded) == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
                        instIdx++;
                    }
                    ZwClose(hDev);
                }
            }
        }
        devIdx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hPci);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] AMD video device UIDs patched\n");
}

```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_network.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

NTSTATUS SpoofNICAdapter(ULONG adapterIndex, PUCHAR newMAC) {
    NTSTATUS status;
    UNICODE_STRING regPath;
    WCHAR regPathBuf[512];
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES objAttrs;
    HANDLE hKey;
    UNICODE_STRING valueName;
    WCHAR macString[18];

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(regPathBuf, sizeof(regPathBuf),
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class\\"
        L"{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}\\%04d", adapterIndex);

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, regPathBuf);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&objAttrs, &regPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);

    status = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &objAttrs);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofNICAdapter: ZwOpenKey adapter %u failed: 0x%08X\n",
                 adapterIndex, status);
        return status;
    }

    /* Read current NetworkAddress before overwriting */
    {
        WCHAR oldAddr[20] = {0};
        UNICODE_STRING qvn;
        PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION kvpi;
        ULONG kvpiSz = sizeof(KEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION) + sizeof(oldAddr);
        kvpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(kvpiSz, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (kvpi) {
            ULONG rl = 0;
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&qvn, L"NetworkAddress");
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hKey, &qvn, KeyValuePartialInformation, kvpi, kvpiSz, &rl))
                    && kvpi->Type == REG_SZ)
                RtlCopyMemory(oldAddr, kvpi->Data, min(kvpi->DataLength, sizeof(oldAddr) - sizeof(WCHAR)));
            ExFreePoolWithTag(kvpi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] NIC %u NetworkAddress before: %S\n",
                adapterIndex, oldAddr[0] ? oldAddr : L"(unset)");
        }
    }

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(macString, sizeof(macString),
        L"%02X%02X%02X%02X%02X%02X",
        newMAC[0], newMAC[1], newMAC[2], newMAC[3], newMAC[4], newMAC[5]);

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&valueName, L"NetworkAddress");
    status = ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &valueName, 0, REG_SZ, macString,
                           (ULONG)(wcslen(macString) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR));
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofNICAdapter: ZwSetValueKey adapter %u failed: 0x%08X\n",
                 adapterIndex, status);
    } else {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] NIC %u NetworkAddress after:  %S\n", adapterIndex, macString);
    }
    ZwClose(hKey);

    if (g_NICSpoofCount < MAX_NIC_SPOOFS) {
        KIRQL oldIrql;
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);
        g_NICSpoofs[g_NICSpoofCount].AdapterIndex = adapterIndex;
        RtlCopyMemory(g_NICSpoofs[g_NICSpoofCount].SpoofedMAC, newMAC, 6);
        g_NICSpoofs[g_NICSpoofCount].Active = TRUE;
        g_NICSpoofCount++;
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);
    }

    return status;
}


NTSTATUS SpoofBluetoothMAC(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hRadios;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG idx = 0;
    WCHAR subKeyBuf[256];
    ULONG subKeyLen;
    PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION keyInfo;
    ULONG keyInfoSize = sizeof(KEY_BASIC_INFORMATION) + 256 * sizeof(WCHAR);

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Services\\BTHPORT\\Parameters\\Radios");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);

    st = ZwOpenKey(&hRadios, KEY_READ, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Bluetooth radios key not found: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return st;
    }

    keyInfo = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(keyInfoSize, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!keyInfo) { ZwClose(hRadios); return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; }

    for (idx = 0; ; idx++) {
        UNICODE_STRING radioPath;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES radioOa;
        HANDLE hRadio;
        UCHAR newMAC[6];
        KIRQL oldIrql;

        RtlZeroMemory(keyInfo, keyInfoSize);
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hRadios, idx, KeyBasicInformation, keyInfo, keyInfoSize, &subKeyLen);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) continue;

        /* Build full path to radio subkey */
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(subKeyBuf, sizeof(subKeyBuf),
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Services\\BTHPORT\\Parameters\\Radios\\%.*s",
            (int)(keyInfo->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR)), keyInfo->Name);

        RtlInitUnicodeString(&radioPath, subKeyBuf);
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&radioOa, &radioPath,
                                   OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hRadio, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &radioOa))) continue;

        /* Read current StoredLocalAddress before overwriting */
        {
            UCHAR origMAC[6] = {0};
            BOOLEAN hasOrig = FALSE;
            PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION kvpi;
            ULONG kvpiSz = sizeof(KEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION) + 8;
            kvpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(kvpiSz, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (kvpi) {
                UNICODE_STRING rn; ULONG rl = 0;
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&rn, L"StoredLocalAddress");
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hRadio, &rn, KeyValuePartialInformation, kvpi, kvpiSz, &rl))
                        && kvpi->Type == REG_BINARY && kvpi->DataLength >= 6) {
                    RtlCopyMemory(origMAC, kvpi->Data, 6);
                    hasOrig = TRUE;
                }
                ExFreePoolWithTag(kvpi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            }
            if (hasOrig)
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] BT radio %.*S before: %02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X\n",
                    (int)(keyInfo->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR)), keyInfo->Name,
                    origMAC[0], origMAC[1], origMAC[2], origMAC[3], origMAC[4], origMAC[5]);
        }

        /* Generate a new vendor-OUI MAC for BT radio */
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);
        KernelGenMAC(newMAC, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);

        {
            UNICODE_STRING vname;
            NTSTATUS btSt;
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"StoredLocalAddress");
            btSt = ZwSetValueKey(hRadio, &vname, 0, REG_BINARY, newMAC, 6);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(btSt))
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] BT radio %.*S after:  %02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X\n",
                    (int)(keyInfo->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR)), keyInfo->Name,
                    newMAC[0], newMAC[1], newMAC[2], newMAC[3], newMAC[4], newMAC[5]);
            else
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] BT radio %.*S ZwSetValueKey failed: 0x%08X\n",
                    (int)(keyInfo->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR)), keyInfo->Name, btSt);
        }
        ZwClose(hRadio);
    }

    ExFreePoolWithTag(keyInfo, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    ZwClose(hRadios);
    g_BluetoothSpoofed = TRUE;
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}


VOID SpoofNicInterfaceGuids(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR nicClassPath =
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
        L"\\{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}";
    UNICODE_STRING nicPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hClass = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG idx = 0;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&nicPath, nicClassPath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &nicPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_READ, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofNicInterfaceGuids: can't open NIC class: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return;
    }

    for (;;) {
        ULONG len = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION ki;
```
