```c
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &len);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!len || len > 0x400) { idx++; continue; }
        ki = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(len + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!ki) { idx++; continue; }
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, ki, len, &len))) {
            UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)ki->NameLength, (USHORT)ki->NameLength, ki->Name };
            HANDLE hInst = NULL;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaI;
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaI, &sub, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hClass, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaI))) {
                UCHAR qbuf[160];
                ULONG qlen = sizeof(qbuf);
                UNICODE_STRING vname;
                PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION vpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)qbuf;
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"NetCfgInstanceId");
                st = ZwQueryValueKey(hInst, &vname, KeyValuePartialInformation, vpi, qlen, &qlen);
                if (NT_SUCCESS(st) && vpi->Type == REG_SZ &&
                    vpi->DataLength >= 38 * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                    UCHAR uuid[16];
                    WCHAR newGuidStr[40];
                    KIRQL irql;
                    ULONG copyLen;

                    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
                    KernelGenUUID(uuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
                    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);

                    RtlStringCbPrintfW(newGuidStr, sizeof(newGuidStr),
                        L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
                        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
                        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
                        uuid[8],uuid[9],
                        uuid[10],uuid[11],uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);

                    if (g_NicGuidCount < MAX_NIC_GUID_SPOOFS) {
                        copyLen = min(vpi->DataLength / sizeof(WCHAR) - 1, 39u);
                        RtlCopyMemory(g_NicGuids[g_NicGuidCount].Old, vpi->Data,
                                      copyLen * sizeof(WCHAR));
                        g_NicGuids[g_NicGuidCount].Old[copyLen] = L'\0';
                        RtlCopyMemory(g_NicGuids[g_NicGuidCount].New, newGuidStr,
                                      39 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                        g_NicGuids[g_NicGuidCount].New[39] = L'\0';
                        g_NicGuidCount++;
                    }

                    ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, newGuidStr,
                                  (ULONG)((wcslen(newGuidStr) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
                    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NIC[%u] NetCfgInstanceId -> %S\n", idx, newGuidStr);
                }
                ZwClose(hInst);
            }
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(ki, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        idx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hClass);

    if (g_NicGuidCount > 0) {
        g_NicGuidSpoofActive = TRUE;
        RegisterPciEnumCallback(); /* ensure CM callback is active for alias routing */
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NIC interface GUIDs spoofed: %u adapter(s)\n", g_NicGuidCount);
    }
}

/* ============================================================
 * ACPI TABLE OEM ID SPOOFING
 * Patches OEM ID (offset 10, 6 bytes) and OEM Table ID (offset 16,
 * 8 bytes) in the kernel's live DSDT and FACP table buffers.
 * EAC reads these fields to fingerprint the motherboard vendor.
 * ============================================================ */


/* ============================================================
 * NETWORK SPOOFING
 * 1. NIC registry NetworkAddress
 * 2. NDISWAN + per-NIC dispatch hook (OID_802_3_PERMANENT/CURRENT_ADDRESS)
 * 3. NDIS IF_BLOCK in-place patch via ndis.sys global interface list
 * 4. nsiproxy dispatch hook (IOCTL_NSI_PROXY_ARP, ARP neighbor table)
 * 5. Bluetooth dispatch hook (BTHPORT/BTHUSB)
 * 6. TCP dispatch hook (IOCTL_TCP_QUERY_INFORMATION_EX)
 * NtAllocateUuids seed patch omitted: ntoskrnl.text scan produces
 * false-positive matches that corrupt svchost → 0xEF ~9 min post-boot.
 * ============================================================ */

/* --- Globals ------------------------------------------------ */

#define NET_MAX_NIC_DRIVERS  20
#define NET_MAX_MAC_CACHE    32

typedef struct { PDRIVER_OBJECT DriverObj; PDRIVER_DISPATCH OrigDispatch; } NET_NIC_ENTRY;
typedef struct { UCHAR Orig[6]; UCHAR Spoofed[6]; } NET_MAC_CACHE_ENTRY;
typedef struct { PIO_COMPLETION_ROUTINE OldRoutine; PVOID OldContext; } NET_IOC_CTX;

/* IF_PHYSICAL_ADDRESS_LH: USHORT Length + UCHAR Address[IF_MAX_PHYS_ADDRESS_LENGTH] */
#define IFPHYS_STRUCT_SIZE (sizeof(USHORT) + IF_MAX_PHYS_ADDRESS_LENGTH)
/* NDIS filter block */
typedef struct _NET_NDIS_FILTER { char _pad0[8]; struct _NET_NDIS_FILTER* Next; char _pad1[0x18]; PVOID FilterInstanceName; } NET_NDIS_FILTER;
/* KSTRING-like wrapper ndis.sys uses */
typedef struct { char _pad[0x10]; WCHAR Buffer[1]; } NET_KSTRING;

static UCHAR             g_NetSpoofedMAC[6]            = {0};
static BOOLEAN           g_NetMACReady                  = FALSE;
static ULONG             g_SpoofedMBRSig               = 0;
static NET_NIC_ENTRY     g_NicDrivers[NET_MAX_NIC_DRIVERS];
static int               g_NicDriverCount               = 0;
static NET_MAC_CACHE_ENTRY g_MacCache[NET_MAX_MAC_CACHE];
static int               g_MacCacheCount                = 0;
KSPIN_LOCK        g_NicLock;

static PDRIVER_DISPATCH  g_OrigNsiProxy  = NULL;
static PDRIVER_DISPATCH  g_OrigBth       = NULL;
/* Per-CPU reentrance depth for the nsiproxy hook.  nsiproxy can call back into
 * itself via IoCallDriver during ARP-table processing; without this guard the
 * two synchronous hooks drive each other into mutual recursion → stack overflow
 * → double fault (0x7F/8).  Only the outermost call (depth==1) patches MACs. */
static volatile LONG     g_NsiProxyDepth[64] = {0};
static PDRIVER_DISPATCH  g_OrigTcp       = NULL;

#define IOCTL_NSI_PROXY_ARP             0x0012001B
#define IOCTL_NDIS_QUERY_GLOBAL_STATS   0x00170002
#define IOCTL_TCP_QUERY_INFORMATION_EX  CTL_CODE(FILE_DEVICE_NETWORK, 0, METHOD_NEITHER, FILE_ANY_ACCESS)
#define OID_802_3_PERMANENT_ADDRESS     0x01010101
#define OID_802_3_CURRENT_ADDRESS       0x01010102

/* --- Helpers ------------------------------------------------ */

VOID NetGenMAC(UCHAR out[6], ULONG seed) {
    /* Real NIC vendor OUIs so EAC's locally-administered bit check doesn't fire */
    static const UCHAR ouiNet[24][3] = {
        {0x00, 0x1B, 0x21}, /* Intel */
        {0x8C, 0xEC, 0x4B}, /* Intel */
        {0xF4, 0x4D, 0x30}, /* Intel */
        {0xA0, 0x36, 0x9F}, /* Intel */
        {0x48, 0x51, 0xB7}, /* Intel */
        {0x00, 0xE0, 0x4C}, /* Realtek */
        {0x10, 0x02, 0xB5}, /* Realtek */
        {0x90, 0x2B, 0x34}, /* Realtek */
        {0xE0, 0x4F, 0x43}, /* Realtek */
        {0x40, 0xB0, 0x76}, /* Realtek */
        {0x18, 0xE7, 0x28}, /* Broadcom */
        {0x60, 0x67, 0x20}, /* Broadcom */
        {0x00, 0x90, 0x4C}, /* Broadcom */
        {0xB8, 0x27, 0xEB}, /* Broadcom */
        {0x00, 0x50, 0x43}, /* Marvell */
        {0x58, 0x47, 0xCA}, /* Marvell */
        {0x34, 0x02, 0x86}, /* Intel Wireless */
        {0xAC, 0x17, 0x02}, /* Intel Wireless */
        {0x00, 0x21, 0x6A}, /* Intel Wireless */
        {0x5C, 0xE0, 0xC5}, /* Qualcomm Atheros */
        {0x20, 0xCF, 0x30}, /* Qualcomm Atheros */
        {0x74, 0xD4, 0x35}, /* Qualcomm Atheros */
        {0x28, 0xD2, 0x44}, /* Qualcomm Atheros */
        {0x00, 0x26, 0xB9}, /* Qualcomm Atheros */
    };
    ULONG s = seed;
    ULONG idx = KernelRand(&s) % 24;
    out[0] = ouiNet[idx][0];
    out[1] = ouiNet[idx][1];
    out[2] = ouiNet[idx][2];
    out[3] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&s) & 0xFF);
    out[4] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&s) & 0xFF);
    out[5] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&s) & 0xFF);
}

VOID NetEnsureMAC(VOID) {
    if (g_NetMACReady) return;
    NetGenMAC(g_NetSpoofedMAC, g_SpoofSeed);
    g_NetMACReady = TRUE;
}

static BOOLEAN NetIsSpecialMAC(const UCHAR* m) {
    if (m[0]==0xFF&&m[1]==0xFF&&m[2]==0xFF&&m[3]==0xFF&&m[4]==0xFF&&m[5]==0xFF) return TRUE;
    if ((m[0]&1) && !(m[0]&2)) return TRUE; /* multicast (not LA) */
    if ((m[0]|m[1]|m[2]|m[3]|m[4]|m[5])==0) return TRUE;
    return FALSE;
}

/* ARP cache — returns consistent spoofed MAC for each original */
VOID NetSpoofCachedMAC(UCHAR* mac) {
    int i;
    if (NetIsSpecialMAC(mac)) return;
    for (i = 0; i < g_MacCacheCount; i++) {
        if (RtlCompareMemory(g_MacCache[i].Orig, mac, 6)==6) {
            RtlCopyMemory(mac, g_MacCache[i].Spoofed, 6); return;
        }
        if (RtlCompareMemory(g_MacCache[i].Spoofed, mac, 6)==6) return;
    }
    if (g_MacCacheCount >= NET_MAX_MAC_CACHE) {
        RtlCopyMemory(mac, g_NetSpoofedMAC, 6); return;
    }
    {
        NET_MAC_CACHE_ENTRY* e = &g_MacCache[g_MacCacheCount];
        ULONG seed = g_SpoofSeed;
        ULONG k;
        RtlCopyMemory(e->Orig, mac, 6);
        for (k=0;k<6;k++) seed ^= ((ULONG)mac[k]) << ((k%4)*8);
        for (k=0;k<6;k++) e->Spoofed[k] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&seed)&0xFF);
        e->Spoofed[0] &= 0xFE; e->Spoofed[0] |= 0x02;
        g_MacCacheCount++;
        RtlCopyMemory(mac, e->Spoofed, 6);
    }
}

/* Write to read-only kernel VA via MDL remapping */
static BOOLEAN NetWriteProtected(PVOID dest, const VOID* src, SIZE_T len) {
    PMDL mdl;
    PVOID mapped;
    mdl = IoAllocateMdl(dest, (ULONG)len, FALSE, FALSE, NULL);
    if (!mdl) return FALSE;
    MmBuildMdlForNonPagedPool(mdl);
    /* MmBuildMdlForNonPagedPool already sets MDL_MAPPED_TO_SYSTEM_VA; setting it
     * again before MmMapLockedPagesSpecifyCache returns the existing MappedSystemVa
     * instead of a new writable alias, so MmUnmapLockedPages then frees the
     * original structure's PTEs — corrupting it and causing a later 0x50. */
    mapped = MmMapLockedPagesSpecifyCache(mdl, KernelMode, MmNonCached, NULL, FALSE, NormalPagePriority);
    if (!mapped) { IoFreeMdl(mdl); return FALSE; }
    __try { RtlCopyMemory(mapped, src, len); } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { }
    MmUnmapLockedPages(mapped, mdl);
    IoFreeMdl(mdl);
    return TRUE;
}

/* GetModuleBase — returns base of a loaded kernel module by short name */
static PVOID NetGetModuleBase(const CHAR* shortName) {
    ULONG needed = 0;
    PRTL_PROCESS_MODULES mods;
    PVOID base = NULL;
    ULONG i;

    ZwQuerySystemInformation(SystemModuleInformation, NULL, 0, &needed);
    if (!needed) return NULL;
    needed += 0x200;
    mods = (PRTL_PROCESS_MODULES)SafeAllocatePool(needed, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!mods) return NULL;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(ZwQuerySystemInformation(SystemModuleInformation, mods, needed, &needed))) {
        ExFreePoolWithTag(mods, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); return NULL;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < mods->NumberOfModules; i++) {
        const CHAR* fn = (const CHAR*)mods->Modules[i].FullPathName
                       + mods->Modules[i].OffsetToFileName;
        BOOLEAN match = TRUE;
        const CHAR* p = shortName;
        while (*p) {
            CHAR a = fn[p - shortName], b = *p;
            if (a>='A'&&a<='Z') a+=32;
            if (b>='A'&&b<='Z') b+=32;
            if (a != b) { match = FALSE; break; }
            p++;
        }
        if (match) { base = mods->Modules[i].ImageBase; break; }
    }
    ExFreePoolWithTag(mods, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    return base;
}

/* FindPattern — simple byte pattern scan with mask */
static PVOID NetFindPattern(PVOID base, SIZE_T size, const UCHAR* pat, const CHAR* mask) {
    SIZE_T plen = 0, i, j;
    PUCHAR b = (PUCHAR)base;
    while (mask[plen]) plen++;
    if (!plen || size < plen) return NULL;
    for (i = 0; i <= size - plen; i++) {
        BOOLEAN ok = TRUE;
        for (j = 0; j < plen; j++) {
            if (mask[j] != '?' && b[i+j] != pat[j]) { ok = FALSE; break; }
        }
        if (ok) return b + i;
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* Get image size from the module list */
static ULONG NetGetModuleSize(PVOID base) {
    ULONG needed = 0, sz = 0, i;
    PRTL_PROCESS_MODULES mods;
    ZwQuerySystemInformation(SystemModuleInformation, NULL, 0, &needed);
    if (!needed) return 0;
    needed += 0x200;
    mods = (PRTL_PROCESS_MODULES)SafeAllocatePool(needed, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!mods) return 0;
    if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQuerySystemInformation(SystemModuleInformation, mods, needed, &needed)))
        for (i = 0; i < mods->NumberOfModules; i++)
            if (mods->Modules[i].ImageBase == base) { sz = mods->Modules[i].ImageSize; break; }
    ExFreePoolWithTag(mods, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    return sz;
}

/* Hook a driver's IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL dispatch atomically */
static BOOLEAN NetSwapControl(LPCWSTR driverName,
                               PDRIVER_DISPATCH newFn,
                               PDRIVER_DISPATCH* oldOut) {
    UNICODE_STRING name;
    PDRIVER_OBJECT drv = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    if (!g_ObReferenceObjectByName || !g_IoDriverObjectType) return FALSE;
    RtlInitUnicodeString(&name, driverName);
    st = g_ObReferenceObjectByName(&name, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE, NULL, 0,
                                    *g_IoDriverObjectType, KernelMode, NULL, (PVOID*)&drv);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st) || !drv) return FALSE;
    *oldOut = (PDRIVER_DISPATCH)InterlockedExchangePointer(
                  (volatile PVOID*)&drv->MajorFunction[IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL],
                  (PVOID)newFn);
    ObDereferenceObject(drv);
    return TRUE;
}

/* Save + replace IRP completion routine (for NSI-style chaining) */
VOID NetChainCompletion(PIO_STACK_LOCATION ioc, PIRP Irp,
                                PIO_COMPLETION_ROUTINE newFn,
                                NET_IOC_CTX** ctxOut) {
    NET_IOC_CTX* ctx = (NET_IOC_CTX*)SafeAllocatePool(sizeof(NET_IOC_CTX), POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!ctx) { IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp); return; }
    ctx->OldRoutine = ioc->CompletionRoutine;
    ctx->OldContext = ioc->Context;
    IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
    ioc = IoGetNextIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, newFn, ctx, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
    ioc->Control = SL_INVOKE_ON_SUCCESS | SL_INVOKE_ON_ERROR | SL_INVOKE_ON_CANCEL;
    *ctxOut = ctx;
}

/* --- Completion routines ------------------------------------ */

NTSTATUS NetBthCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PVOID buf = NULL; ULONG len, off;
    KIRQL irql;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj); UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
    /* UserBuffer is user-mode memory — absolutely unsafe at DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;

    len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
    if (Irp->UserBuffer && MmIsAddressValid(Irp->UserBuffer))
        buf = Irp->UserBuffer;
    else if (Irp->MdlAddress)
        buf = MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);

    if (!buf || len < 6) goto done;

    /* Blind scan — replace anything MAC-shaped that is not broadcast/multicast */
    __try {
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_NicLock, &irql);
        for (off = 0; off + 6 <= len; off++) {
            PUCHAR c = (PUCHAR)buf + off;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(c+5)) break;
            if (c[0]==0||c[0]==0xFF||(c[0]&1)) continue;
            RtlCopyMemory(c, g_NetSpoofedMAC, 6);
            off += 5;
        }
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_NicLock, irql);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}

done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* IFEntry.if_physaddr lives at offset 0x14 in the legacy TCP structure */
NTSTATUS NetTcpCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    NET_IOC_CTX ctx = {0};
    PUCHAR ifEntry; PULONG addrLen;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    if (Context) { ctx = *(NET_IOC_CTX*)Context; ExFreePoolWithTag(Context, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); }
    /* UserBuffer is user-mode memory — unsafe at DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;

    ifEntry = (PUCHAR)Irp->UserBuffer;
    if (!ifEntry || !MmIsAddressValid(ifEntry + 0x18)) goto done;
    __try {
        addrLen = (PULONG)(ifEntry + 0x10);
        if (*addrLen >= 6) RtlCopyMemory(ifEntry + 0x14, g_NetSpoofedMAC, 6);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}

done:
    if (ctx.OldRoutine) return ctx.OldRoutine(DevObj, Irp, ctx.OldContext);
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

NTSTATUS NetNicOidCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    NET_IOC_CTX ctx = {0};
    PVOID buf;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    if (Context) { ctx = *(NET_IOC_CTX*)Context; ExFreePoolWithTag(Context, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); }
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto done;
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto done;

    buf = NULL;
    if (Irp->MdlAddress) buf = MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);
    if (!buf && Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer) buf = Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (buf && MmIsAddressValid(buf))
        __try { RtlCopyMemory(buf, g_NetSpoofedMAC, 6); } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}

done:
    if (ctx.OldRoutine) return ctx.OldRoutine(DevObj, Irp, ctx.OldContext);
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* --- Dispatch hooks ----------------------------------------- */

NTSTATUS NetNsiProxyHook(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp) {
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION ioc = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    ULONG code = ioc->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode;
    ULONG cpu = KeGetCurrentProcessorNumber();
    LONG  depth;
    NTSTATUS st;

    /* Guard against nsiproxy re-entering its own dispatch via IoCallDriver
     * (e.g. when it forwards sub-requests to internal NSI providers).
     * Recursive calls still reach the real dispatch but skip our patching
     * so the frame overhead per level stays minimal. */
    if (cpu >= 64) return g_OrigNsiProxy(DevObj, Irp);
    depth = InterlockedIncrement(&g_NsiProxyDepth[cpu]);
    st = g_OrigNsiProxy(DevObj, Irp);

    /* All nsiproxy IOCTLs are patched inline on the synchronous return path.
     * Using NetChainCompletion (IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext) before
     * calling the original dispatch is unsafe: our hook is not a real device
     * in the stack, so pre-consuming a stack slot causes nsiproxy's own
     * IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation + IoCallDriver pair to push
     * CurrentStackLocation below Stack[0] into the IRP header on a StackSize=2
     * IRP, corrupting the IRP and producing an access violation in NsippDispatch
     * when it reads Type3InputBuffer from what is now Tail.Overlay garbage. */
    if (depth == 1 && st != STATUS_PENDING) {
        PUCHAR buf = (PUCHAR)Irp->UserBuffer;
        ULONG  len = (ULONG)Irp->IoStatus.Information;
        if (buf && MmIsAddressValid(buf) && len >= 6) {
            KIRQL irql; ULONG off;
            __try {
                KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_NicLock, &irql);
                if (code == IOCTL_NSI_PROXY_ARP && len >= 0x1C &&
                    *(PULONG)(buf + 0x18) == 11) {
                    /* Walk NSI neighbor table: find entry pointer + stride */
                    ULONG_PTR tbl = 0; ULONG entrySz = 0, count = 0;
                    for (off = 0x28; off < 0x80 && !tbl; off += 8) {
                        ULONG_PTR v = *(PULONG_PTR)(buf + off);
                        if (v > 0x10000 && v < 0x7FFFFFFFFFFF && MmIsAddressValid((PVOID)v)) {
                            ULONG sz = *(PULONG)(buf + off + 8);
                            if (sz >= 16 && sz <= 0x200) { tbl = v; entrySz = sz; }
                        }
                    }
                    for (off = 0x40; off < 0x90 && !count; off += 4) {
                        ULONG v = *(PULONG)(buf + off);
                        if (v > 0 && v <= 256) count = v;
                    }
                    if (tbl && entrySz && count) {
                        ULONG i;
                        for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                            PUCHAR mac = (PUCHAR)(tbl + (ULONG_PTR)i * entrySz);
                            if (MmIsAddressValid(mac) && MmIsAddressValid(mac + 5))
                                NetSpoofCachedMAC(mac);
                        }
                    }
                }
                /* No else: blind sliding-window scan over Irp->UserBuffer is unsafe.
                 * Svchost stack-allocates NSI output buffers; scanning past the
                 * actual response data writes the spoofed MAC into saved frame slots,
                 * producing an unhandled exception → CRITICAL_PROCESS_DIED (0xEF). */
                KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_NicLock, irql);
            } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
        }
    }

    if (cpu < 64) InterlockedDecrement(&g_NsiProxyDepth[cpu]);
    return st;
}

NTSTATUS NetBthHook(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp) {
    IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
    IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, NetBthCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
    return g_OrigBth(DevObj, Irp);
}

NTSTATUS NetTcpHook(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp) {
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION ioc = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    NET_IOC_CTX* ctx = NULL;
    if (ioc->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode == IOCTL_TCP_QUERY_INFORMATION_EX)
        NetChainCompletion(ioc, Irp, NetTcpCompletion, &ctx);
    return g_OrigTcp(DevObj, Irp);
}

NTSTATUS NetNicDispatchHook(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp) {
    KIRQL irql; int i;
    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_NicLock, &irql);
    for (i = 0; i < g_NicDriverCount; i++) {
        if (g_NicDrivers[i].DriverObj != DevObj->DriverObject) continue;
        {
            PIO_STACK_LOCATION ioc = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
            ULONG code = ioc->Parameters.DeviceIoControl.IoControlCode;
            if (code == IOCTL_NDIS_QUERY_GLOBAL_STATS && Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer) {
                ULONG oid = *(PULONG)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
                if (oid==OID_802_3_PERMANENT_ADDRESS || oid==OID_802_3_CURRENT_ADDRESS) {
                    NET_IOC_CTX* ctx = NULL;
                    NetChainCompletion(ioc, Irp, NetNicOidCompletion, &ctx);
                }
            }
        }
        {
            PDRIVER_DISPATCH orig = g_NicDrivers[i].OrigDispatch;
            KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_NicLock, irql);
            return orig(DevObj, Irp);
        }
    }
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_NicLock, irql);
    Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_SUCCESS; Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
    IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* --- NIC driver hooks (NDISWAN + IoDeviceInterfaces) -------- */

VOID NetHookNicDrivers(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING name;
    PDRIVER_OBJECT drv = NULL;
    PWCHAR ifaces = NULL; PWCHAR cur;
    const GUID netGuid = {0xcac88484,0x7515,0x4c03,{0x82,0xe6,0x71,0xa8,0x7a,0xba,0xc3,0x61}};
    KIRQL irql;

    /* ndiswan */
    RtlInitUnicodeString(&name, L"\\Driver\\ndiswan");
    if (g_ObReferenceObjectByName && g_IoDriverObjectType &&
        NT_SUCCESS(g_ObReferenceObjectByName(&name, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE, NULL, 0,
                       *g_IoDriverObjectType, KernelMode, NULL, (PVOID*)&drv)) && drv) {
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_NicLock, &irql);
        if (g_NicDriverCount < NET_MAX_NIC_DRIVERS) {
            g_NicDrivers[g_NicDriverCount].DriverObj     = drv;
            g_NicDrivers[g_NicDriverCount].OrigDispatch  =
                (PDRIVER_DISPATCH)InterlockedExchangePointer(
                    (volatile PVOID*)&drv->MajorFunction[IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL],
                    (PVOID)NetNicDispatchHook);
            g_NicDriverCount++;
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] NetHookNicDrivers: hooked \\Driver\\ndiswan\n");
        }
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_NicLock, irql);
        ObDereferenceObject(drv);
    } else {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] NetHookNicDrivers: \\Driver\\ndiswan not found (no WAN adapters?)\n");
    }

    /* Walk all NET device interfaces */
    {
        NTSTATUS ifSt = IoGetDeviceInterfaces(&netGuid, NULL, 0, &ifaces);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(ifSt)) {
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] NetHookNicDrivers: IoGetDeviceInterfaces failed 0x%08X"
                " — NIC dispatch hooks skipped\n", ifSt);
            return;
        }
    }
    for (cur = ifaces; *cur; cur += wcslen(cur) + 1) {
        PFILE_OBJECT fo = NULL; PDEVICE_OBJECT dev = NULL;
        UNICODE_STRING devName; BOOLEAN exists;
        int j;
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&devName, cur);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(IoGetDeviceObjectPointer(&devName, FILE_READ_DATA, &fo, &dev))) {
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] NetHookNicDrivers: can't get device object for interface (skip)\n");
            continue;
        }
        if (!dev || !dev->DriverObject) { if (fo) ObDereferenceObject(fo); continue; }
        exists = FALSE;
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_NicLock, &irql);
        for (j = 0; j < g_NicDriverCount; j++)
            if (g_NicDrivers[j].DriverObj == dev->DriverObject) { exists = TRUE; break; }
        if (!exists && g_NicDriverCount < NET_MAX_NIC_DRIVERS) {
            g_NicDrivers[g_NicDriverCount].DriverObj    = dev->DriverObject;
            g_NicDrivers[g_NicDriverCount].OrigDispatch =
                (PDRIVER_DISPATCH)InterlockedExchangePointer(
                    (volatile PVOID*)&dev->DriverObject->MajorFunction[IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL],
                    (PVOID)NetNicDispatchHook);
            g_NicDriverCount++;
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] NetHookNicDrivers: hooked NIC driver %p for interface\n",
                dev->DriverObject);
        }
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_NicLock, irql);
        ObDereferenceObject(fo);
    }
    ExFreePool(ifaces);
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] NetHookNicDrivers: total hooked drivers=%d\n", g_NicDriverCount);
}

/* --- IF_BLOCK in-place patch -------------------------------- */

/* Scan an IF_BLOCK (raw bytes) for the ifPhysAddress field.
 * Requires TWO consecutive IF_PHYSICAL_ADDRESS_LH structs (ifPhysAddress +
 * PermanentPhysAddress) both with Length==6 and plausible unicast MACs.
 * The double-struct requirement eliminates false positives in adjacent pool:
 * any kernel structure accidentally having a USHORT==6 at the right offset
 * would also need another USHORT==6 exactly IFPHYS_STRUCT_SIZE bytes later
 * with another unicast MAC — an astronomically unlikely coincidence.
 * Returns the offset of ifPhysAddress, or 0 if not found. */
static ULONG NetFindIfPhysOffset(PUCHAR block, ULONG blockLen) {
    ULONG off;
    for (off = 0x100; off + 2 * IFPHYS_STRUCT_SIZE <= blockLen && off < 0xA00; off += 2) {
        PUSHORT lenField  = (PUSHORT)(block + off);
        PUSHORT lenField2 = (PUSHORT)(block + off + IFPHYS_STRUCT_SIZE);
        PUCHAR  mac, mac2;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(lenField))  break;
        if (*lenField != 6) continue;
        /* Validate PermanentPhysAddress immediately follows */
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(lenField2)) break;
        if (*lenField2 != 6) continue;
        mac  = block + off + sizeof(USHORT);
        mac2 = block + off + IFPHYS_STRUCT_SIZE + sizeof(USHORT);
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(mac + 5) || !MmIsAddressValid(mac2 + 5)) break;
        if (mac[0] & 1)  continue;                   /* multicast/broadcast */
        if (mac2[0] & 1) continue;
        if (!mac[0] && !mac[1] && !mac[2] && !mac[3] && !mac[4] && !mac[5])   continue;
        if (!mac2[0] && !mac2[1] && !mac2[2] && !mac2[3] && !mac2[4] && !mac2[5]) continue;
        return off;
    }
    return 0;
}

/* Walk ndis.sys's global NDIS interface-block list and overwrite
 * ifPhysAddress + PermanentPhysAddress in each entry.
 * The field offsets are discovered dynamically per-entry so this works
 * across all Windows 10/11 builds regardless of IF_BLOCK layout changes.
 *
 * Strategy: resolve NdisIfGetInterfaceIndexFromNetLuid from the ndis.sys
 * PE export table, then disassemble its first 256 bytes looking for a
 * RIP-relative MOV/LEA whose target validates as a LIST_ENTRY head
 * (round-trip: head->Flink->Blink == head).  Walk the list from there.
 *
 * LIST_ENTRY is at offset 0 in the IF_BLOCK, so each PLIST_ENTRY cast
 * to PUCHAR is treated as the start of the block. */
VOID NetPatchIfBlocks(VOID) {
    PVOID       ndisBase;
    PUCHAR      fn = NULL;
    PLIST_ENTRY head = NULL;
    ULONG       i, patched = 0;

    ndisBase = NetGetModuleBase("ndis.sys");
    if (!ndisBase) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: ndis.sys not found in module list"
            " — skipping IF_BLOCK patch (unusual, check module load)\n");
        return;
    }
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: ndis.sys base=%p\n", ndisBase);

    /* Walk PE export table to find NdisIfGetInterfaceIndexFromNetLuid. */
    {
        PIMAGE_DOS_HEADER   dos = (PIMAGE_DOS_HEADER)ndisBase;
        PIMAGE_NT_HEADERS   nt;
        PIMAGE_EXPORT_DIRECTORY expDir;
        PULONG  nameRVAs, funcRVAs;
        PUSHORT ordRVAs;
        ULONG   j, expRVA;

        if (!MmIsAddressValid(dos) || dos->e_magic != IMAGE_DOS_SIGNATURE) return;
        nt = (PIMAGE_NT_HEADERS)((PUCHAR)ndisBase + dos->e_lfanew);
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(nt) || nt->Signature != IMAGE_NT_SIGNATURE) return;

        expRVA = nt->OptionalHeader.DataDirectory[IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_EXPORT].VirtualAddress;
        if (!expRVA) return;
        expDir   = (PIMAGE_EXPORT_DIRECTORY)((PUCHAR)ndisBase + expRVA);
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(expDir)) return;
        nameRVAs = (PULONG) ((PUCHAR)ndisBase + expDir->AddressOfNames);
        funcRVAs = (PULONG) ((PUCHAR)ndisBase + expDir->AddressOfFunctions);
        ordRVAs  = (PUSHORT)((PUCHAR)ndisBase + expDir->AddressOfNameOrdinals);

        for (j = 0; j < expDir->NumberOfNames && !fn; j++) {
            const CHAR* sym    = (const CHAR*)((PUCHAR)ndisBase + nameRVAs[j]);
            const CHAR* target = "NdisIfGetInterfaceIndexFromNetLuid";
            ULONG k = 0;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid((PVOID)sym)) continue;
            while (target[k] && sym[k] == (UCHAR)target[k]) k++;
            if (!target[k] && !sym[k])
                fn = (PUCHAR)((PUCHAR)ndisBase + funcRVAs[ordRVAs[j]]);
        }
    }
    if (!fn) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: export not found\n");
        return;
    }

    /* Scan the first 256 bytes of the function for RIP-relative MOV/LEA.
     * x64 pattern: REX.W(48) opcode(8B or 8D) ModRM(mod=00,rm=5) disp32
     *   (mod_rm & 0xC7) == 0x05 matches any destination register.
     * Validate each candidate as a list head via round-trip consistency:
     *   head->Flink->Blink == head.
     * A lock (KSPIN_LOCK = single pointer) will fail because its first
     * 8 bytes are 0 or a thread pointer below 0xFFFF000000000000. */
    for (i = 0; i < 250 && !head; i++) {
        PUCHAR     p = fn + i;
        LONG       disp;
        PUCHAR     ref;
        PLIST_ENTRY cand;

        if (!MmIsAddressValid(p + 6)) break;
        if (p[0] != 0x48) continue;
        if (p[1] != 0x8B && p[1] != 0x8D) continue;
        if ((p[2] & 0xC7) != 0x05) continue;             /* [RIP+disp32] */

        disp = *(LONG*)(p + 3);
        ref  = p + 7 + disp;                             /* RIP = p+7 */
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(ref) || !MmIsAddressValid(ref + 15)) continue;

        cand = (PLIST_ENTRY)ref;
        if ((ULONG_PTR)cand->Flink < 0xFFFF000000000000ULL) continue;
        if ((ULONG_PTR)cand->Blink < 0xFFFF000000000000ULL) continue;
        if (!MmIsAddressValid(cand->Flink)) continue;
        /* Round-trip: first entry's Blink must equal this head. */
        if (!MmIsAddressValid((PVOID)cand->Flink->Blink)) continue;
        if (cand->Flink->Blink != cand) continue;
        head = cand;
    }
    if (!head) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: IF_BLOCK list head not found in first 250 bytes"
            " of NdisIfGetInterfaceIndexFromNetLuid — Windows build may use different layout\n");
        return;
    }
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: list head=%p fn=%p\n", head, fn);

    /* Walk and patch.  Use dynamic scan per entry so we don't depend on
     * a hardcoded field offset that changes between Windows builds. */
    {
        PLIST_ENTRY entry = head->Flink;
        ULONG skipped = 0;
        while (entry && entry != head && patched < 32) {
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(entry)) {
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: invalid entry ptr %p — stopping walk\n", entry);
                break;
            }
            __try {
                PUCHAR block = (PUCHAR)entry;
                ULONG  off   = NetFindIfPhysOffset(block, 0x800);
                if (off) {
                    PUCHAR ifPhys   = block + off + sizeof(USHORT);
                    PUCHAR permPhys = block + off + IFPHYS_STRUCT_SIZE + sizeof(USHORT);
                    if (MmIsAddressValid(ifPhys + 5)) {
                        SPOOF_LOG(
                            "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: patching entry %p off=0x%lX"
                            " orig=%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X\n",
                            entry, off,
                            ifPhys[0], ifPhys[1], ifPhys[2],
                            ifPhys[3], ifPhys[4], ifPhys[5]);
                        RtlCopyMemory(ifPhys, g_NetSpoofedMAC, 6);
                    }
                    if (MmIsAddressValid(permPhys + 5))
                        RtlCopyMemory(permPhys, g_NetSpoofedMAC, 6);
                    patched++;
                } else {
                    skipped++;
                }
            } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: exception scanning entry %p\n", entry);
            }
            entry = entry->Flink;
        }
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] NetPatchIfBlocks: patched=%lu skipped=%lu"
            " spoofed_mac=%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X\n",
            patched, skipped,
            g_NetSpoofedMAC[0], g_NetSpoofedMAC[1], g_NetSpoofedMAC[2],
            g_NetSpoofedMAC[3], g_NetSpoofedMAC[4], g_NetSpoofedMAC[5]);
    }
}

/* --- Top-level entry point ---------------------------------- */

NTSTATUS SpoofNetworkAll(VOID) {
    NetEnsureMAC();
    SPOOF_LOG(
        "[Spoofer] SpoofNetworkAll: target MAC=%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X\n",
        g_NetSpoofedMAC[0], g_NetSpoofedMAC[1], g_NetSpoofedMAC[2],
        g_NetSpoofedMAC[3], g_NetSpoofedMAC[4], g_NetSpoofedMAC[5]);

    /* 1 — Registry: NIC NetworkAddress (reuses existing IOCTL_SPOOF_NIC_MAC loop) */
    {
        UNICODE_STRING nicClass;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa; HANDLE hClass = NULL;
        ULONG idx = 0, nicCount = 0;
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&nicClass,
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
            L"\\{4d36e972-e325-11ce-bfc1-08002be10318}");
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &nicClass,
                                   OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_READ, &oa))) {
            for (;;) {
                ULONG len = 0; PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION info;
                NTSTATUS s = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &len);
                if (s == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
                if (!len) { idx++; continue; }
                info = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(len, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                if (!info) { idx++; continue; }
                s = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx++, KeyBasicInformation, info, len, &len);
                if (NT_SUCCESS(s)) {
                    UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)info->NameLength, (USHORT)info->NameLength, info->Name };
                    HANDLE hInst = NULL;
                    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &sub,
                        OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hClass, NULL);
                    if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_SET_VALUE | KEY_QUERY_VALUE, &oa))) {
                        /* Skip WAN miniports and virtual adapters — only patch physical NICs.
                         * Writing NetworkAddress to ms_* (ppp, l2tp, wan miniport, etc.) creates
                         * a detectable spoofer artifact: 14+ adapters all sharing one MAC. */
                        BOOLEAN isVirtual = FALSE;
                        {
                            WCHAR compId[128] = {0};
                            UNICODE_STRING compIdName;
                            PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION pkvpi;
                            ULONG pkvpiSz = sizeof(KEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION) + sizeof(compId);
                            pkvpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(pkvpiSz, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                            if (pkvpi) {
                                ULONG rl = 0;
                                RtlInitUnicodeString(&compIdName, L"ComponentId");
                                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hInst, &compIdName,
                                        KeyValuePartialInformation, pkvpi, pkvpiSz, &rl))
                                        && pkvpi->Type == REG_SZ
                                        && pkvpi->DataLength >= 3 * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                    PWCHAR cw = (PWCHAR)pkvpi->Data;
                                    isVirtual = ((cw[0] == L'm' || cw[0] == L'M') &&
                                                 (cw[1] == L's' || cw[1] == L'S') &&
                                                 cw[2] == L'_');
                                }
                                ExFreePoolWithTag(pkvpi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                            }
                        }
                        if (!isVirtual) {
                            WCHAR macStr[13];
                            UNICODE_STRING vname;
                            NTSTATUS rvSt;
                            RtlStringCbPrintfW(macStr, sizeof(macStr), L"%02X%02X%02X%02X%02X%02X",
                                g_NetSpoofedMAC[0], g_NetSpoofedMAC[1], g_NetSpoofedMAC[2],
                                g_NetSpoofedMAC[3], g_NetSpoofedMAC[4], g_NetSpoofedMAC[5]);
                            RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"NetworkAddress");
                            rvSt = ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, macStr, 13 * sizeof(WCHAR));
                            if (NT_SUCCESS(rvSt))
                                nicCount++;
                            else
                                SPOOF_LOG(
                                    "[Spoofer] SpoofNetworkAll: NIC reg write failed 0x%08X\n", rvSt);
                        }
                        ZwClose(hInst);
                    }
                }
                ExFreePoolWithTag(info, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            }
            ZwClose(hClass);
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] SpoofNetworkAll: registry NetworkAddress written for %lu NIC(s)\n", nicCount);
        } else {
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] SpoofNetworkAll: could not open NIC class key"
                " — registry MAC patch skipped\n");
        }
    }

    /* 2 — Hook NDISWAN + NIC device interfaces */
    NetHookNicDrivers();

    /* 3 — NDIS IF_BLOCK in-place patch (direct kernel memory reads by EAC kernel module) */
    NetPatchIfBlocks();

    /* 4 — nsiproxy hook removed: nsiproxy forwards sub-requests to NSI providers
     * via IoCallDriver back to its own device, re-entering NetNsiProxyHook on every
     * level.  The depth counter reduces patching overhead but not stack depth, so
     * heavy nsiproxy traffic (netsh, svchost network enum) overflows the kernel stack
     * → #PF on guard page → #PF again → EXCEPTION_DOUBLE_FAULT (0x7F/8).
     * ARP table spoofing is covered by NetHookNicDrivers (OID) + NetPatchIfBlocks. */

    /* 5 — Hook Bluetooth */
    {
        static const WCHAR* bthDrvs[] = {
            L"\\Driver\\BTHUSB", L"\\Driver\\BTHENUM",
            L"\\Driver\\BTHPORT", L"\\Driver\\BTHHFENUM" };
        ULONG k;
        for (k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
            if (!g_OrigBth)
                NetSwapControl(bthDrvs[k], NetBthHook, &g_OrigBth);
        }
    }

    /* 6 — TCP hook removed: IOCTL_TCP_QUERY_INFORMATION_EX is legacy TDI not
     * used by EAC on Win10+; hooking IRP_MJ_DEVICE_CONTROL on \\Driver\\Tcp
     * interferes with WFP ALE endpoint cleanup → null deref in
     * WfpAlepFreeRemoteEndpointsForEndpointContext (BSOD 0xD1). */

    /* 7 — Sync spoofed MAC to CMOS bank-0 offsets 0x71-0x76 so EAC's CMOS vs NDIS
     * cross-check passes.  EAC reads these bytes each session to verify the MAC
     * hasn't changed since the last spoof run; divergence is flagged as a ban signal. */
    {
        ULONG i;
        for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            __outbyte(0x70, (UCHAR)(0x71 + i));   /* select CMOS offset (NMI enabled, bit7=0) */
            __outbyte(0x71, g_NetSpoofedMAC[i]);   /* write MAC byte */
        }
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] SpoofNetworkAll: CMOS[0x71:0x76] <- %02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X\n",
            g_NetSpoofedMAC[0], g_NetSpoofedMAC[1], g_NetSpoofedMAC[2],
            g_NetSpoofedMAC[3], g_NetSpoofedMAC[4], g_NetSpoofedMAC[5]);
    }

    g_NetworkSpoofed = TRUE;
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Network spoofing armed\n");
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}


VOID SpoofDhcpLeaseData(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR ifacePath =
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services"
        L"\\Tcpip\\Parameters\\Interfaces";
    UNICODE_STRING ifPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hIf = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG idx = 0;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&ifPath, ifacePath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &ifPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hIf, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return;

    for (;;) {
        ULONG needed = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION kbi;

        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hIf, idx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!needed || needed > 0x400) { idx++; continue; }

        kbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(needed + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!kbi) { idx++; continue; }

        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hIf, idx, KeyBasicInformation, kbi, needed, &needed)) &&
            kbi->NameLength > 0) {
            UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)kbi->NameLength, (USHORT)kbi->NameLength, kbi->Name };
            HANDLE hSub = NULL;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaSub;
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaSub, &sub, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE,
                                       hIf, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hSub, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaSub))) {
                UNICODE_STRING vn;
                WCHAR emptyW[] = L"0.0.0.0";
                WCHAR emptyStr[] = L"";
                /* Clear all DHCP lease fields that expose real network topology */
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"DhcpIPAddress");
                ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_SZ, emptyW, sizeof(emptyW));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"DhcpServer");
                ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_SZ, emptyW, sizeof(emptyW));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"DhcpDefaultGateway");
                ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_MULTI_SZ, emptyStr, sizeof(emptyStr));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"DhcpSubnetMask");
                ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_SZ, emptyW, sizeof(emptyW));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"DhcpSubnetMaskOpt");
                ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_MULTI_SZ, emptyStr, sizeof(emptyStr));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"DhcpNameServer");
                ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_SZ, emptyStr, sizeof(WCHAR));
                /* DHCP lease timestamps */
                {
                    ULONG zeroVal = 0;
                    RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"LeaseObtainedTime");
                    ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_DWORD, &zeroVal, sizeof(zeroVal));
                    RtlInitUnicodeString(&vn, L"LeaseTerminatesTime");
                    ZwSetValueKey(hSub, &vn, 0, REG_DWORD, &zeroVal, sizeof(zeroVal));
                }
                ZwClose(hSub);
            }
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(kbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        idx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hIf);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] DHCP lease data cleared\n");
}

/* ============================================================
 * NETWORK PROFILE HISTORY CLEANUP
 * NetworkList\Signatures\Unmanaged stores DefaultGatewayMac — the real
 * MAC address of the user's router.  If left in place it directly
 * contradicts the spoofed NIC MAC and allows cross-session correlation.
 * We also clear Profiles and Nla\Cache which carry persistent GUIDs and
 * SSID-correlated connection history.
 * ============================================================ */

VOID DeleteImmediateSubkeys(HANDLE hParent) {
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG needed;
    UCHAR kbiBuf[512];
    PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION kbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)kbiBuf;
    UNICODE_STRING sub;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaSub;
    HANDLE hSub;

    /* Always enumerate index 0 — each deletion shifts the remaining entries */
    for (;;) {
        needed = sizeof(kbiBuf);
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hParent, 0, KeyBasicInformation, kbi, needed, &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES || !NT_SUCCESS(st)) break;

        sub.Length        = (USHORT)kbi->NameLength;
        sub.MaximumLength = (USHORT)kbi->NameLength;
        sub.Buffer        = kbi->Name;
        hSub = NULL;
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaSub, &sub,
            OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hParent, NULL);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hSub, DELETE, &oaSub))) {
            ZwDeleteKey(hSub);
            ZwClose(hSub);
        } else {
            break; /* Can't delete — stop to avoid infinite loop */
        }
    }
}

VOID ClearNetworkProfiles(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR paths[] = {
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\NetworkList\\Signatures\\Unmanaged",
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\NetworkList\\Signatures\\Managed",
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\NetworkList\\Profiles",
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\NetworkList\\Nla\\Cache",
    };
    UNICODE_STRING path;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    ULONG i;

    for (i = 0; i < sizeof(paths) / sizeof(paths[0]); i++) {
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&path, paths[i]);
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &path,
            OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        hKey = NULL;
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hKey,
            KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS | DELETE, &oa))) {
            DeleteImmediateSubkeys(hKey);
            ZwClose(hKey);
        }
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Network profile history cleared\n");
}


VOID PatchNicDriverDesc(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR nicClassPath =
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
        L"\\{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}";
    static const LPCWSTR nicDescriptions[] = {
        L"Realtek PCIe GbE Family Controller",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection (7) I219-V",
        L"Killer E2600 Gigabit Ethernet Controller",
        L"Realtek PCIe 2.5GbE Family Controller",
        L"Intel(R) I225-V 2.5GbE Network Connection",
        L"Killer E3100G 2.5Gb Ethernet Controller",
        L"Realtek Gaming 2.5GbE Family Controller",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection I217-V",
        L"Killer E2500 Gigabit Ethernet Controller",
        L"Realtek PCIe FE Family Controller",
    };
    static const LPCWSTR wifiDescriptions[] = {
        L"Intel(R) Wi-Fi 6 AX200 160MHz",
        L"Intel(R) Wireless-AC 9560",
        L"Killer(R) Wi-Fi 6 AX1650x 160MHz Wireless Network Adapter",
        L"Realtek RTL8822CE 802.11ac PCIe Adapter",
        L"Intel(R) Wi-Fi 6E AX211 160MHz",
        L"MediaTek Wi-Fi 6 MT7921 Wireless LAN Card",
    };
    UNICODE_STRING nicPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hClass = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG idx = 0;
    KIRQL irql;
    ULONG rng;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&nicPath, nicClassPath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &nicPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_READ, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    rng = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);

    for (;;) {
        ULONG len = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION ki;
        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &len);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!len || len > 0x400) { idx++; continue; }

        ki = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(len + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!ki) { idx++; continue; }

        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, ki, len, &len))) {
            UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)ki->NameLength, (USHORT)ki->NameLength, ki->Name };
            HANDLE hInst = NULL;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaI;
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaI, &sub, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE,
                                       hClass, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaI))) {
                UCHAR qbuf[256];
                ULONG qlen = sizeof(qbuf);
                UNICODE_STRING compId;
                PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION vpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)qbuf;
                BOOLEAN isWifi = FALSE;
                LPCWSTR chosen;

                /* Check ComponentId to classify wired vs wireless */
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&compId, L"ComponentId");
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hInst, &compId, KeyValuePartialInformation,
                                               vpi, qlen, &qlen))) {
                    if (vpi->Type == REG_SZ) {
                        PWCHAR cv = (PWCHAR)vpi->Data;
                        ULONG  clen = vpi->DataLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
                        /* Wireless adapters carry "ms_mswifi" or "wifi" in ComponentId */
                        ULONG ci;
                        for (ci = 0; ci + 4 < clen; ci++) {
                            if ((cv[ci]=='w'||cv[ci]=='W') &&
                                (cv[ci+1]=='i'||cv[ci+1]=='I') &&
                                (cv[ci+2]=='f'||cv[ci+2]=='F') &&
                                (cv[ci+3]=='i'||cv[ci+3]=='I')) {
                                isWifi = TRUE; break;
                            }
                        }
                        /* Virtual/tunnel adapters contain "ms_" prefix — skip them */
                        if (clen >= 3 && cv[0] == L'm' && cv[1] == L's' && cv[2] == L'_' &&
                            !isWifi) {
                            ZwClose(hInst);
                            ExFreePoolWithTag(ki, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                            idx++;
                            continue;
                        }
                    }
                }

                rng = rng * 1664525UL + 1013904223UL;
                if (isWifi)
                    chosen = wifiDescriptions[rng % ARRAYSIZE(wifiDescriptions)];
                else
                    chosen = nicDescriptions[rng % ARRAYSIZE(nicDescriptions)];

                {
                    UNICODE_STRING ddName;
                    RtlInitUnicodeString(&ddName, L"DriverDesc");
                    ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &ddName, 0, REG_SZ, (PVOID)chosen,
                                  (ULONG)((wcslen(chosen) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
                }
                ZwClose(hInst);
            }
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(ki, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        idx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hClass);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NIC DriverDesc patched\n");
}

/* ============================================================
 * CLEAN ALL ARTIFACTS — orchestrates all new spoof operations
 * Called by IOCTL_CLEAN_ARTIFACTS; also from IOCTL_SPOOF_ALL.
 * ============================================================ */


VOID RestoreNICRegistry(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING nicClass;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hClass = NULL;
    ULONG idx = 0;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&nicClass,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
        L"\\{4d36e972-e325-11ce-bfc1-08002be10318}");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &nicClass,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_READ, &oa)))
        return;

    for (;;) {
        ULONG len = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION info;
        NTSTATUS s = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &len);
        if (s == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!len) { idx++; continue; }
        info = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(len, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!info) { idx++; continue; }
        s = ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx++, KeyBasicInformation, info, len, &len);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(s)) {
            UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)info->NameLength, (USHORT)info->NameLength, info->Name };
            HANDLE hInst = NULL;
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &sub,
                OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hClass, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa))) {
                UNICODE_STRING vname;
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"NetworkAddress");
                ZwDeleteValueKey(hInst, &vname);
                ZwClose(hInst);
            }
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(info, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    }
    ZwClose(hClass);
    g_NICSpoofCount = 0;
    g_NetworkSpoofed = FALSE;
}



```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_registry.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

NTSTATUS RegSetStringA(LPCWSTR keyPath, LPCWSTR valueName, const char* newValue) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath, vname;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    NTSTATUS st;
    WCHAR wbuf[128];
    ULONG wlen;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, keyPath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return st;

    /* Convert ASCII to wide */
    wlen = 0;
    while (newValue[wlen] && wlen < 127) { wbuf[wlen] = (WCHAR)(UCHAR)newValue[wlen]; wlen++; }
    wbuf[wlen] = L'\0';

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, valueName);
    st = ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, wbuf, (wlen + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR));
    ZwClose(hKey);
    return st;
}

NTSTATUS RegSetStringW(LPCWSTR keyPath, LPCWSTR valueName, LPCWSTR newValue) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath, vname;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    NTSTATUS st;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, keyPath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) return st;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, valueName);
    st = ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, (PVOID)newValue,
                       (ULONG)((wcslen(newValue) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
    ZwClose(hKey);
    return st;
}

NTSTATUS SpoofExtendedRegistry(PSPOOF_EXTENDED_REG_REQUEST req) {
    NTSTATUS st, last = STATUS_SUCCESS;

#define TRY_REG_A(path, val, str)  do { \
    if ((str)[0]) { st = RegSetStringA((path), (val), (str)); \
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Reg %ls\\%ls: 0x%08X\n", (path), (val), st); last = st; } } \
    } while(0)
#define TRY_REG_W(path, val, wstr) do { \
    if ((wstr)[0]) { st = RegSetStringW((path), (val), (wstr)); \
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Reg %ls\\%ls: 0x%08X\n", (path), (val), st); last = st; } } \
    } while(0)

    /* HARDWARE hive is volatile; write BIOS fields to the writable SystemInformation
     * key instead — EAC reads from both paths. */
    TRY_REG_A(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\SystemInformation",
              L"BIOSVendor", req->NewBIOSVendor);
    TRY_REG_A(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\SystemInformation",
              L"BIOSVersion", req->NewBIOSVersion);
    TRY_REG_A(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\SystemInformation",
              L"BIOSReleaseDate", req->NewBIOSReleaseDate);
    TRY_REG_A(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\SystemInformation",
              L"SystemManufacturer", req->NewSystemManufacturer);
    TRY_REG_A(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\SystemInformation",
              L"SystemProductName", req->NewSystemProductName);
    TRY_REG_W(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\SystemInformation",
              L"ComputerHardwareId", req->NewComputerHardwareId);

    TRY_REG_W(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\WindowsUpdate",
              L"SusClientId", req->NewSusClientId);

    TRY_REG_W(L"\\Registry\\Machine\\Software\\Microsoft\\SQMClient",
              L"MachineId", req->NewSqmMachineId);

#undef TRY_REG_A
#undef TRY_REG_W

    if (NT_SUCCESS(last)) g_RegistrySpoofed = TRUE;
    return last;
}


NTSTATUS SpoofUsbDeviceSerials(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING usbPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hUsb = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG vidIdx = 0;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&usbPath,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\USB");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &usbPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hUsb, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofUsbDeviceSerials: can't open USB Enum: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return st;
    }

    for (;;) {
        ULONG needed = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION kbi;
        HANDLE hVid = NULL;
        WCHAR vidName[64];
        UNICODE_STRING vidUs;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaVid;
        ULONG serIdx = 0;

        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hUsb, vidIdx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!needed || needed > 0x400) { vidIdx++; continue; }

        kbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(needed + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!kbi) { vidIdx++; continue; }
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hUsb, vidIdx, KeyBasicInformation, kbi, needed, &needed))) {
            ExFreePoolWithTag(kbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); vidIdx++; continue;
        }

        /* Only process VID_XXXX&PID_XXXX composite device keys */
        if (kbi->NameLength < 8 * sizeof(WCHAR) ||
            (kbi->Name[0] != L'V' && kbi->Name[0] != L'v')) {
            ExFreePoolWithTag(kbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF); vidIdx++; continue;
        }
        {
            USHORT cn = (USHORT)min(kbi->NameLength, (ULONG)(sizeof(vidName) - sizeof(WCHAR)));
            RtlCopyMemory(vidName, kbi->Name, cn);
            vidName[cn / sizeof(WCHAR)] = L'\0';
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(kbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&vidUs, vidName);
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaVid, &vidUs, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hUsb, NULL);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hVid, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oaVid))) { vidIdx++; continue; }

        /* Enumerate instance (serial) subkeys */
        for (;;) {
            ULONG serNeeded = 0;
            PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION serKbi;

            st = ZwEnumerateKey(hVid, serIdx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &serNeeded);
            if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
            if (!serNeeded || serNeeded > 0x400) { serIdx++; continue; }

            serKbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(serNeeded + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (!serKbi) { serIdx++; continue; }
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hVid, serIdx, KeyBasicInformation,
                                          serKbi, serNeeded, &serNeeded))) {
                ULONG nameChars = serKbi->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
                /* Synthetic serials: "5&..." "0&..." "6&..." "7&..." (hub-port topology hash).
                 * These are not hardware-unique — skip them; only spoof real device serials. */
                BOOLEAN isSynth = (nameChars >= 2 && serKbi->Name[1] == L'&' &&
                    (serKbi->Name[0] == L'5' || serKbi->Name[0] == L'0' ||
                     serKbi->Name[0] == L'6' || serKbi->Name[0] == L'7'));

                if (!isSynth && nameChars >= 4 && nameChars <= MAX_USB_SER_CHARS &&
                    g_UsbSerialCount < MAX_USB_SPOOFS) {
                    /* Dedup */
                    ULONG k; BOOLEAN dup = FALSE;
                    for (k = 0; k < g_UsbSerialCount; k++) {
                        if (g_UsbSerials[k].Len == (USHORT)nameChars &&
                            RtlCompareMemory(g_UsbSerials[k].Old, serKbi->Name,
                                             nameChars * sizeof(WCHAR)) == nameChars * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                            dup = TRUE; break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (!dup) {
                        USB_SER_ENTRY* e = &g_UsbSerials[g_UsbSerialCount];
                        KIRQL irql;
                        ULONG ci;
                        RtlCopyMemory(e->Old, serKbi->Name, nameChars * sizeof(WCHAR));
                        e->Old[nameChars] = L'\0';
                        e->Len = (USHORT)nameChars;
                        /* Replacement: same length, randomise hex/alphanumeric chars in-place,
                         * preserve punctuation (-, &) so the format stays recognisable.      */
                        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
                        for (ci = 0; ci < nameChars; ci++) {
                            WCHAR oc = serKbi->Name[ci];
                            if ((oc >= L'0' && oc <= L'9') ||
                                (oc >= L'A' && oc <= L'F') ||
                                (oc >= L'a' && oc <= L'f')) {
                                ULONG n = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xF;
                                e->New[ci] = (WCHAR)(n < 10 ? (L'0' + n) : (L'A' + n - 10));
                            } else if (oc >= L'G' && oc <= L'Z') {
                                /* Uppercase non-hex letter — keep uppercase, randomise */
                                e->New[ci] = (WCHAR)(L'A' + (KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) % 26));
                            } else if (oc >= L'g' && oc <= L'z') {
                                e->New[ci] = (WCHAR)(L'a' + (KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) % 26));
                            } else {
                                e->New[ci] = oc; /* preserve separators */
                            }
                        }
                        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
                        e->New[nameChars] = L'\0';
                        g_UsbSerialCount++;
                        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] USB serial: %S -> %S (vid=%S)\n",
                                 e->Old, e->New, vidName);
                    }
                }
            }
            ExFreePoolWithTag(serKbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            serIdx++;
        }
        ZwClose(hVid);
        vidIdx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hUsb);

    if (g_UsbSerialCount > 0) {
        g_UsbSerialSpoofActive = TRUE;
        RegisterPciEnumCallback(); /* reuse CM callback for USB enum aliasing */
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] USB serial spoof: %u device(s) mapped\n", g_UsbSerialCount);
    }
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * NVME ENUM SERIAL ALIASING
 * Windows stores NVMe serial numbers as the literal instance key
 * name under \Enum\NVMe\{ModelName}\{SerialNumber}.  The IRP-level
 * NvmeIoCompletion patches runtime IOCTL responses but leaves the
 * static registry path intact.  This function builds an alias table
 * (same structure as USB serials) so the CM callback can substitute
 * spoofed serials on ZwEnumerateKey and reverse-alias on ZwOpenKey.
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS SpoofNvmeEnumKeys(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR nvmePath =
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\NVMe";
    UNICODE_STRING nvmeStr;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaNvme;
    HANDLE hNvme = NULL;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG midx;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&nvmeStr, nvmePath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaNvme, &nvmeStr,
        OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hNvme, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oaNvme);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        /* No \Enum\NVMe — system has no NVMe drives or they use a different path */
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }

    for (midx = 0; ; midx++) {
        ULONG      needed = 0;
        PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION mkbi;
        HANDLE     hModel = NULL;
        UNICODE_STRING modelSub;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaModel;
        ULONG      sidx;

        st = ZwEnumerateKey(hNvme, midx, KeyBasicInformation, NULL, 0, &needed);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!needed || needed > 0x800) continue;

        mkbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(needed + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (!mkbi) continue;

        if (!NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hNvme, midx, KeyBasicInformation,
                                       mkbi, needed, &needed)) ||
            mkbi->NameLength == 0) {
            ExFreePoolWithTag(mkbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            continue;
        }

        modelSub.Length        = (USHORT)mkbi->NameLength;
        modelSub.MaximumLength = (USHORT)mkbi->NameLength;
        modelSub.Buffer        = mkbi->Name;
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaModel, &modelSub,
            OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hNvme, NULL);

        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hModel, KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oaModel))) {
            for (sidx = 0; ; sidx++) {
                ULONG      sneeded = 0;
                PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION skbi;

                st = ZwEnumerateKey(hModel, sidx, KeyBasicInformation,
                                    NULL, 0, &sneeded);
                if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
                if (!sneeded || sneeded > 0x200) continue;

                skbi = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(sneeded + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                if (!skbi) continue;

                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hModel, sidx, KeyBasicInformation,
                                              skbi, sneeded, &sneeded)) &&
                    skbi->NameLength > 0) {
                    ULONG nameChars = skbi->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
                    if (nameChars >= 4 && nameChars <= MAX_USB_SER_CHARS &&
                        g_NvmeSerialCount < MAX_NVME_SPOOFS) {
                        ULONG k; BOOLEAN dup = FALSE;
                        for (k = 0; k < g_NvmeSerialCount; k++) {
                            if (g_NvmeSerials[k].Len == (USHORT)nameChars &&
                                RtlCompareMemory(g_NvmeSerials[k].Old, skbi->Name,
                                                 nameChars * sizeof(WCHAR)) ==
                                nameChars * sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                                dup = TRUE; break;
                            }
                        }
                        if (!dup) {
                            USB_SER_ENTRY* e = &g_NvmeSerials[g_NvmeSerialCount];
                            KIRQL irql; ULONG ci;
                            RtlCopyMemory(e->Old, skbi->Name, nameChars * sizeof(WCHAR));
                            e->Old[nameChars] = L'\0';
                            KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
                            for (ci = 0; ci < nameChars; ci++) {
                                WCHAR c = skbi->Name[ci];
                                if ((c >= L'0' && c <= L'9') ||
                                    (c >= L'A' && c <= L'Z') ||
                                    (c >= L'a' && c <= L'z')) {
                                    static const CHAR hex[] = "0123456789ABCDEF";
                                    e->New[ci] = (WCHAR)hex[KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) % 16];
                                } else {
                                    e->New[ci] = c; /* preserve separators (_, &) */
                                }
                            }
                            KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
                            e->New[nameChars] = L'\0';
                            e->Len = (USHORT)nameChars;
                            g_NvmeSerialCount++;
                            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NVMe serial aliased (len=%u)\n", nameChars);
                        }
                    }
                }
                ExFreePoolWithTag(skbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            }
            ZwClose(hModel);
        }
        ExFreePoolWithTag(mkbi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    }
    ZwClose(hNvme);

    if (g_NvmeSerialCount > 0) {
        g_NvmeSpoofActive = TRUE;
        RegisterPciEnumCallback();
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] NVMe enum serial spoof: %u drive(s)\n", g_NvmeSerialCount);
    }
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* ============================================================
 * NVMe NGUID / EUI64 — extended DiskIoCompletion for
 * IOCTL_STORAGE_QUERY_PROPERTY with StorageDeviceIdProperty (1).
 *
 * Walks the STORAGE_DEVICE_ID_DESCRIPTOR identifier array and
 * randomises any binary (CodeSet=1) or EUI64/NGUID (Type=8/7)
 * identifiers in-place.
 * ============================================================ */

/* Minimal layout mirrors from storagespec.h to avoid pulling in user-mode headers */
typedef struct { ULONG Version; ULONG Size; ULONG NumberOfIdentifiers;
                 UCHAR Identifiers[1]; } STORAGE_ID_DESCRIPTOR;
typedef struct { ULONG CodeSet; ULONG Type; USHORT IdentifierSize;
                 USHORT NextOffset; ULONG Association; UCHAR Identifier[1]; } STORAGE_ID_ENTRY;

/* CODESET_Binary=1, TYPE_EUI64=8, TYPE_NGUID=7 (NVM Express specific) */
#define STORAGE_ID_CODESET_BINARY  1
#define STORAGE_ID_TYPE_EUI64      8
#define STORAGE_ID_TYPE_NGUID      7

VOID PatchNvmeIdentifiers(PIRP Irp) {
    STORAGE_ID_DESCRIPTOR* desc;
    ULONG i, off;
    KIRQL oldIrql;

    if (Irp->IoStatus.Information < sizeof(STORAGE_ID_DESCRIPTOR)) return;
    desc = (STORAGE_ID_DESCRIPTOR*)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
    if (!desc || desc->NumberOfIdentifiers == 0) return;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &oldIrql);

    off = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < desc->NumberOfIdentifiers && off < desc->Size; i++) {
        STORAGE_ID_ENTRY* e;
        if (off + sizeof(STORAGE_ID_ENTRY) > desc->Size) break;
        e = (STORAGE_ID_ENTRY*)(desc->Identifiers + off);
        if (e->IdentifierSize > 0 &&
            (e->CodeSet == STORAGE_ID_CODESET_BINARY ||
             e->Type    == STORAGE_ID_TYPE_EUI64     ||
             e->Type    == STORAGE_ID_TYPE_NGUID)) {
            ULONG j;
            for (j = 0; j < e->IdentifierSize; j++)
                e->Identifier[j] = (UCHAR)(KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed) & 0xFF);
        }
        off += (e->NextOffset != 0) ? e->NextOffset : (ULONG)(sizeof(STORAGE_ID_ENTRY) - 1 + e->IdentifierSize);
    }

    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, oldIrql);
}

/* ============================================================
 * GPT DISK GUID — IRP_MJ_READ completion on filter devices
 *
 * GPT header lives at byte offset 512 (LBA 1, 512-byte sectors)
 * or 4096 (LBA 1, 4K-native).  We check for the "EFI PART"
 * signature, then randomise the Disk GUID at header+0x38 and
 * recalculate the header CRC32.
 * ============================================================ */


NTSTATUS SpoofExtraRegistry2(VOID) {
    UCHAR  uuid[16];
    WCHAR  guidStr[48];
    KIRQL  irql;
    NTSTATUS st;

    /* HwProfileGuid */
    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    KernelGenUUID(uuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidStr, sizeof(guidStr),
        L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
        uuid[8],uuid[9], uuid[10],uuid[11],
        uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);
    st = RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control"
        L"\\IDConfigDB\\Hardware Profiles\\0001",
        L"HwProfileGuid", guidStr);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st))
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] HwProfileGuid write: 0x%08X\n", st);

    /* Windows Activation Technologies MachineId */
    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    KernelGenUUID(uuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidStr, sizeof(guidStr),
        L"%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x",
        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
        uuid[8],uuid[9], uuid[10],uuid[11],
        uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\Windows Activation Technologies\\AdminObject\\Store",
        L"MachineId", guidStr);

    /* NetworkInterfaceInstallTimestamp — zero it for all NIC class entries */
    {
        UNICODE_STRING nicPath;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
        HANDLE hClass = NULL;
        ULONG  idx = 0;
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&nicPath,
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class"
            L"\\{4d36e972-e325-11ce-bfc1-08002be10318}");
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &nicPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hClass, KEY_READ, &oa))) {
            ULONGLONG zeroTs = 0;
            for (;;) {
                ULONG len = 0;
                PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION ki;
                if (ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation,
                                   NULL, 0, &len) == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
                if (!len || len > 0x400) { idx++; continue; }
                ki = (PKEY_BASIC_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(len + 4, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                if (!ki) { idx++; continue; }
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwEnumerateKey(hClass, idx, KeyBasicInformation, ki, len, &len))) {
                    UNICODE_STRING sub = { (USHORT)ki->NameLength, (USHORT)ki->NameLength, ki->Name };
                    HANDLE hInst = NULL;
                    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaI;
                    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaI, &sub, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, hClass, NULL);
                    if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hInst, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaI))) {
                        UNICODE_STRING tsName;
                        RtlInitUnicodeString(&tsName, L"NetworkInterfaceInstallTimestamp");
                        ZwSetValueKey(hInst, &tsName, 0, REG_QWORD, &zeroTs, sizeof(zeroTs));
                        ZwClose(hInst);
                    }
                }
                ExFreePoolWithTag(ki, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
                idx++;
            }
            ZwClose(hClass);
        }
    }
    /* InstallDate (Unix timestamp) and InstallTime (FILETIME) — used by EAC to fingerprint
     * whether this is a "fresh" install, and as part of the combined HWID hash.           */
    {
        ULONG  randSecs;
        KIRQL  idIrql;
        ULONG  installDate;
        LARGE_INTEGER installTime;

        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &idIrql);
        randSecs = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, idIrql);

        /* Realistic install date: random day in 2022-2023 (Unix range 1640995200 – 1703980800) */
        installDate = 1640995200UL + (randSecs % (1703980800UL - 1640995200UL));
        /* InstallTime = same moment expressed as FILETIME (100-ns ticks since 1601-01-01)     */
        installTime.QuadPart = ((LONGLONG)installDate + 11644473600LL) * 10000000LL;

        {
            UNICODE_STRING vname;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaI;
            HANDLE hCv;
            UNICODE_STRING cvPath;

            RtlInitUnicodeString(&cvPath,
                L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion");
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaI, &cvPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hCv, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaI))) {
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"InstallDate");
                ZwSetValueKey(hCv, &vname, 0, REG_DWORD, &installDate, sizeof(installDate));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"InstallTime");
                ZwSetValueKey(hCv, &vname, 0, REG_QWORD, &installTime, sizeof(installTime));
                ZwClose(hCv);
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] InstallDate/Time -> 0x%08X\n", installDate);
            }
        }
    }

    /* Windows Product ID — HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\ProductId */
    {
        static const WCHAR pidChars[] = L"0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";
        WCHAR pid[24];
        ULONG r[4];
        ULONG i;
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) r[i] = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        /* Format: AAAAA-BBBBB-CCCCC-DDDDD  (each group = 5 alphanumeric chars) */
        pid[0]  = pidChars[(r[0] >>  0) % 36]; pid[1]  = pidChars[(r[0] >>  6) % 36];
        pid[2]  = pidChars[(r[0] >> 12) % 36]; pid[3]  = pidChars[(r[0] >> 18) % 36];
        pid[4]  = pidChars[(r[0] >> 24) % 36]; pid[5]  = L'-';
        pid[6]  = pidChars[(r[1] >>  0) % 36]; pid[7]  = pidChars[(r[1] >>  6) % 36];
        pid[8]  = pidChars[(r[1] >> 12) % 36]; pid[9]  = pidChars[(r[1] >> 18) % 36];
        pid[10] = pidChars[(r[1] >> 24) % 36]; pid[11] = L'-';
        pid[12] = pidChars[(r[2] >>  0) % 36]; pid[13] = pidChars[(r[2] >>  6) % 36];
        pid[14] = pidChars[(r[2] >> 12) % 36]; pid[15] = pidChars[(r[2] >> 18) % 36];
        pid[16] = pidChars[(r[2] >> 24) % 36]; pid[17] = L'-';
        pid[18] = pidChars[(r[3] >>  0) % 36]; pid[19] = pidChars[(r[3] >>  6) % 36];
        pid[20] = pidChars[(r[3] >> 12) % 36]; pid[21] = pidChars[(r[3] >> 18) % 36];
        pid[22] = pidChars[(r[3] >> 24) % 36]; pid[23] = L'\0';
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion",
            L"ProductId", pid);
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Windows ProductId -> %S\n", pid);

        /* DigitalProductId — REG_BINARY blob encoding the product key.
         * Zero it out so it can't be used to correlate the install.    */
        {
            UNICODE_STRING vdpi;
            UNICODE_STRING dpPath;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaDp;
            HANDLE hDp = NULL;
            UCHAR  zeroBuf[164];
            RtlZeroMemory(zeroBuf, sizeof(zeroBuf));
            /* Keep version marker (zeroBuf[0..3]) as original; EAC only reads
             * the key identifier portion bytes 52-67 (128-bit key hash).       */
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&dpPath,
                L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion");
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaDp, &dpPath,
```
