```c
                OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hDp, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaDp))) {
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vdpi, L"DigitalProductId");
                ZwSetValueKey(hDp, &vdpi, 0, REG_BINARY, zeroBuf, sizeof(zeroBuf));
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vdpi, L"DigitalProductId4");
                ZwSetValueKey(hDp, &vdpi, 0, REG_BINARY, zeroBuf, sizeof(zeroBuf));
                ZwClose(hDp);
            }
        }
    }

    /* Computer Name — four registry keys cover WMI (Win32_ComputerSystem.Name,
     * .DNSHostName), WMIC, and direct registry reads by EAC's kernel driver.
     * Live GetComputerNameW (kernel global set by SMSS at boot) takes effect
     * after the next reboot.                                                  */
    {
        static const WCHAR cnChars[] = L"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789";
        WCHAR   newName[16]; /* "DESKTOP-XXXXXXX\0" = 15 chars + null */
        ULONG   rng;
        ULONG   i;
        KIRQL   cnIrql;

        RtlCopyMemory(newName, L"DESKTOP-", 8 * sizeof(WCHAR));
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &cnIrql);
        rng = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, cnIrql);

        /* 7 random uppercase alphanumeric characters (Windows auto-gen format) */
        for (i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
            newName[8 + i] = cnChars[rng % 36];
            rng = rng * 1664525UL + 1013904223UL; /* Numerical Recipes LCG */
        }
        newName[15] = L'\0';

        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control"
            L"\\ComputerName\\ComputerName",
            L"ComputerName", newName);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control"
            L"\\ComputerName\\ActiveComputerName",
            L"ComputerName", newName);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services"
            L"\\Tcpip\\Parameters",
            L"Hostname", newName);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services"
            L"\\Tcpip\\Parameters",
            L"NV Hostname", newName);
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] ComputerName -> %S (live GetComputerNameW takes effect after reboot)\n",
                 newName);
    }

    /* Windows Advertising ID — unique per-device GUID used by telemetry */
    {
        UCHAR  adUuid[16];
        WCHAR  adGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(adUuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(adGuid, sizeof(adGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            adUuid[3],adUuid[2],adUuid[1],adUuid[0],
            adUuid[5],adUuid[4], adUuid[7],adUuid[6],
            adUuid[8],adUuid[9],
            adUuid[10],adUuid[11],adUuid[12],adUuid[13],adUuid[14],adUuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\AdvertisingInfo",
            L"Id", adGuid);
    }

    /* Connected Devices Platform machine ID */
    {
        UCHAR  cdpUuid[16];
        WCHAR  cdpGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(cdpUuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(cdpGuid, sizeof(cdpGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            cdpUuid[3],cdpUuid[2],cdpUuid[1],cdpUuid[0],
            cdpUuid[5],cdpUuid[4], cdpUuid[7],cdpUuid[6],
            cdpUuid[8],cdpUuid[9],
            cdpUuid[10],cdpUuid[11],cdpUuid[12],cdpUuid[13],cdpUuid[14],cdpUuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\CDPGraph",
            L"MachineId", cdpGuid);
    }

    /* DNS search suffix — clear to prevent ISP/domain correlation */
    {
        UNICODE_STRING dnsPath, dnsVal;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaDns;
        HANDLE hDns = NULL;
        WCHAR emptyW[] = L"";
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&dnsPath,
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services\\Tcpip\\Parameters");
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaDns, &dnsPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hDns, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaDns))) {
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&dnsVal, L"SearchList");
            ZwSetValueKey(hDns, &dnsVal, 0, REG_SZ, emptyW, sizeof(WCHAR));
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&dnsVal, L"DhcpDomain");
            ZwSetValueKey(hDns, &dnsVal, 0, REG_SZ, emptyW, sizeof(WCHAR));
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&dnsVal, L"Domain");
            ZwSetValueKey(hDns, &dnsVal, 0, REG_SZ, emptyW, sizeof(WCHAR));
            ZwClose(hDns);
        }
    }

    /* LanmanWorkstation ComputerName — SMB redirector caches its own copy */
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services"
        L"\\LanmanWorkstation\\Parameters",
        L"ComputerName", L"WORKSTATION");

    /* Session Manager\Environment — environment block baked at boot;
     * COMPUTERNAME and PROCESSOR_IDENTIFIER are queried directly by EAC. */
    {
        /* Re-use the same ComputerName already written to ActiveComputerName.
         * Read it back from the previously-written registry key.             */
        UCHAR   qbuf[256] = {0};
        UNICODE_STRING cvPath, vname;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaCV;
        HANDLE  hCV = NULL;
        ULONG   qlen = sizeof(qbuf);
        PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION vpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)qbuf;

        RtlInitUnicodeString(&cvPath,
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control"
            L"\\ComputerName\\ActiveComputerName");
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaCV, &cvPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hCV, KEY_QUERY_VALUE, &oaCV))) {
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"ComputerName");
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hCV, &vname, KeyValuePartialInformation,
                                           vpi, qlen, &qlen)) && vpi->DataLength > 2) {
                /* Write the same name into Session Manager environment */
                UNICODE_STRING envPath;
                OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaEnv;
                HANDLE hEnv = NULL;
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&envPath,
                    L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control"
                    L"\\Session Manager\\Environment");
                InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaEnv, &envPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hEnv, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaEnv))) {
                    UNICODE_STRING envName;
                    RtlInitUnicodeString(&envName, L"COMPUTERNAME");
                    ZwSetValueKey(hEnv, &envName, 0, REG_SZ, vpi->Data, vpi->DataLength);
                    ZwClose(hEnv);
                }
            }
            ZwClose(hCV);
        }

        /* PROCESSOR_IDENTIFIER — CPU family/model/stepping string, distinct
         * from CPUID and SMBIOS; read directly from this registry location.  */
        static const LPCWSTR cpuIdStrings[] = {
            L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 154 Stepping 4, GenuineIntel",
            L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 167 Stepping 1, GenuineIntel",
            L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 141 Stepping 1, GenuineIntel",
            L"AMD64 Family 25 Model 33 Stepping 0, AuthenticAMD",
            L"AMD64 Family 25 Model 80 Stepping 0, AuthenticAMD",
        };
        {
            ULONG rndCpu;
            KIRQL cpuIrql;
            UNICODE_STRING envPath2, envName2;
            OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oaEnv2;
            HANDLE hEnv2 = NULL;
            LPCWSTR chosen;
            KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &cpuIrql);
            rndCpu = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
            KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, cpuIrql);
            chosen = cpuIdStrings[rndCpu % 5];
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&envPath2,
                L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control"
                L"\\Session Manager\\Environment");
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oaEnv2, &envPath2, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hEnv2, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oaEnv2))) {
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&envName2, L"PROCESSOR_IDENTIFIER");
                ZwSetValueKey(hEnv2, &envName2, 0, REG_SZ, (PVOID)chosen,
                              (ULONG)((wcslen(chosen) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
                ZwClose(hEnv2);
            }
        }
    }

    /* NTP server — reset to generic to prevent ISP/timezone correlation */
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services"
        L"\\W32Time\\Parameters",
        L"NtpServer", L"time.windows.com,0x9");

    /* Terminal Server machine ID — unique hardware binding for RDP */
    {
        UCHAR  tsuuid[16];
        WCHAR  tsGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(tsuuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(tsGuid, sizeof(tsGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            tsuuid[3],tsuuid[2],tsuuid[1],tsuuid[0],
            tsuuid[5],tsuuid[4], tsuuid[7],tsuuid[6],
            tsuuid[8],tsuuid[9],
            tsuuid[10],tsuuid[11],tsuuid[12],tsuuid[13],tsuuid[14],tsuuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Terminal Server",
            L"MachineID", tsGuid);
    }

    /* Windows Error Reporting machine binding GUID */
    {
        UCHAR  weruuid[16];
        WCHAR  werGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(weruuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(werGuid, sizeof(werGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            weruuid[3],weruuid[2],weruuid[1],weruuid[0],
            weruuid[5],weruuid[4], weruuid[7],weruuid[6],
            weruuid[8],weruuid[9],
            weruuid[10],weruuid[11],weruuid[12],weruuid[13],weruuid[14],weruuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\Windows Error Reporting",
            L"MachineID", werGuid);
    }

    /* Windows Update last-check / last-install timestamps — replace with
     * plausible historical UTC strings so fresh-install detection fails.     */
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\WindowsUpdate\\Auto Update\\Results\\Detect",
        L"LastSuccessTime", L"2023-08-12 04:12:11");
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion"
        L"\\WindowsUpdate\\Auto Update\\Results\\Install",
        L"LastSuccessTime", L"2023-08-12 04:18:44");

    /* DiagTrack DeviceId — persistent cross-reinstall hardware binding used
     * by telemetry pipeline; distinct from MachineId / SQMClient.           */
    {
        UCHAR  dtuuid[16];
        WCHAR  dtGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(dtuuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(dtGuid, sizeof(dtGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            dtuuid[3],dtuuid[2],dtuuid[1],dtuuid[0],
            dtuuid[5],dtuuid[4], dtuuid[7],dtuuid[6],
            dtuuid[8],dtuuid[9],
            dtuuid[10],dtuuid[11],dtuuid[12],dtuuid[13],dtuuid[14],dtuuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion"
            L"\\Diagnostics\\DiagTrack",
            L"DeviceId", dtGuid);
    }

    /* DiagTrack ClientId — separate persistent identifier from DeviceId;
     * shares the same key but is a distinct cross-reinstall correlator.   */
    {
        UCHAR  cluuid[16];
        WCHAR  clGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(cluuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(clGuid, sizeof(clGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            cluuid[3],cluuid[2],cluuid[1],cluuid[0],
            cluuid[5],cluuid[4], cluuid[7],cluuid[6],
            cluuid[8],cluuid[9],
            cluuid[10],cluuid[11],cluuid[12],cluuid[13],cluuid[14],cluuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion"
            L"\\Diagnostics\\DiagTrack",
            L"ClientId", clGuid);
    }

    /* Windows Defender MachineGuid — separate from Cryptography\MachineGuid;
     * used by Defender to identify the endpoint, persists across reinstalls. */
    {
        UCHAR  dfuuid[16];
        WCHAR  dfGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(dfuuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(dfGuid, sizeof(dfGuid),
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            dfuuid[3],dfuuid[2],dfuuid[1],dfuuid[0],
            dfuuid[5],dfuuid[4], dfuuid[7],dfuuid[6],
            dfuuid[8],dfuuid[9],
            dfuuid[10],dfuuid[11],dfuuid[12],dfuuid[13],dfuuid[14],dfuuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows Defender\\MpClient",
            L"MachineGuid", dfGuid);
        /* Also patch legacy MSE path used on older Windows 10 builds */
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Microsoft Antimalware\\MpClient",
            L"MachineGuid", dfGuid);
    }

    /* SoftwareProtectionPlatform ClientMachineId — Windows activation machine
     * binding GUID; read by WPA/licensing stack and leaks to any process that
     * calls SLGetWindowsInformationDWORD.                                      */
    {
        UCHAR  spuuid[16];
        WCHAR  spGuid[48];
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        KernelGenUUID(spuuid, &g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        RtlStringCbPrintfW(spGuid, sizeof(spGuid),
            L"%08x-%04x-%04x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x",
            ((ULONG*)spuuid)[0],
            ((USHORT*)spuuid)[2], ((USHORT*)spuuid)[3],
            spuuid[8], spuuid[9],
            spuuid[10],spuuid[11],spuuid[12],spuuid[13],spuuid[14],spuuid[15]);
        RegSetStringW(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion"
            L"\\SoftwareProtectionPlatform",
            L"ClientMachineId", spGuid);
    }

    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Extra registry patches applied\n");
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}


VOID SpoofHardwareDescRegistry(VOID) {
    static const LPCWSTR biosManuf[] = {
        L"LENOVO", L"ASUSTeK COMPUTER INC.", L"Dell Inc.",
        L"HP", L"Gigabyte Technology Co., Ltd.", L"Micro-Star International Co., Ltd."
    };
    static const LPCWSTR biosProduct[] = {
        L"CB-01",       L"ROG STRIX B550-F GAMING", L"Inspiron 5579",
        L"HP Pavilion", L"B550M DS3H AC",            L"MPG B550 GAMING PLUS"
    };
    static const LPCWSTR biosVersion[] = {
        L"R1MET58W", L"3203", L"1.21.0", L"F.70 Rev.A", L"F14", L"E7C56IMS.160"
    };
    static const LPCWSTR biosDate[] = {
        L"05/17/2022", L"11/25/2022", L"06/04/2023",
        L"09/20/2022", L"03/14/2023", L"01/10/2023"
    };
    static const LPCWSTR cpuIdents[] = {
        L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 154 Stepping 4, GenuineIntel",
        L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 167 Stepping 1, GenuineIntel",
        L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 141 Stepping 1, GenuineIntel",
        L"AMD64 Family 25 Model 33 Stepping 0, AuthenticAMD",
        L"AMD64 Family 25 Model 80 Stepping 0, AuthenticAMD",
        L"Intel64 Family 6 Model 158 Stepping 13, GenuineIntel",
    };
    static const LPCWSTR cpuBrands[] = {
        L"12th Gen Intel(R) Core(TM) i7-12700K",
        L"11th Gen Intel(R) Core(TM) i9-11900K @ 3.50GHz",
        L"11th Gen Intel(R) Core(TM) i5-11400F @ 2.60GHz",
        L"AMD Ryzen 5 5600X 6-Core Processor",
        L"AMD Ryzen 7 5800X3D 8-Core Processor",
        L"Intel(R) Core(TM) i7-9700K CPU @ 3.60GHz",
    };
    ULONG rnd, idx;
    KIRQL irql;

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    rnd = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    idx = rnd % 6;

    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"SystemManufacturer", biosManuf[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"SystemProductName", biosProduct[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"BIOSVendor", biosManuf[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"BIOSVersion", biosVersion[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"BIOSReleaseDate", biosDate[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"BaseBoardManufacturer", biosManuf[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
        L"BaseBoardProduct", biosProduct[idx]);
    if (g_SpoofedBoardSerial[0])
        RegSetStringA(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
            L"BaseBoardSerial", g_SpoofedBoardSerial);
    if (g_SpoofedSystemSerial[0])
        RegSetStringA(
            L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\BIOS",
            L"SystemSerial", g_SpoofedSystemSerial);

    KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
    rnd = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
    KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
    idx = rnd % 6;

    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\CentralProcessor\\0",
        L"Identifier", cpuIdents[idx]);
    RegSetStringW(
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System\\CentralProcessor\\0",
        L"ProcessorNameString", cpuBrands[idx]);

    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] HARDWARE\\DESCRIPTION\\System patched (idx=%u board=%s sys=%s)\n",
             idx, g_SpoofedBoardSerial, g_SpoofedSystemSerial);
}

/* ============================================================ */

```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_smbios.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"


/* ============================================================
 * MSSMBIOS WMI FILTER — intercept IRP_MJ_SYSTEM_CONTROL completions
 * so WMI queries (Win32_BaseBoard, Win32_BIOS etc.) return spoofed
 * SMBIOS values consistent with the kernel pool patch.
 *
 * WNODE_ALL_DATA layout (offsets from buffer start):
 *   0x00-0x2F : WNODE_HEADER (48 bytes)
 *   0x30      : DataBlockOffset (ULONG) — QUERY_ALL_DATA
 * WNODE_SINGLE_INSTANCE layout:
 *   0x38      : DataBlockOffset (ULONG) — QUERY_SINGLE_INSTANCE
 * The data at buf[DataBlockOffset] may be prefixed by a uint32 Size field
 * (MSSMBios_RawSMBiosTables layout on Windows 10/11 UEFI systems) before
 * the raw SMBIOS table bytes.  MssmbiosWmiCompletion auto-detects this.
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS MssmbiosWmiCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack;
    UCHAR  minorFn;
    PUCHAR buf;
    ULONG  bufSize, dataOffset;

    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);

    /* WMI buffer lives in paged pool; MmIsAddressValid is unreliable at DISPATCH_LEVEL. */
    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) goto mwc_done;

    if (!NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status)) goto mwc_done;
    if (!g_SMBIOSSpoofActive || !g_SmbiosReqSaved) goto mwc_done;

    stack   = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    minorFn = stack->MinorFunction;
    buf     = (PUCHAR)stack->Parameters.WMI.Buffer;
    bufSize = stack->Parameters.WMI.BufferSize;

    if (!buf || bufSize < 0x44u) goto mwc_done;
    if (!MmIsAddressValid(buf) || !MmIsAddressValid(buf + bufSize - 1)) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] MssmbiosWmiCompletion: WMI buffer %p+%u invalid VA — skipped\n",
            buf, bufSize);
        goto mwc_done;
    }

    if (minorFn == IRP_MN_QUERY_ALL_DATA)
        dataOffset = *(PULONG)(buf + 0x30);      /* WNODE_ALL_DATA::DataBlockOffset */
    else if (minorFn == IRP_MN_QUERY_SINGLE_INSTANCE)
        dataOffset = *(PULONG)(buf + 0x38);      /* WNODE_SINGLE_INSTANCE::DataBlockOffset */
    else
        goto mwc_done;

    if (dataOffset < 0x30u || dataOffset >= bufSize) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] MssmbiosWmiCompletion: bad dataOffset 0x%X (bufSize=%u) — skipped\n",
            dataOffset, bufSize);
        goto mwc_done;
    }

    __try {
        PUCHAR smData  = buf + dataOffset;
        ULONG  smAvail = bufSize - dataOffset;
        /* MSSMBios_RawSMBiosTables prefixes the SMBIOS structures with a uint32 Size
         * field on most Windows 10/11 builds.  SMBIOS Type 0 (BIOS Information) is
         * always the first structure, so its first byte is 0x00.  If byte 0 is not
         * 0x00 but byte 4 is, the Size DWORD is present — skip it. */
        if (smAvail > 4 && smData[0] != 0) {
            ULONG pfxSize = *(PULONG)smData;
            if (pfxSize > 0 && pfxSize <= smAvail - 4 && smData[4] == 0) {
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] MssmbiosWmiCompletion: Size prefix detected (0x%lX) — skipping\n",
                    pfxSize);
                smData  += 4;
                smAvail  = pfxSize;
            }
        }
        PatchKernelSMBIOSTableData(smData, smAvail, &g_LastSmbiosReq);
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] MssmbiosWmiCompletion: WMI SMBIOS buffer patched (minor=%u off=0x%X size=%u)\n",
            minorFn, dataOffset, bufSize);
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] MssmbiosWmiCompletion: exception 0x%08X patching WMI buffer\n",
            GetExceptionCode());
    }

mwc_done:
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

NTSTATUS MssmbiosSystemControlDispatch(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION  ext   = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)DeviceObject->DeviceExtension;
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);

    if (!ext || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        Irp->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
    }

    if (g_SMBIOSSpoofActive && g_SmbiosReqSaved &&
        (stack->MinorFunction == IRP_MN_QUERY_ALL_DATA ||
         stack->MinorFunction == IRP_MN_QUERY_SINGLE_INSTANCE)) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, MssmbiosWmiCompletion, NULL, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }

    IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
}


void DetachSmbiosFilter(void) {
    if (g_SmbiosFilterDevice) {
        PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)g_SmbiosFilterDevice->DeviceExtension;
        if (ext && ext->LowerDevice) IoDetachDevice(ext->LowerDevice);
        IoDeleteDevice(g_SmbiosFilterDevice);
        g_SmbiosFilterDevice = NULL;
    }
}

/* ============================================================
 * KERNEL SMBIOS BUFFER — locate and patch the pool allocation that
 * backs NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemFirmwareTableInformation/RSMB).
 * This is what GetSystemFirmwareTable() and EAC read at runtime.
 * ============================================================ */

/* Class 76 = SystemFirmwareTableInformation */
#ifndef SFTI_CLASS
#define SFTI_CLASS  76
#endif

typedef struct _SFTI {
    ULONG ProviderSignature;
    ULONG Action;               /* 1 = SystemFirmwareTable_Get */
    ULONG TableID;
    ULONG TableBufferLength;    /* in: space; out: bytes written */
    UCHAR TableBuffer[1];
} SFTI, *PSFTI;

VOID FindKernelSMBIOSBuffer(VOID) {
    PSFTI    sfti;
    ULONG    needed = 0, allocSize;
    NTSTATUS st;
    UCHAR    pat[64];
    ULONG    patLen;

    /* First call: ask for required size */
    {
        SFTI probe;
        RtlZeroMemory(&probe, sizeof(probe));
        probe.ProviderSignature = 'RSMB';
        probe.Action            = 1;
        ZwQuerySystemInformation(SFTI_CLASS, &probe, sizeof(probe), &needed);
    }
    if (needed < sizeof(SFTI) + 16) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindKernelSMBIOSBuffer: size query failed (needed=%u)\n", needed);
        return;
    }

    allocSize = needed + 0x100;
    sfti = (PSFTI)SafeAllocatePool(allocSize, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
    if (!sfti) return;
    RtlZeroMemory(sfti, allocSize);
    sfti->ProviderSignature = 'RSMB';
    sfti->Action            = 1;
    sfti->TableBufferLength = allocSize - FIELD_OFFSET(SFTI, TableBuffer);

    st = ZwQuerySystemInformation(SFTI_CLASS, sfti, allocSize, &needed);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st) || sfti->TableBufferLength < 16) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindKernelSMBIOSBuffer: data query failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
        ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        return;
    }

    /* TableBuffer = RAWSMBIOSDATA: [0]=Used20, [1]=Major, [2]=Minor, [3]=Dmi,
     *                               [4-7]=Length, [8..]=SMBIOS structures.    */
    /* Skip the 8-byte RAWSMBIOSDATA header (UsedCallerBuffer/Major/Minor/DmiRevision/Length)
     * when building the search pattern — the header fields can differ between the probe copy
     * and the live HAL source buffer (e.g. UsedCallerBuffer is set by the caller, not HAL).
     * Zero the probe buffer before freeing so the backward scan doesn't match the freed pool
     * memory and mistake it for the live HAL source. */
    patLen = (sfti->TableBufferLength > 8) ? min(56, sfti->TableBufferLength - 8) : 0;
    if (patLen == 0) {
        ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        SPOOF_LOG(
                   "[Spoofer] FindKernelSMBIOSBuffer: SMBIOS table too small\n");
        return;
    }
    /* RAWSMBIOSDATA[1] = SMBIOSMajorVersion — save before zeroing/freeing the buffer.
     * pat is copied from TableBuffer+8 (first SMBIOS structure), so pat[1] is the
     * structure's Length field, NOT the version — they must not be confused. */
    {
        UCHAR smbiosMajorVer = sfti->TableBuffer[1];
        RtlCopyMemory(pat, sfti->TableBuffer + 8, patLen);
        RtlZeroMemory(sfti->TableBuffer, sfti->TableBufferLength);
        ExFreePoolWithTag(sfti, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

        if (smbiosMajorVer < 2 || smbiosMajorVer > 3) {
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindKernelSMBIOSBuffer: bad SMBIOS major version %u\n", smbiosMajorVer);
            return;
        }
    }

    /* Anchor: fresh allocation gives us a "current" pool address.
     * The SMBIOS buffer is an early-boot allocation → almost always
     * at a LOWER virtual address than runtime allocations.          */
    {
        PUCHAR   anchor = (PUCHAR)SafeAllocatePool(16, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        PUCHAR   base;
        LONG_PTR delta;
        if (!anchor) return;
        base = (PUCHAR)((ULONG_PTR)anchor & ~(ULONG_PTR)0xFFF);
        ExFreePoolWithTag(anchor, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);

        /* Backward pass — 512 MB.
         * Pool allocations are 16-byte aligned, not page-aligned, so scan at
         * page granularity for the outer loop but step through 16-byte offsets
         * within each valid page for the inner loop.                          */
        for (delta = 0; delta > -(LONG_PTR)(512ULL << 20); delta -= 0x1000) {
            PUCHAR page = base + delta;
            ULONG  off;
            if ((ULONG_PTR)page < 0xFFFF800000000000ULL) break;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(page)) continue;
            for (off = 0; off + 8 + patLen <= 0x1000; off += 16) {
                PUCHAR cand = page + off;
                ULONG  tblLen;
                if (!MmIsAddressValid(cand + 8 + patLen - 1)) break;
                if (RtlCompareMemory(cand + 8, pat, patLen) != patLen) continue;
                tblLen = *(PULONG)(cand + 4);
                if (tblLen < 100 || tblLen > 0x100000) continue;
                g_SMBIOSKernelBuf    = cand;
                g_SMBIOSKernelBufLen = 8 + tblLen;
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Kernel SMBIOS buffer @ %p (%u bytes)\n", cand, tblLen);
                return;
            }
        }
        /* Forward pass — 64 MB (fallback for early-boot mappers) */
        for (delta = 0x1000; delta < (LONG_PTR)(64ULL << 20); delta += 0x1000) {
            PUCHAR page = base + delta;
            ULONG  off;
            if (!MmIsAddressValid(page)) continue;
            for (off = 0; off + 8 + patLen <= 0x1000; off += 16) {
                PUCHAR cand = page + off;
                ULONG  tblLen;
                if (!MmIsAddressValid(cand + 8 + patLen - 1)) break;
                if (RtlCompareMemory(cand + 8, pat, patLen) != patLen) continue;
                tblLen = *(PULONG)(cand + 4);
                if (tblLen < 100 || tblLen > 0x100000) continue;
                g_SMBIOSKernelBuf    = cand;
                g_SMBIOSKernelBufLen = 8 + tblLen;
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Kernel SMBIOS buffer (fwd) @ %p (%u bytes)\n", cand, tblLen);
                return;
            }
        }
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindKernelSMBIOSBuffer: not found in 512+64 MB scan\n");
}

/* Walk SMBIOS table data and apply UUID + serial patches in-place.
 * Same logic as the physical-memory walk in FindAndPatchSMBIOS so
 * both code paths stay consistent.                                  */
VOID PatchKernelSMBIOSTableData(PUCHAR tableData, ULONG tableLen,
                                        PSPOOF_SMBIOS_REQUEST req) {
    PUCHAR ptr = tableData;
    PUCHAR end = tableData + tableLen;
    __try {
        while (ptr < end - 4) {
            UCHAR type   = ptr[0];
            UCHAR length = ptr[1];
            if (length < 4) break;
            if (type == 0) {
                if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewBIOSVendor[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x04], req->NewBIOSVendor);
                if (length >= 0x06 && req->NewBIOSVersion[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x05], req->NewBIOSVersion);
                if (length >= 0x09 && req->NewBIOSReleaseDate[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x08], req->NewBIOSReleaseDate);
            }
            if (type == 1) {
                if (length >= 0x19) RtlCopyMemory(ptr + 0x08, req->NewUUID, 16);
                if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewSystemManufacturer[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x04], req->NewSystemManufacturer);
                if (length >= 0x06 && req->NewSystemProduct[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x05], req->NewSystemProduct);
                if (length >= 0x08) PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x07], req->NewSystemSerial);
            }
            if (type == 2) {
                if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewBoardManufacturer[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x04], req->NewBoardManufacturer);
                if (length >= 0x06 && req->NewBoardProduct[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x05], req->NewBoardProduct);
                if (length >= 0x08) PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x07], req->NewBoardSerial);
            }
            if (type == 3 && length >= 0x08) PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x07], req->NewChassisSerial);
            if (type == 4) {
                if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewProcessorSocket[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x04], req->NewProcessorSocket);
                if (length >= 0x08 && req->NewProcessorManufacturer[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x07], req->NewProcessorManufacturer);
                if (length >= 0x10 && req->NewProcessorLeaf1EAX)
                    *(PULONG)(ptr + 0x08) = req->NewProcessorLeaf1EAX;
                if (length >= 0x10 && req->NewProcessorLeaf1EDX)
                    *(PULONG)(ptr + 0x0C) = req->NewProcessorLeaf1EDX;
                if (length >= 0x11 && req->NewProcessorVersion[0])
                    PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x10], req->NewProcessorVersion);
            }
            if (type == 17) {
                /* Numeric fields first (no string table involvement) */
                if (length >= 0x0E && req->NewMemorySize)
                    *(PUSHORT)(ptr + 0x0C) = req->NewMemorySize;
                if (length >= 0x13 && req->NewMemoryType)
                    ptr[0x12] = req->NewMemoryType;
                if (length >= 0x17 && req->NewMemorySpeed)
                    *(PUSHORT)(ptr + 0x15) = req->NewMemorySpeed;
                if (length >= 0x22 && req->NewMemoryConfiguredSpeed)
                    *(PUSHORT)(ptr + 0x20) = req->NewMemoryConfiguredSpeed;
                /* String fields (SMBIOS 2.3+, length >= 0x1B) */
                if (length >= 0x1B) {
                    if (req->NewMemoryManufacturer[0])
                        PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x17], req->NewMemoryManufacturer);
                    if (req->NewMemorySerial[0])
                        PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x18], req->NewMemorySerial);
                    if (req->NewMemoryPartNumber[0])
                        PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x1A], req->NewMemoryPartNumber);
                }
            }
            if (type == 22 && length >= 0x08 && req->NewBatterySerial[0])
                PatchSMBIOSString(ptr, end, ptr[0x07], req->NewBatterySerial);
            if (type == 127) break;
            ptr += length;
            while (ptr < end - 1 && !(ptr[0] == 0 && ptr[1] == 0)) ptr++;
            ptr += 2;
        }
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchKernelSMBIOSTableData: exception 0x%08X\n", GetExceptionCode());
    }
}

/* Overwrite HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Cryptography\MachineGuid with a new
 * GUID derived from newUUID.  EAC reads this on every session start.    */
VOID PatchMachineGuid(PUCHAR uuid) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath, valueName;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    NTSTATUS st;
    WCHAR guidStr[48];

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Cryptography");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchMachineGuid: ZwOpenKey failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return;
    }

    /* Read current MachineGuid before overwriting */
    {
        WCHAR oldGuid[48] = {0};
        UNICODE_STRING qvn;
        PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION kvpi;
        ULONG kvpiSz = sizeof(KEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION) + sizeof(oldGuid);
        kvpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)SafeAllocatePool(kvpiSz, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
        if (kvpi) {
            ULONG rl = 0;
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&qvn, L"MachineGuid");
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwQueryValueKey(hKey, &qvn, KeyValuePartialInformation, kvpi, kvpiSz, &rl))
                    && kvpi->Type == REG_SZ)
                RtlCopyMemory(oldGuid, kvpi->Data, min(kvpi->DataLength, sizeof(oldGuid) - sizeof(WCHAR)));
            ExFreePoolWithTag(kvpi, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (oldGuid[0])
                SPOOF_LOG(
                    "[Spoofer] MachineGuid before: %S\n", oldGuid);
        }
    }

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidStr, sizeof(guidStr),
        L"%08x-%04x-%04x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x",
        *(PULONG) uuid,
        *(PUSHORT)(uuid + 4),
        *(PUSHORT)(uuid + 6),
        uuid[8],  uuid[9],
        uuid[10], uuid[11], uuid[12], uuid[13], uuid[14], uuid[15]);
    RtlInitUnicodeString(&valueName, L"MachineGuid");
    st = ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &valueName, 0, REG_SZ, guidStr,
                       (ULONG)(wcslen(guidStr) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR));
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st))
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] PatchMachineGuid: ZwSetValueKey failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
    else
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] MachineGuid after:  %S\n", guidStr);
    ZwClose(hKey);
}

/* ============================================================
 * SMBIOS SPOOFING — physical memory patch
 *
 * Patches:
 *   Type 1 — System UUID (fixed offset 0x08) + Serial Number string
 *   Type 2 — Base Board Serial Number string
 *   Type 3 — Chassis Serial Number string
 * ============================================================ */

VOID PatchSMBIOSString(PUCHAR structBase, PUCHAR endPtr,
                               UCHAR stringIndex, const char* newValue) {
    PUCHAR strPtr;
    UCHAR  idx;
    SIZE_T oldLen, newLen, copyLen;
    PUCHAR tmp;

    if (stringIndex == 0 || !newValue || newValue[0] == '\0') return;

    strPtr = structBase + structBase[1];
    idx    = 1;

    while (strPtr < endPtr && idx < stringIndex) {
        while (strPtr < endPtr && *strPtr != 0) strPtr++;
        if (strPtr < endPtr) strPtr++;
        idx++;
    }

    if (strPtr >= endPtr || idx != stringIndex) return;

    oldLen = 0;
    tmp = strPtr;
    while (tmp < endPtr && *tmp != 0) { tmp++; oldLen++; }

    newLen  = strlen(newValue);
    copyLen = (newLen < oldLen) ? newLen : oldLen;
    RtlCopyMemory(strPtr, newValue, copyLen);
    if (newLen < oldLen) {
        RtlZeroMemory(strPtr + newLen, oldLen - newLen);
    }
}

NTSTATUS FindAndPatchSMBIOS(PSPOOF_SMBIOS_REQUEST req) {
    PHYSICAL_ADDRESS physAddr;
    PVOID   mappedMem;
    PUCHAR  searchPtr;
    SIZE_T  i;

    physAddr.QuadPart = 0xF0000;
    mappedMem = MmMapIoSpace(physAddr, 0x10000, MmNonCached);
    if (!mappedMem) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindAndPatchSMBIOS: MmMapIoSpace(0xF0000) failed\n");
        return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
    }

    searchPtr = (PUCHAR)mappedMem;

    for (i = 0; i < 0x10000 - 0x20; i += 16) {
        if (searchPtr[i]   == '_' && searchPtr[i+1] == 'S' &&
            searchPtr[i+2] == 'M' && searchPtr[i+3] == '_') {

            ULONG64 tableAddr  = *(PULONG64)(searchPtr + i + 0x18);
            USHORT tableLength = *(PUSHORT)(searchPtr + i + 0x16);
            PUCHAR structPtr, endPtr;

            if (tableAddr == 0 || tableLength < 32 || tableLength > 0x100000) {
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SMBIOS bad table addr=0x%llX len=%u\n",
                         tableAddr, (ULONG)tableLength);
                MmUnmapIoSpace(mappedMem, 0x10000);
                return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
            }

            MmUnmapIoSpace(mappedMem, 0x10000);

            physAddr.QuadPart = tableAddr;
            mappedMem = MmMapIoSpace(physAddr, tableLength, MmNonCached);
            if (!mappedMem) {
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindAndPatchSMBIOS: MmMapIoSpace(0x%llX, %u) failed\n",
                         tableAddr, (ULONG)tableLength);
                return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
            }

            g_SMBIOSPatchAddress = mappedMem;
            g_SMBIOSPatchSize    = tableLength;
            g_SMBIOSOriginalData = SafeAllocatePool(tableLength, POOL_TAG_SPOOF);
            if (g_SMBIOSOriginalData) {
                RtlCopyMemory(g_SMBIOSOriginalData, mappedMem, tableLength);
            } else {
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindAndPatchSMBIOS: backup alloc failed (%u bytes)"
                         " — SMBIOS restore on unload will be skipped\n", (ULONG)tableLength);
            }

            structPtr = (PUCHAR)mappedMem;
            endPtr    = structPtr + tableLength;

            __try {
                while (structPtr < endPtr - 4) {
                    UCHAR type   = structPtr[0];
                    UCHAR length = structPtr[1];

                    if (length < 4) break;

                    if (type == 0) {
                        if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewBIOSVendor[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x04], req->NewBIOSVendor);
                        if (length >= 0x06 && req->NewBIOSVersion[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x05], req->NewBIOSVersion);
                        if (length >= 0x09 && req->NewBIOSReleaseDate[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x08], req->NewBIOSReleaseDate);
                    } else if (type == 1) {
                        if (length >= 0x19) RtlCopyMemory(structPtr + 0x08, req->NewUUID, 16);
                        if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewSystemManufacturer[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x04], req->NewSystemManufacturer);
                        if (length >= 0x06 && req->NewSystemProduct[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x05], req->NewSystemProduct);
                        if (length >= 0x08)
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x07], req->NewSystemSerial);
                    } else if (type == 2) {
                        if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewBoardManufacturer[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x04], req->NewBoardManufacturer);
                        if (length >= 0x06 && req->NewBoardProduct[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x05], req->NewBoardProduct);
                        if (length >= 0x08)
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x07], req->NewBoardSerial);
                    } else if (type == 3 && length >= 0x08) {
                        PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x07], req->NewChassisSerial);
                    } else if (type == 4) {
                        if (length >= 0x05 && req->NewProcessorSocket[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x04], req->NewProcessorSocket);
                        if (length >= 0x08 && req->NewProcessorManufacturer[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x07], req->NewProcessorManufacturer);
                        if (length >= 0x10 && req->NewProcessorLeaf1EAX)
                            *(PULONG)(structPtr + 0x08) = req->NewProcessorLeaf1EAX;
                        if (length >= 0x10 && req->NewProcessorLeaf1EDX)
                            *(PULONG)(structPtr + 0x0C) = req->NewProcessorLeaf1EDX;
                        if (length >= 0x11 && req->NewProcessorVersion[0])
                            PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x10], req->NewProcessorVersion);
                    } else if (type == 17) {
                        if (length >= 0x0E && req->NewMemorySize)
                            *(PUSHORT)(structPtr + 0x0C) = req->NewMemorySize;
                        if (length >= 0x13 && req->NewMemoryType)
                            structPtr[0x12] = req->NewMemoryType;
                        if (length >= 0x17 && req->NewMemorySpeed)
                            *(PUSHORT)(structPtr + 0x15) = req->NewMemorySpeed;
                        if (length >= 0x22 && req->NewMemoryConfiguredSpeed)
                            *(PUSHORT)(structPtr + 0x20) = req->NewMemoryConfiguredSpeed;
                        if (length >= 0x1B) {
                            if (req->NewMemoryManufacturer[0])
                                PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x17], req->NewMemoryManufacturer);
                            if (req->NewMemorySerial[0])
                                PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x18], req->NewMemorySerial);
                            if (req->NewMemoryPartNumber[0])
                                PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x1A], req->NewMemoryPartNumber);
                        }
                    } else if (type == 22 && length >= 0x08 && req->NewBatterySerial[0]) {
                        PatchSMBIOSString(structPtr, endPtr, structPtr[0x07], req->NewBatterySerial);
                    } else if (type == 127) {
                        break;
                    }

                    structPtr += length;
                    while (structPtr < endPtr - 1 &&
                           !(structPtr[0] == 0 && structPtr[1] == 0)) {
                        structPtr++;
                    }
                    structPtr += 2;
                }
            }
            __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SMBIOS walk exception: 0x%08X\n",
                         GetExceptionCode());
            }

            return STATUS_SUCCESS;
        }
    }

    MmUnmapIoSpace(mappedMem, 0x10000);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] FindAndPatchSMBIOS: '_SM_' anchor not found in 0xF0000-0xFFFFF\n");
    return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
}


NTSTATUS AttachFilterToMssmbios(PDRIVER_OBJECT DriverObject) {
    NTSTATUS       status;
    UNICODE_STRING smbiosName;
    PDRIVER_OBJECT smbiosDriver = NULL;
    PDEVICE_OBJECT smbiosDevice;
    PDEVICE_OBJECT filterDevice = NULL;
    PFILTER_EXTENSION ext;

    if (!g_ObReferenceObjectByName || !g_IoDriverObjectType) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Undocumented APIs not resolved — skipping mssmbios filter\n");
        return STATUS_NOT_IMPLEMENTED;
    }

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&smbiosName, L"\\Driver\\mssmbios");
    status = g_ObReferenceObjectByName(
        &smbiosName, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE, NULL, 0,
        *g_IoDriverObjectType, KernelMode, NULL, (PVOID*)&smbiosDriver);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || !smbiosDriver) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] mssmbios not found: 0x%08X\n", status);
        return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
    }

    smbiosDevice = smbiosDriver->DeviceObject;
    if (!smbiosDevice) {
        ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDriver);
        return STATUS_NOT_FOUND;
    }
    ObReferenceObject(smbiosDevice);

    status = IoCreateDevice(
        DriverObject, sizeof(FILTER_EXTENSION), NULL,
        smbiosDevice->DeviceType, 0, FALSE, &filterDevice);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] IoCreateDevice for mssmbios filter failed: 0x%08X\n", status);
        ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDevice);
        ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDriver);
        return status;
    }

    ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)filterDevice->DeviceExtension;
    ext->Magic = FILTER_MAGIC;
    ext->Type  = FILTER_TYPE_SMBIOS;

    filterDevice->Flags |= smbiosDevice->Flags & (DO_BUFFERED_IO | DO_DIRECT_IO);
    filterDevice->Characteristics |= smbiosDevice->Characteristics;

    ext->LowerDevice = IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack(filterDevice, smbiosDevice);
    if (!ext->LowerDevice) {
        IoDeleteDevice(filterDevice);
        ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDevice);
        ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDriver);
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack failed for mssmbios\n");
        return STATUS_UNSUCCESSFUL;
    }

    filterDevice->AlignmentRequirement = ext->LowerDevice->AlignmentRequirement;
    filterDevice->Flags &= ~DO_DEVICE_INITIALIZING;
    g_SmbiosFilterDevice = filterDevice;

    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] mssmbios WMI filter attached (dev=%p lower=%p)\n",
             smbiosDevice, ext->LowerDevice);
    ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDevice);
    ObDereferenceObject(smbiosDriver);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}


VOID PatchSmbiosRegistryCache(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath, vname;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    NTSTATUS st;

    if (!g_SMBIOSKernelBuf || g_SMBIOSKernelBufLen == 0) return;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath,
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services\\mssmbios\\Data");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] mssmbios key not accessible: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return;
    }
    RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"SMBiosData");
    st = ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &vname, 0, REG_BINARY, g_SMBIOSKernelBuf, g_SMBIOSKernelBufLen);
    ZwClose(hKey);
    if (NT_SUCCESS(st))
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] mssmbios\\SMBiosData registry cache updated\n");
    else
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] mssmbios\\SMBiosData write failed: 0x%08X\n", st);
}

/* ============================================================
 * MOTHERBOARD UUID REGISTRY CLEANUP
 * Patches SYSTEM\HardwareConfig (LastConfig value and UUID subkeys)
 * and Office ClientTelemetry MotherboardUUID.
 * ============================================================ */

VOID PatchMotherboardUuidKeys(PUCHAR uuid) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath, vname;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    NTSTATUS st;
    WCHAR guidBrace[48]; /* {xxxxxxxx-xxxx-...} */
    WCHAR guidPlain[48]; /* xxxxxxxx-xxxx-...   */

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidBrace, sizeof(guidBrace),
        L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
        uuid[8],uuid[9], uuid[10],uuid[11],
        uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(guidPlain, sizeof(guidPlain),
        L"%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x",
        uuid[3],uuid[2],uuid[1],uuid[0],
        uuid[5],uuid[4], uuid[7],uuid[6],
        uuid[8],uuid[9], uuid[10],uuid[11],
        uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);

    /* HKLM\SYSTEM\HardwareConfig\LastConfig */
    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\HardwareConfig");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa))) {
        /* Read the CURRENT LastConfig before overwriting it — we must keep
         * that subkey intact so the live PnP Manager (in the critical
         * DcomLaunch svchost) can still reference its in-session profile.
         * Deleting the active hardware-profile subkey mid-session causes
         * PnP to dereference a stale handle and crash with 0xEF. */
        WCHAR oldLastConfig[48] = {0};
        {
            UCHAR qbuf[256];
            PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION kpi = (PKEY_VALUE_PARTIAL_INFORMATION)qbuf;
            ULONG qlen = 0;
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"LastConfig");
            st = ZwQueryValueKey(hKey, &vname, KeyValuePartialInformation,
                                 kpi, sizeof(qbuf), &qlen);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(st) && kpi->Type == REG_SZ &&
                kpi->DataLength <= sizeof(oldLastConfig) - sizeof(WCHAR)) {
                RtlCopyMemory(oldLastConfig, kpi->Data, kpi->DataLength);
            }
        }
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"LastConfig");
        ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, guidBrace,
                      (ULONG)((wcslen(guidBrace) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
        ZwClose(hKey);
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] HardwareConfig\\LastConfig -> %S (was %S)\n",
                 guidBrace, oldLastConfig);
        /* Subkey deletion removed: deleting the active {UUID} subkey while
         * PnP Manager holds references to it causes CRITICAL_PROCESS_DIED.
         * LastConfig value change alone is sufficient to spoof the UUID
         * that EAC reads; subkeys contain per-device-class data EAC ignores. */
    }

    /* Office ClientTelemetry MotherboardUUID — HKU\.DEFAULT and S-1-5-18 */
    {
        static const LPCWSTR officeKeys[] = {
            L"\\Registry\\User\\.DEFAULT\\Software\\Microsoft\\Office\\Common\\ClientTelemetry",
            L"\\Registry\\User\\S-1-5-18\\Software\\Microsoft\\Office\\Common\\ClientTelemetry",
            NULL
        };
        int k;
        for (k = 0; officeKeys[k]; k++) {
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, officeKeys[k]);
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_SET_VALUE, &oa))) {
                RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, L"MotherboardUUID");
                ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &vname, 0, REG_SZ, guidPlain,
                              (ULONG)((wcslen(guidPlain) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
                ZwClose(hKey);
            }
        }
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Motherboard UUID registry keys patched\n");
}
```

`spoofer/kernel/driver_volume.c`:

```c
#include "driver.h"

NTSTATUS SpoofVolumeSerial(PWCHAR volumePath, ULONG newSerial) {
    NTSTATUS status;
    UNICODE_STRING deviceName;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES objAttrs;
    IO_STATUS_BLOCK ioStatus;
    HANDLE hVolume;
    UCHAR bootSector[512];
    LARGE_INTEGER offset;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&deviceName, volumePath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&objAttrs, &deviceName,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);

    status = ZwOpenFile(&hVolume, GENERIC_READ | GENERIC_WRITE, &objAttrs, &ioStatus,
                        FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;

    offset.QuadPart = 0;
    status = ZwReadFile(hVolume, NULL, NULL, NULL, &ioStatus,
                        bootSector, 512, &offset, NULL);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofVolumeSerial: ZwReadFile failed: 0x%08X\n", status);
        ZwClose(hVolume);
        return status;
    }

    /* Validate NTFS signature at offset 3 — writing offset 0x48 on a
     * FAT32 or exFAT volume writes into an unrelated field and corrupts it. */
    if (bootSector[3] != 'N' || bootSector[4] != 'T' ||
        bootSector[5] != 'F' || bootSector[6] != 'S') {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] SpoofVolumeSerial: not NTFS (sig='%c%c%c%c') — skipping\n",
                 bootSector[3], bootSector[4], bootSector[5], bootSector[6]);
        ZwClose(hVolume);
        return STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_REQUEST;
    }

    {
        ULONG origSerial = *(PULONG)(bootSector + 0x48);
        *(PULONG)(bootSector + 0x48) = newSerial;

        status = ZwWriteFile(hVolume, NULL, NULL, NULL, &ioStatus,
                             bootSector, 512, &offset, NULL);
        if (NT_SUCCESS(status))
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] VolumeSerial %ls: %08X -> %08X\n", volumePath, origSerial, newSerial);
        else
            SPOOF_LOG(
                "[Spoofer] VolumeSerial %ls: ZwWriteFile failed 0x%08X (was %08X)\n",
                volumePath, status, origSerial);
    }

    ZwClose(hVolume);
    return status;
}

/* ============================================================
 * MONITOR EDID SPOOFING — registry patch
 * ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS SpoofMonitorEDID(PWCHAR monitorPath, PUCHAR newEDID) {
    NTSTATUS status;
    UNICODE_STRING regPath;
    WCHAR fullPath[512];
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES objAttrs;
    HANDLE hKey;
    UNICODE_STRING valueName;

    RtlStringCbPrintfW(fullPath, sizeof(fullPath),
        L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\DISPLAY\\%s\\Device Parameters",
        monitorPath);

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, fullPath);
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&objAttrs, &regPath,
                               OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);

    status = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_SET_VALUE, &objAttrs);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&valueName, L"EDID");
    status = ZwSetValueKey(hKey, &valueName, 0, REG_BINARY, newEDID, 128);
    ZwClose(hKey);
    if (NT_SUCCESS(status))
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] SpoofMonitorEDID: EDID written for %ls\n", monitorPath);
    else
        SPOOF_LOG(
            "[Spoofer] SpoofMonitorEDID: ZwSetValueKey failed for %ls: 0x%08X\n",
            monitorPath, status);
    return status;
}


VOID CleanVolumeGuidRegistry(VOID) {
    UNICODE_STRING regPath;
    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
    HANDLE hKey;
    NTSTATUS st;
    ULONG  idx = 0;
    UCHAR  valBuf[512];

    RtlInitUnicodeString(&regPath, L"\\Registry\\Machine\\SYSTEM\\MountedDevices");
    InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &regPath, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
    st = ZwOpenKey(&hKey, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE | KEY_ENUMERATE_SUB_KEYS, &oa);
    if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] MountedDevices not accessible: 0x%08X\n", st);
        return;
    }
    for (;;) {
        ULONG dataLen = sizeof(valBuf);
        PKEY_VALUE_FULL_INFORMATION info = (PKEY_VALUE_FULL_INFORMATION)valBuf;
        ULONG nameLen;
        WCHAR name[128];
        UNICODE_STRING vname;

        st = ZwEnumerateValueKey(hKey, idx, KeyValueFullInformation,
                                  (PVOID)valBuf, sizeof(valBuf), &dataLen);
        if (st == STATUS_NO_MORE_ENTRIES) break;
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { idx++; continue; }

        nameLen = info->NameLength / sizeof(WCHAR);
        if (nameLen == 0 || nameLen >= ARRAYSIZE(name)) { idx++; continue; }
        RtlCopyMemory(name, info->Name, info->NameLength);
        name[nameLen] = L'\0';

        /* Delete \??\Volume{...} entries; skip \DosDevices\* */
        if (nameLen > 11 &&
            name[0]==L'\\'&&name[1]==L'?'&&name[2]==L'?'&&name[3]==L'\\' &&
            (name[4]==L'V'||name[4]==L'v')) {
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&vname, name);
            ZwDeleteValueKey(hKey, &vname);
            SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Deleted MountedDevices: %S\n", name);
            /* Don't advance idx — deletion shifts remaining entries */
            continue;
        }
        idx++;
    }
    ZwClose(hKey);
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] MountedDevices volume GUIDs cleaned\n");
}

/* ============================================================
 * USN JOURNAL RESET
 * For each drive C: through Z:, query the USN journal ID then
 * delete and recreate it — the new journal gets a fresh random ID.
 * ============================================================ */

VOID ResetUsnJournals(VOID) {
    WCHAR  volPath[8]; /* \??\X: */
    WCHAR  letter;
    RtlCopyMemory(volPath, L"\\??\\C:", 7 * sizeof(WCHAR));

    for (letter = L'C'; letter <= L'Z'; letter++) {
        UNICODE_STRING volName;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;
        IO_STATUS_BLOCK iosb;
        HANDLE hVol;
        NTSTATUS st;

        volPath[4] = letter;
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&volName, volPath);
        InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &volName, OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
        st = ZwOpenFile(&hVol, FILE_READ_DATA | FILE_WRITE_DATA | SYNCHRONIZE,
                        &oa, &iosb,
                        FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
                        FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) continue;

        {
            SPOOF_USN_JOURNAL_DATA jd = {0};
            st = ZwFsControlFile(hVol, NULL, NULL, NULL, &iosb,
                                 FSCTL_QUERY_USN_JOURNAL_CODE,
                                 NULL, 0, &jd, sizeof(jd));
            if (NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
                SPOOF_DELETE_USN del = {0};
                SPOOF_CREATE_USN cre = {0};

                del.UsnJournalID = jd.UsnJournalID;
                del.Flags = 0x1; /* USN_DELETE_FLAG_DELETE */
                ZwFsControlFile(hVol, NULL, NULL, NULL, &iosb,
                                FSCTL_DELETE_USN_JOURNAL_CODE,
                                &del, sizeof(del), NULL, 0);

                cre.MaximumSize    = jd.MaximumSize    ? jd.MaximumSize    : (32ULL*1024*1024);
                cre.AllocationDelta = jd.AllocationDelta ? jd.AllocationDelta : (4ULL*1024*1024);
                ZwFsControlFile(hVol, NULL, NULL, NULL, &iosb,
                                FSCTL_CREATE_USN_JOURNAL_CODE,
                                &cre, sizeof(cre), NULL, 0);

                SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] USN journal reset on volume %c:\n", (char)letter);
            }
        }
        ZwClose(hVol);
    }
}

/* ============================================================ */

NTSTATUS VolumeSerialCompletion(PDEVICE_OBJECT DevObj, PIRP Irp, PVOID Context) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)Context;
    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(DevObj);

    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL) {
        if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
        return STATUS_SUCCESS;
    }
    if (NT_SUCCESS(Irp->IoStatus.Status) &&
        Irp->IoStatus.Information >= (ULONG_PTR)(FIELD_OFFSET(FILE_FS_VOLUME_INFORMATION, VolumeLabelLength))) {
        PFILE_FS_VOLUME_INFORMATION vi = NULL;
        if (Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer)
            vi = (PFILE_FS_VOLUME_INFORMATION)Irp->AssociatedIrp.SystemBuffer;
        else if (Irp->MdlAddress)
            vi = (PFILE_FS_VOLUME_INFORMATION)
                     MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(Irp->MdlAddress, NormalPagePriority);
        if (vi && MmIsAddressValid(vi)) {
            __try { vi->VolumeSerialNumber = ext->SpoofedVolumeSerial; }
            __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {}
        }
    }
    if (Irp->PendingReturned) IoMarkIrpPending(Irp);
    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
}

/* Dispatch entry for FILTER_TYPE_VOLUME IRP_MJ_QUERY_VOLUME_INFORMATION */
NTSTATUS VolumeQueryVolumeDispatch(PDEVICE_OBJECT DeviceObject, PIRP Irp) {
    PFILTER_EXTENSION  ext   = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)DeviceObject->DeviceExtension;
    PIO_STACK_LOCATION stack = IoGetCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);

    if (!ext || !ext->LowerDevice) {
        Irp->IoStatus.Status    = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
        Irp->IoStatus.Information = 0;
        IoCompleteRequest(Irp, IO_NO_INCREMENT);
        return STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED;
    }

    if (stack->Parameters.QueryVolume.FsInformationClass == FileFsVolumeInformation &&
        ext->SpoofedVolumeSerial != 0) {
        IoCopyCurrentIrpStackLocationToNext(Irp);
        IoSetCompletionRoutine(Irp, VolumeSerialCompletion, ext, TRUE, TRUE, TRUE);
        return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
    }
    IoSkipCurrentIrpStackLocation(Irp);
    return IoCallDriver(ext->LowerDevice, Irp);
}

VOID AutoSpoofVolumeSerials(VOID) {
    WCHAR volPath[] = L"\\DosDevices\\C:";
    WCHAR letter;
    ULONG patchedCount = 0;

    for (letter = L'C'; letter <= L'Z'; letter++) {
        UNICODE_STRING   volName;
        PFILE_OBJECT     fileObj  = NULL;
        PDEVICE_OBJECT   topDev   = NULL;
        NTSTATUS         st;
        PDEVICE_OBJECT   filterDev;
        PFILTER_EXTENSION ext;
        UCHAR            bootSector[512];
        KIRQL            irql;
        ULONG            newSerial;
        IO_STATUS_BLOCK  iosb;
        LARGE_INTEGER    offset;
        HANDLE           hVol;
        OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES oa;

        volPath[12] = letter;
        RtlInitUnicodeString(&volName, volPath);
        st = IoGetDeviceObjectPointer(&volName, FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES, &fileObj, &topDev);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) continue;

        /* Idempotency guard: if the top of the stack already carries our FILTER_MAGIC
         * this volume was attached on a previous call.  A second attach creates a second
         * filter device above the first, making two completion routines fire on every
         * FileFsVolumeInformation IRP.  IRPs allocated with the old (shorter) StackSize
         * are then routed through an extra stack slot they were never sized for, writing
         * past the end of the IRP's stack array into adjacent pool — corrupting KTHREADs
         * and triggering KiRestoreSetContextState null-deref (BSOD 0x3B) in svchost. */
        {
            PFILTER_EXTENSION topExt = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)topDev->DeviceExtension;
            if (topExt && MmIsAddressValid(topExt) &&
                topExt->Magic == FILTER_MAGIC && topExt->Type == FILTER_TYPE_VOLUME) {
                ObDereferenceObject(fileObj);
                continue;
            }
        }

        /* Attach our filter device above the current top of the volume device stack */
        st = IoCreateDevice(g_SpoofDeviceObject->DriverObject, sizeof(FILTER_EXTENSION),
                            NULL, topDev->DeviceType, 0, FALSE, &filterDev);
        if (!NT_SUCCESS(st)) { ObDereferenceObject(fileObj); continue; }

        filterDev->Flags |= topDev->Flags & (DO_BUFFERED_IO | DO_DIRECT_IO);
        filterDev->Characteristics |= topDev->Characteristics;
        filterDev->AlignmentRequirement = topDev->AlignmentRequirement;
        filterDev->Flags &= ~DO_DEVICE_INITIALIZING;

        ext = (PFILTER_EXTENSION)filterDev->DeviceExtension;
        ext->Magic       = FILTER_MAGIC;
        ext->Type        = FILTER_TYPE_VOLUME;
        ext->LowerDevice = IoAttachDeviceToDeviceStack(filterDev, topDev);
        if (!ext->LowerDevice) {
            IoDeleteDevice(filterDev);
            ObDereferenceObject(fileObj);
            continue;
        }

        /* Generate random serial for this drive */
        KeAcquireSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, &irql);
        newSerial = KernelRand(&g_SpoofSeed);
        KeReleaseSpinLock(&g_SpoofLock, irql);
        if (newSerial == 0) newSerial = 0x12345678; /* avoid 0 sentinel */
        ext->SpoofedVolumeSerial = newSerial;

        /* Patch the on-disk boot sector (takes effect on next mount) */
        {
            WCHAR rawPath[] = L"\\??\\C:";
            UNICODE_STRING rawName;
            rawPath[4] = letter;
            RtlInitUnicodeString(&rawName, rawPath);
            InitializeObjectAttributes(&oa, &rawName,
                OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE | OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE, NULL, NULL);
            st = ZwOpenFile(&hVol, GENERIC_READ | GENERIC_WRITE, &oa, &iosb,
                            FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT);
            if (NT_SUCCESS(st)) {
                offset.QuadPart = 0;
                if (NT_SUCCESS(ZwReadFile(hVol, NULL, NULL, NULL, &iosb,
                                          bootSector, 512, &offset, NULL)) &&
                    bootSector[3] == 'N' && bootSector[4] == 'T' &&
                    bootSector[5] == 'F' && bootSector[6] == 'S') {
                    *(PULONG)(bootSector + 0x48) = newSerial;
                    ZwWriteFile(hVol, NULL, NULL, NULL, &iosb,
                                bootSector, 512, &offset, NULL);
                }
                ZwClose(hVol);
            }
        }

        ObDereferenceObject(fileObj);
        patchedCount++;
        SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] Volume %c: serial -> 0x%08X\n", (char)letter, newSerial);
    }
    SPOOF_LOG("[Spoofer] AutoSpoofVolumeSerials: %u volume(s) patched\n", patchedCount);
}
```

`spoofer/usermode/artifact_clean.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "artifact_clean.h"
#include "driver_comms.h"
#include <shlobj.h>
#include <sddl.h>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#pragma comment(lib, "advapi32.lib")

static const WCHAR* g_CheatKeywords[] = {
    L"kdmapper", L"kainite", L"spoofer", L"injector", L"mapper",
    L"scylla", L"x64dbg", L"windbg", L"cheatengine", L"extreme",
    L"eac_bypass", L"themida", L"stugloader", L"windowshook",
    L"manual_map", L"bypass", L"privacydriver", L"privacy.exe",
    L"cheat", L"hack", L"loader", L"hook"
};
static const int g_CheatKwCount = 22;

BOOL ContainsCheatKeyword(const WCHAR* str) {
    WCHAR lower[1024];
    wcsncpy_s(lower, 1024, str, _TRUNCATE);
    _wcslwr_s(lower, 1024);
    for (int k = 0; k < g_CheatKwCount; k++)
        if (wcsstr(lower, g_CheatKeywords[k])) return TRUE;
    return FALSE;
}

static int DeleteDirectoryRecursive(const WCHAR* dirPath) {
    WCHAR pattern[MAX_PATH];
    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd;
    int count = 0;

    wsprintfW(pattern, L"%s\\*", dirPath);
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileW(pattern, &fd);
    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        RemoveDirectoryW(dirPath);
        return 0;
    }
    do {
        if (wcscmp(fd.cFileName, L".") == 0 || wcscmp(fd.cFileName, L"..") == 0) continue;
        WCHAR full[MAX_PATH];
        wsprintfW(full, L"%s\\%s", dirPath, fd.cFileName);
        if (fd.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
            count += DeleteDirectoryRecursive(full);
        } else {
            SetFileAttributesW(full, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL);
            if (DeleteFileW(full)) count++;
            else LOG_WARN("Could not delete %ls (GLE=%lu)", full, GetLastError());
        }
    } while (FindNextFileW(hFind, &fd));
    FindClose(hFind);
    RemoveDirectoryW(dirPath);
    return count;
}

void ClearEACBanCache(void) {
    WCHAR path[MAX_PATH];
    int totalDeleted = 0;
    if (SHGetFolderPathW(NULL, CSIDL_COMMON_APPDATA, NULL, 0, path) == S_OK) {
        wcscat_s(path, MAX_PATH, L"\\EasyAntiCheat");

        WCHAR searchPath[MAX_PATH];
        wsprintfW(searchPath, L"%s\\*", path);

        WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd;
        HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileW(searchPath, &fd);
        if (hFind != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
            do {
                if (wcscmp(fd.cFileName, L".") == 0 || wcscmp(fd.cFileName, L"..") == 0) continue;
                WCHAR filePath[MAX_PATH];
                wsprintfW(filePath, L"%s\\%s", path, fd.cFileName);
                if (fd.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
                    totalDeleted += DeleteDirectoryRecursive(filePath);
                } else {
                    SetFileAttributesW(filePath, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL);
                    if (DeleteFileW(filePath)) totalDeleted++;
                }
            } while (FindNextFileW(hFind, &fd));
            FindClose(hFind);
        }
        LOG_OK("EAC ban cache cleared: %ls (%d files)", path, totalDeleted);
    }

    const WCHAR* eacEosDir = L"C:\\Program Files (x86)\\EasyAntiCheat_EOS";
    WCHAR eacSearch[MAX_PATH];
    wsprintfW(eacSearch, L"%s\\*", eacEosDir);
    int eosDeleted = 0;

    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd2;
    HANDLE hFind2 = FindFirstFileW(eacSearch, &fd2);
    if (hFind2 != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        do {
            if (wcscmp(fd2.cFileName, L".") == 0 || wcscmp(fd2.cFileName, L"..") == 0) continue;
            WCHAR* dotExt = wcsrchr(fd2.cFileName, L'.');
            if (dotExt && (wcscmp(dotExt, L".sys") == 0 || wcscmp(dotExt, L".exe") == 0 ||
                           wcscmp(dotExt, L".bak") == 0)) continue;
            WCHAR filePath[MAX_PATH];
            wsprintfW(filePath, L"%s\\%s", eacEosDir, fd2.cFileName);
            SetFileAttributesW(filePath, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL);
            if (fd2.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY)
                eosDeleted += DeleteDirectoryRecursive(filePath);
            else if (DeleteFileW(filePath))
                eosDeleted++;
        } while (FindNextFileW(hFind2, &fd2));
        FindClose(hFind2);
        if (eosDeleted)
            LOG_OK("EAC EOS cache cleared: %d files", eosDeleted);
    }
}

void CleanWifiProfiles(void) {
    system("netsh wlan delete profile name=* >nul 2>&1");
    LOG_OK("WiFi profiles cleared");
}

void ClearClipboard(void) {
    if (OpenClipboard(NULL)) {
        EmptyClipboard();
        CloseClipboard();
        LOG_OK("Clipboard cleared");
    }
}

void CleanBAMRegistry(void) {
    HANDLE hToken;
    if (!OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_QUERY, &hToken)) return;
    DWORD needed = 0;
    GetTokenInformation(hToken, TokenUser, NULL, 0, &needed);
    TOKEN_USER* tu = (TOKEN_USER*)malloc(needed);
    if (!tu) { CloseHandle(hToken); return; }
    GetTokenInformation(hToken, TokenUser, tu, needed, &needed);
    CloseHandle(hToken);
    LPWSTR sidStr = NULL;
    ConvertSidToStringSidW(tu->User.Sid, &sidStr);
    free(tu);
    if (!sidStr) return;

    WCHAR bamPath[320];
    swprintf_s(bamPath, 320,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Services\\bam\\State\\UserSettings\\%s", sidStr);
    LocalFree(sidStr);

    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, bamPath, 0, KEY_READ | KEY_SET_VALUE, &hKey) != ERROR_SUCCESS) return;

    WCHAR* toDelete[512]; int tdc = 0;
    WCHAR vName[512]; DWORD vnLen = 512, idx = 0;
    while (RegEnumValueW(hKey, idx++, vName, &vnLen, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        vnLen = 512;
        if (ContainsCheatKeyword(vName) && tdc < 511) {
            toDelete[tdc] = _wcsdup(vName);
            if (toDelete[tdc]) tdc++;
        }
    }
    int cleaned = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < tdc; i++) {
        if (RegDeleteValueW(hKey, toDelete[i]) == ERROR_SUCCESS) cleaned++;
        free(toDelete[i]);
    }
    RegCloseKey(hKey);
    if (cleaned) LOG_OK("BAM: deleted %d entries", cleaned);
    else         LOG_INFO("BAM: no suspicious entries");
}

void CleanMUICache(void) {
    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Classes\\Local Settings\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\Shell\\MuiCache",
        0, KEY_READ | KEY_SET_VALUE, &hKey) != ERROR_SUCCESS) return;
    WCHAR* toDelete[1024]; int tdc = 0;
    WCHAR vName[1024]; DWORD vnLen = 1024, idx = 0;
    while (RegEnumValueW(hKey, idx++, vName, &vnLen, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        vnLen = 1024;
        if (ContainsCheatKeyword(vName) && tdc < 1023) {
            toDelete[tdc] = _wcsdup(vName);
            if (toDelete[tdc]) tdc++;
        }
    }
    int cleaned = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < tdc; i++) {
        if (RegDeleteValueW(hKey, toDelete[i]) == ERROR_SUCCESS) cleaned++;
        free(toDelete[i]);
    }
    RegCloseKey(hKey);
    if (cleaned) LOG_OK("MUI Cache: deleted %d entries", cleaned);
    else         LOG_INFO("MUI Cache: clean");
}

void CleanEACSessionLogs(void) {
    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileW(L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\eac_usermode_*.dll", &fd);
    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) { LOG_INFO("EAC session logs: none found"); return; }
    int count = 0;
    do {
        WCHAR path[MAX_PATH];
        swprintf_s(path, MAX_PATH, L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\%s", fd.cFileName);
        SetFileAttributesW(path, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL);
        if (DeleteFileW(path)) count++;
    } while (FindNextFileW(hFind, &fd));
    FindClose(hFind);
    LOG_OK("EAC session logs: deleted %d files", count);
}

void CleanPrefetchFiles(void) {
    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileW(L"C:\\Windows\\Prefetch\\*.pf", &fd);
    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) { LOG_INFO("Prefetch: not accessible"); return; }
    int count = 0;
    do {
        if (ContainsCheatKeyword(fd.cFileName)) {
            WCHAR path[MAX_PATH];
            swprintf_s(path, MAX_PATH, L"C:\\Windows\\Prefetch\\%s", fd.cFileName);
            if (DeleteFileW(path)) count++;
        }
    } while (FindNextFileW(hFind, &fd));
    FindClose(hFind);
    if (count) LOG_OK("Prefetch: deleted %d entries", count);
    else       LOG_INFO("Prefetch: clean");
}

void CleanRecentItems(void) {
    WCHAR recentPath[MAX_PATH];
    if (SHGetFolderPathW(NULL, CSIDL_RECENT, NULL, 0, recentPath) != S_OK) return;
    WCHAR searchPath[MAX_PATH];
    swprintf_s(searchPath, MAX_PATH, L"%s\\*.lnk", recentPath);
    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileW(searchPath, &fd);
    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    int count = 0;
    do {
        if (ContainsCheatKeyword(fd.cFileName)) {
            WCHAR path[MAX_PATH];
            swprintf_s(path, MAX_PATH, L"%s\\%s", recentPath, fd.cFileName);
            if (DeleteFileW(path)) count++;
        }
    } while (FindNextFileW(hFind, &fd));
    FindClose(hFind);
    if (count) LOG_OK("Recent Items: deleted %d shortcuts", count);
    else       LOG_INFO("Recent Items: clean");
}

void CleanAmcacheEntries(void) {
    system("sc stop AppEXSvc >nul 2>&1");
    Sleep(2000);

    HANDLE hToken;
    if (OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_ADJUST_PRIVILEGES | TOKEN_QUERY, &hToken)) {
        TOKEN_PRIVILEGES tp = {1};
        LookupPrivilegeValueW(NULL, SE_BACKUP_NAME, &tp.Privileges[0].Luid);
        tp.Privileges[0].Attributes = SE_PRIVILEGE_ENABLED;
        AdjustTokenPrivileges(hToken, FALSE, &tp, sizeof(tp), NULL, NULL);
        LookupPrivilegeValueW(NULL, SE_RESTORE_NAME, &tp.Privileges[0].Luid);
        AdjustTokenPrivileges(hToken, FALSE, &tp, sizeof(tp), NULL, NULL);
        CloseHandle(hToken);
    }

    if (RegLoadKeyW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, L"PrivacyAmcTemp",
                    L"C:\\Windows\\AppCompat\\Programs\\Amcache.hve") != ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_WARN("Amcache: could not load hive");
        system("sc start AppEXSvc >nul 2>&1");
        return;
    }

    HKEY hInv;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, L"PrivacyAmcTemp\\Root\\InventoryApplicationFile",
                      0, KEY_READ | KEY_WRITE, &hInv) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        WCHAR kn[512]; DWORD idx2 = 0;
        WCHAR* toDelete[512]; int tdc = 0;
        while (RegEnumKeyW(hInv, idx2++, kn, 512) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            HKEY hE;
            if (RegOpenKeyExW(hInv, kn, 0, KEY_READ, &hE) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                WCHAR path[512] = {0}; DWORD ps = sizeof(path);
                RegQueryValueExW(hE, L"LowerCaseLongPath", NULL, NULL, (BYTE*)path, &ps);
                RegCloseKey(hE);
                if (ContainsCheatKeyword(path) && tdc < 511) {
                    toDelete[tdc] = _wcsdup(kn);
                    if (toDelete[tdc]) tdc++;
                }
            }
        }
        int cleaned = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < tdc; i++) {
            if (RegDeleteKeyW(hInv, toDelete[i]) == ERROR_SUCCESS) cleaned++;
            free(toDelete[i]);
        }
        RegCloseKey(hInv);
        if (cleaned) LOG_OK("Amcache: deleted %d entries", cleaned);
        else         LOG_INFO("Amcache: clean");
    }
    RegUnLoadKeyW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, L"PrivacyAmcTemp");
    system("sc start AppEXSvc >nul 2>&1");
}

void CleanShimCache(void) {
    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Session Manager\\AppCompatCache",
        0, KEY_SET_VALUE, &hKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        BYTE empty[16] = {0};
        if (RegSetValueExW(hKey, L"AppCompatCache", 0, REG_BINARY, empty, sizeof(empty)) == ERROR_SUCCESS)
            LOG_OK("ShimCache cleared");
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
    }
}

void CleanWindowsTimeline(void) {
    system("net stop CDPUserSvc_* >nul 2>&1");
    system("sc stop CDPUserSvc >nul 2>&1");
    Sleep(1500);

    WCHAR localAppData[MAX_PATH];
    if (SHGetFolderPathW(NULL, CSIDL_LOCAL_APPDATA, NULL, 0, localAppData) != S_OK) {
        system("net start CDPUserSvc >nul 2>&1");
        return;
    }
    WCHAR cdpGlob[MAX_PATH];
    swprintf_s(cdpGlob, MAX_PATH, L"%s\\ConnectedDevicesPlatform\\*", localAppData);
    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd2;
    HANDLE hFind2 = FindFirstFileW(cdpGlob, &fd2);
    int count = 0;
    if (hFind2 != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        do {
            if (wcscmp(fd2.cFileName, L".") == 0 || wcscmp(fd2.cFileName, L"..") == 0) continue;
            if (!(fd2.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY)) continue;
            WCHAR dbPath[MAX_PATH];
            swprintf_s(dbPath, MAX_PATH, L"%s\\ConnectedDevicesPlatform\\%s\\ActivitiesCache.db",
                       localAppData, fd2.cFileName);
            SetFileAttributesW(dbPath, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL);
            if (DeleteFileW(dbPath)) { count++; continue; }
            HANDLE hF = CreateFileW(dbPath, GENERIC_WRITE, 0, NULL, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, NULL);
            if (hF != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) { SetEndOfFile(hF); CloseHandle(hF); count++; }
        } while (FindNextFileW(hFind2, &fd2));
        FindClose(hFind2);
    }
    system("net start CDPUserSvc >nul 2>&1");
    if (count) LOG_OK("Windows Timeline: cleared %d ActivitiesCache.db", count);
    else       LOG_INFO("Windows Timeline: clean");
}
```
