```cpp
    BOOL ok = DeleteFileW(L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\sru\\SRUDB.dat");
    system("net start SRUMon >nul 2>&1");

    else    LOG_WARN("SRUM: could not delete SRUDB.dat");
}

void CleanPSCommandHistory(void) {
    WCHAR appData[MAX_PATH];
    if (SHGetFolderPathW(NULL, CSIDL_APPDATA, NULL, 0, appData) != S_OK) return;
    WCHAR histPath[MAX_PATH];
    swprintf_s(histPath, MAX_PATH,
        L"%s\\Microsoft\\Windows\\PowerShell\\PSReadLine\\ConsoleHost_history.txt", appData);
    HANDLE hF = CreateFileW(histPath, GENERIC_WRITE, 0, NULL, TRUNCATE_EXISTING, 0, NULL);
    if (hF != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) { CloseHandle(hF); LOG_OK("PowerShell history cleared"); }
    else LOG_INFO("PowerShell history: not found");
}

void CleanNetworkProfileHistory(void) {
    HKEY hSig;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion\\NetworkList\\Signatures\\Unmanaged",
        0, KEY_READ | KEY_WRITE, &hSig) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        WCHAR kn[256]; DWORD idx3 = 0;
        WCHAR* toDelete[256]; int tdc = 0;
        while (RegEnumKeyW(hSig, idx3++, kn, 256) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            HKEY hE;
            if (RegOpenKeyExW(hSig, kn, 0, KEY_READ, &hE) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                BYTE mac[6] = {0}; DWORD ms = 6;
                RegQueryValueExW(hE, L"DefaultGatewayMac", NULL, NULL, mac, &ms);
                RegCloseKey(hE);
                BOOL nz = FALSE;
                for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) if (mac[i]) { nz = TRUE; break; }
                if (nz && tdc < 255) { toDelete[tdc] = _wcsdup(kn); if (toDelete[tdc]) tdc++; }
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < tdc; i++) { RegDeleteKeyW(hSig, toDelete[i]); free(toDelete[i]); }
        RegCloseKey(hSig);
        if (tdc) LOG_OK("Network: deleted %d gateway MAC signatures", tdc);
    }

    HKEY hProf;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion\\NetworkList\\Profiles",
        0, KEY_READ | KEY_WRITE, &hProf) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        WCHAR pk[256]; DWORD idx4 = 0;
        WCHAR* toDeleteP[256]; int tdcP = 0;
        while (RegEnumKeyW(hProf, idx4++, pk, 256) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            HKEY hP;
            if (RegOpenKeyExW(hProf, pk, 0, KEY_READ | KEY_SET_VALUE, &hP) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                WCHAR name[256] = {0}; DWORD nsz = sizeof(name);
                RegQueryValueExW(hP, L"ProfileName", NULL, NULL, (BYTE*)name, &nsz);
                _wcslwr_s(name, 256);
                BOOL isVPN = (wcsstr(name, L"warp") || wcsstr(name, L"cloudflare") ||
                              wcsstr(name, L"vpn")  || wcsstr(name, L"tunnel") ||
                              wcsstr(name, L"nordvpn") || wcsstr(name, L"expressvpn") ||
                              wcsstr(name, L"proton") || wcsstr(name, L"wireguard"));
                if (isVPN && tdcP < 255) {
                    toDeleteP[tdcP] = _wcsdup(pk);
                    if (toDeleteP[tdcP]) tdcP++;
                } else {
                    BYTE z[16] = {0};
                    RegSetValueExW(hP, L"DateLastConnected", 0, REG_BINARY, z, 16);
                    RegSetValueExW(hP, L"DateCreated",       0, REG_BINARY, z, 16);
                }
                RegCloseKey(hP);
            }
        }
        for (int k = 0; k < tdcP; k++) { RegDeleteKeyW(hProf, toDeleteP[k]); free(toDeleteP[k]); }
        RegCloseKey(hProf);
        LOG_OK("Network: profile timestamps zeroed; %d VPN profiles deleted", tdcP);
    }
}

void CleanWANMiniportArtifacts(void) {
    HKEY hClass;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class\\{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}",
        0, KEY_READ, &hClass) != ERROR_SUCCESS) return;
    WCHAR sub[16]; DWORD idx5 = 0; int cleaned = 0;
    while (RegEnumKeyW(hClass, idx5++, sub, 16) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        if (wcslen(sub) != 4) continue;
        WCHAR fullPath[256];
        swprintf_s(fullPath, 256,
            L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class\\{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}\\%s", sub);
        HKEY hDev;
        if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, fullPath, 0, KEY_READ | KEY_SET_VALUE, &hDev) != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;
        WCHAR compId[128] = {0}; DWORD cs = sizeof(compId);
        RegQueryValueExW(hDev, L"ComponentId", NULL, NULL, (BYTE*)compId, &cs);
        WCHAR lower[128];
        wcsncpy_s(lower, 128, compId, _TRUNCATE);
        _wcslwr_s(lower, 128);
        if (wcsncmp(lower, L"ms_", 3) == 0 || wcsstr(lower, L"pppoe") ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"l2tp") || wcsstr(lower, L"pptp") || wcsstr(lower, L"mupe") ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"ndisuio") || wcsstr(lower, L"wifidirect") || wcsstr(lower, L"sstp")) {
            if (RegDeleteValueW(hDev, L"NetworkAddress") == ERROR_SUCCESS) cleaned++;
        }
        RegCloseKey(hDev);
    }
    RegCloseKey(hClass);
    if (cleaned) LOG_OK("WAN miniport: removed NetworkAddress from %d virtual adapters", cleaned);
    else         LOG_INFO("WAN miniport: clean");
}

void CleanEACBootstrapperLogs(void) {
    WCHAR path[MAX_PATH];
    WCHAR pattern[MAX_PATH];
    WIN32_FIND_DATAW fd;
    int cnt = 0;
    if (!SHGetSpecialFolderPathW(NULL, path, CSIDL_APPDATA, FALSE)) return;
    swprintf_s(pattern, MAX_PATH, L"%s\\EasyAntiCheat\\prod-fn\\*", path);
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileW(pattern, &fd);
    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    do {
        if (fd.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) continue;
        WCHAR full[MAX_PATH];
        swprintf_s(full, MAX_PATH, L"%s\\EasyAntiCheat\\prod-fn\\%s", path, fd.cFileName);
        SetFileAttributesW(full, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL);
        if (DeleteFileW(full)) cnt++;
    } while (FindNextFileW(hFind, &fd));
    FindClose(hFind);
    if (cnt) LOG_OK("EAC bootstrapper logs: deleted %d files", cnt);
}

void CleanVolumeShadowCopies(void) {
    int r = system("vssadmin delete shadows /all /quiet >nul 2>&1");
    if (r == 0) LOG_OK("VSS shadow copies deleted");
    else        LOG_INFO("VSS: vssadmin returned %d", r);
}

static void DeleteKeyRecursive(HKEY hRoot, const WCHAR* subKey) {
    HKEY h;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(hRoot, subKey, 0, KEY_READ | KEY_WRITE, &h) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        WCHAR child[128];
        while (RegEnumKeyW(h, 0, child, 128) == ERROR_SUCCESS)
            DeleteKeyRecursive(h, child);
        RegCloseKey(h);
    }
    RegDeleteKeyW(hRoot, subKey);
}

void CleanAudioEndpointGUIDs(void) {
    static const WCHAR* audioKeys[] = {
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\MMDevices\\Audio\\Render",
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\MMDevices\\Audio\\Capture",
    };
    int cnt = 0;
    system("net stop AudioSrv /y >nul 2>&1");
    Sleep(400);
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        HKEY hRoot;
        if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, audioKeys[i], 0, KEY_READ | KEY_WRITE, &hRoot) != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;
        WCHAR sub[128]; DWORD idx6 = 0;
        WCHAR* toDeleteA[128]; int tdcA = 0;
        while (RegEnumKeyW(hRoot, idx6++, sub, 128) == ERROR_SUCCESS && tdcA < 127) {
            toDeleteA[tdcA] = _wcsdup(sub);
            if (toDeleteA[tdcA]) tdcA++;
        }
        for (int j = 0; j < tdcA; j++) {
            DeleteKeyRecursive(hRoot, toDeleteA[j]);
            cnt++;
            free(toDeleteA[j]);
        }
        RegCloseKey(hRoot);
    }
    system("net start AudioEndpointBuilder >nul 2>&1");
    system("net start AudioSrv >nul 2>&1");
    if (cnt) LOG_OK("Audio endpoint GUIDs cleared (%d entries)", cnt);
}

void CleanUSBHistoryEntries(void) {
    HKEY hEnum;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\USB",
        0, KEY_READ, &hEnum) != ERROR_SUCCESS) return;
    WCHAR vid[128]; DWORD idxU = 0; int cnt = 0;
    while (RegEnumKeyW(hEnum, idxU++, vid, 128) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        HKEY hVid;
        WCHAR vidPath[256];
        swprintf_s(vidPath, 256, L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\USB\\%s", vid);
        if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, vidPath, 0, KEY_READ, &hVid) != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;
        WCHAR ser[128]; DWORD idxS = 0;
        while (RegEnumKeyW(hVid, idxS++, ser, 128) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            WCHAR lower[128];
            wcsncpy_s(lower, 128, ser, _TRUNCATE);
            _wcslwr_s(lower, 128);
            if (ContainsCheatKeyword(lower)) {
                WCHAR fullSer[384];
                swprintf_s(fullSer, 384, L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\USB\\%s\\%s", vid, ser);
                if (RegDeleteKeyW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, fullSer) == ERROR_SUCCESS) cnt++;
            }
        }
        RegCloseKey(hVid);
    }
    RegCloseKey(hEnum);
    if (cnt) LOG_OK("USB history: removed %d suspicious entries", cnt);
}
```

`spoofer/usermode/board_ident.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "board_ident.h"
#include <string.h>

const BoardIdentity* g_PickedBoard     = NULL;
ULONG                g_PickedCPUModelIdx = 0;
char                 g_PickedBIOSVendor[64]  = "";
char                 g_PickedBIOSVersion[32] = "";
char                 g_PickedBIOSDate[32]    = "";

static const BoardIdentity g_Boards[] = {
    { "GIGABYTE", "B560M DS3H", "B560M DS3H", "LGA1200",
      "i5", {"10400", "10400F", "10500", "10600K", "11400", "11400F"}, "2.90GHz", "Corsair" },
    { "GIGABYTE", "Z490M", "Z490M", "LGA1200",
      "i7", {"10700", "10700K", "10700F", "10700KF", "11700", "11700K"}, "2.90GHz", "Kingston" },

const BoardIdentity& PickBoardIdentity(ULONG* seed) {
    return g_Boards[SpoofRand(seed) % (sizeof(g_Boards) / sizeof(g_Boards[0]))];
}

BOOL SpoofSMBIOS(ULONG* seed) {
    SPOOF_SMBIOS_REQUEST req = {0};
    const BoardIdentity& board = PickBoardIdentity(seed);
    g_PickedBoard = &board;

    GenerateRandomUUID(req.NewUUID, seed);
    GenerateRandomSerial(req.NewBoardSerial,   14, seed);
    GenerateRandomSerial(req.NewSystemSerial,  12, seed);
    GenerateRandomSerial(req.NewChassisSerial,  9, seed);

    strncpy_s(req.NewSystemManufacturer, sizeof(req.NewSystemManufacturer), board.mfr,       _TRUNCATE);
    strncpy_s(req.NewSystemProduct,      sizeof(req.NewSystemProduct),      board.sysProd,   _TRUNCATE);
    strncpy_s(req.NewBoardManufacturer,  sizeof(req.NewBoardManufacturer),  board.mfr,       _TRUNCATE);
    strncpy_s(req.NewBoardProduct,       sizeof(req.NewBoardProduct),       board.boardProd, _TRUNCATE);

    {
        static const char* amiVerGigabyte[] = { "F12", "F14", "F16", "F20", "F30" };
        static const char* amiVerAsus[]     = { "1101", "3004", "3002", "2103", "2301" };
        static const char* amiVerMSI[]      = { "1.A0", "1.B0", "A.80", "7D25v1C", "1.E0" };
        static const char* amiVerAsRock[]   = { "P4.20", "P7.10", "L3.40", "P3.60", "L4.10" };
        static const char* biosDates[]      = {
            "04/15/2022", "06/20/2021", "11/30/2020", "01/10/2023",
            "07/22/2023", "03/14/2024", "09/05/2023", "12/01/2022", "05/18/2024"
        };
        const char* bVendor = (SpoofRand(seed) % 2 == 0)
            ? "American Megatrends Inc."
            : "American Megatrends International, LLC.";
        ULONG vi = SpoofRand(seed) % 5;
        const char* bVer;
        if      (!strcmp(board.mfr, "GIGABYTE")) bVer = amiVerGigabyte[vi];
        else if (!strcmp(board.mfr, "ASUS"))     bVer = amiVerAsus[vi];
        else if (!strcmp(board.mfr, "MSI"))      bVer = amiVerMSI[vi];
        else                                     bVer = amiVerAsRock[vi];
        const char* bDate = biosDates[SpoofRand(seed) % 9];
        strncpy_s(g_PickedBIOSVendor,  sizeof(g_PickedBIOSVendor),  bVendor, _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(g_PickedBIOSVersion, sizeof(g_PickedBIOSVersion),  bVer,    _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(g_PickedBIOSDate,    sizeof(g_PickedBIOSDate),     bDate,   _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(req.NewBIOSVendor,      sizeof(req.NewBIOSVendor),      bVendor, _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(req.NewBIOSVersion,     sizeof(req.NewBIOSVersion),     bVer,    _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(req.NewBIOSReleaseDate, sizeof(req.NewBIOSReleaseDate), bDate,   _TRUNCATE);
    }

    {
        ULONG pick = SpoofRand(seed) % 6;
        g_PickedCPUModelIdx = pick;
        BOOL isAMD = (strcmp(board.socket, "AM4") == 0 || strcmp(board.socket, "AM5") == 0);
        strncpy_s(req.NewProcessorSocket,       sizeof(req.NewProcessorSocket),       board.socket, _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(req.NewProcessorManufacturer, sizeof(req.NewProcessorManufacturer),
                  isAMD ? "Advanced Micro Devices, Inc." : "Intel(R) Corporation", _TRUNCATE);
        if (isAMD) {
            _snprintf_s(req.NewProcessorVersion, sizeof(req.NewProcessorVersion), _TRUNCATE,
                        "AMD Ryzen %s %s                 ",
                        board.cpuSeries, board.cpuModels[pick]);
        } else {
            _snprintf_s(req.NewProcessorVersion, sizeof(req.NewProcessorVersion), _TRUNCATE,
                        "Intel(R) Core(TM) i%s-%s CPU @ %s",
                        board.cpuSeries, board.cpuModels[pick], board.cpuFreq);
        }

        if (strcmp(board.socket, "AM5") == 0)
            req.NewProcessorLeaf1EAX = 0x00A60F12u;
        else if (strcmp(board.socket, "AM4") == 0)
            req.NewProcessorLeaf1EAX = 0x00A50F10u;
        else if (strcmp(board.socket, "LGA1700") == 0) {
            BOOL is13th = (board.cpuModels[0][0] == '1' && board.cpuModels[0][1] == '3')
                       || (board.cpuModels[0][0] == '1' && board.cpuModels[0][1] == '4');
            req.NewProcessorLeaf1EAX = is13th ? 0x000B0671u : 0x00090672u;
        } else {
            req.NewProcessorLeaf1EAX = 0x000A0652u;
        }
    }

    {
        static const char* memMfrs[] = {
            "Corsair", "Kingston", "Samsung", "Crucial", "G.Skill",
            "TeamGroup", "SK Hynix", "Micron", "Adata", "PNY", "Patriot"
        };
        static const char* partByCorsair[]  = { "CMK16GX4M2B3200C16", "CMK32GX4M2B3200C16", "CMK16GX4M2D3600C18", "CMK32GX4M2D3600C18" };
        static const char* partByKingston[] = { "KHX3200C16D4/8GX",   "KHX3600C17D4/8GX",   "KVR32N22S6/8",       "KVR32N22D8/16"      };
        static const char* partBySamsung[]  = { "M378A1K43CB2-CTD",   "M378A2G43MX3-CTD",   "M471A1K43CB1-CTD",   "M378A1G44AB0-CWE"   };
        static const char* partByCrucial[]  = { "CT8G4DFS832A",       "CT16G4DFS832A",       "CT8G4DFRA32A",       "CT16G4DFRA32A"      };
        static const char* partByGSkill[]   = { "F4-3200C16D-16GVKB", "F4-3600C18D-32GTZN",  "F4-3200C16D-32GTZR", "F4-3600C16D-16GTZNC" };
        static const char* partByTeam[]     = { "TEAMGROUP-UD4-3200",  "TED416G3200C2201",    "TED432G3200C22DC01"  };
        static const char* partByHynix[]    = { "HMA81GU6AFR8N-UH",   "HMA82GU6AFR8N-UH",   "HMA82GS6JJR8N-VK",   "HMAA1GU6CJR6N-XN"  };
        static const char* partByMicron[]   = { "8ATF1G64HZ-3G2E1",   "18ATF2G64HZ-3G2E1",  "MTA8ATF1G64HZ-3G2R1" };
        static const char* partByAdata[]    = { "AD4U32008G22-BGN",    "AD4U320016G22-BGN",   "AX4U3200716G16A-BWHD35" };
        static const char* partByPNY[]      = { "MD16GSD4320016TS",    "MD8GSD4320016TS",     "MD32GSD43200016"     };
        static const char* partByPatriot[]  = { "PVE416G320C8K",       "PVE432G320C8K",       "PEF416G32C8KT"       };

        const char* mfr = ((SpoofRand(seed) % 4) == 0)
                        ? memMfrs[SpoofRand(seed) % (sizeof(memMfrs)/sizeof(memMfrs[0]))]
                        : board.memMfr;
        strncpy_s(req.NewMemoryManufacturer, sizeof(req.NewMemoryManufacturer), mfr, _TRUNCATE);

        {
            static const USHORT spLGA1200[] = {2666, 3000, 3200, 3200};
            static const USHORT spLGA1700[] = {3200, 3600, 4400, 4800};
            static const USHORT spAM4[]     = {3200, 3200, 3600, 3600};
            static const USHORT spAM5[]     = {4800, 5200, 5600, 6000};
            static const USHORT szSlot[]    = {4096, 8192, 8192, 16384};
            ULONG si = SpoofRand(seed) % 4;
            USHORT spd;
            if      (strcmp(board.socket, "AM5")     == 0) spd = spAM5[si];
            else if (strcmp(board.socket, "AM4")     == 0) spd = spAM4[si];
            else if (strcmp(board.socket, "LGA1700") == 0) spd = spLGA1700[si];
            else                                           spd = spLGA1200[si];
            req.NewMemorySize            = szSlot[SpoofRand(seed) % 4];
            req.NewMemorySpeed           = spd;
            req.NewMemoryConfiguredSpeed = spd;
            req.NewMemoryType            = (strcmp(board.socket, "AM5") == 0) ? 0x22 : 0x1A;
        }

        ULONG pi = SpoofRand(seed);
        const char* partNum = "";
        if      (!strcmp(mfr, "Corsair"))   partNum = partByCorsair [pi % 4];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "Kingston"))  partNum = partByKingston[pi % 4];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "Samsung"))   partNum = partBySamsung [pi % 4];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "Crucial"))   partNum = partByCrucial [pi % 4];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "G.Skill"))   partNum = partByGSkill  [pi % 4];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "TeamGroup")) partNum = partByTeam    [pi % 3];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "SK Hynix")) partNum = partByHynix   [pi % 4];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "Micron"))    partNum = partByMicron  [pi % 3];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "Adata"))     partNum = partByAdata   [pi % 3];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "PNY"))       partNum = partByPNY     [pi % 3];
        else if (!strcmp(mfr, "Patriot"))   partNum = partByPatriot [pi % 3];
        strncpy_s(req.NewMemoryPartNumber, sizeof(req.NewMemoryPartNumber), partNum, _TRUNCATE);
    }
    GenerateRandomSerial(req.NewMemorySerial, 8, seed);
    GenerateRandomSerial(req.NewBatterySerial, 10, seed);

    if (!SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_SMBIOS, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
        LOG_ERR("SMBIOS spoof failed");
        return FALSE;
    }

    LOG_OK("SMBIOS UUID spoofed");
    LOG_OK("Board: %s %s  serial=%s", board.mfr, board.boardProd, req.NewBoardSerial);
    LOG_OK("System: %s %s  serial=%s", board.mfr, board.sysProd, req.NewSystemSerial);
    LOG_OK("Processor: %s", req.NewProcessorVersion);
    LOG_OK("Memory serial: %s", req.NewMemorySerial);
    return TRUE;
}

BOOL SpoofCPUID(ULONG* seed) {
    SPOOF_CPUID_REQUEST req = {0};
    req.HideHypervisor = TRUE;
    req.ZeroLeaf3      = TRUE;

    const BoardIdentity* board = g_PickedBoard ? g_PickedBoard : &g_Boards[0];
    BOOL isAMD = (strcmp(board->socket, "AM4") == 0 || strcmp(board->socket, "AM5") == 0);
    SpoofRand(seed);
    ULONG pick = g_PickedCPUModelIdx;
    const char* model = board->cpuModels[pick];

    if (isAMD) {
        strncpy_s(req.VendorString, sizeof(req.VendorString), "AuthenticAMD", _TRUNCATE);
        req.FakeLeaf1EAX = (strcmp(board->socket, "AM5") == 0) ? 0x00A60F12u : 0x00A50F10u;
        int cores = (board->cpuSeries[0] == '5') ? 6 :
                    (board->cpuSeries[0] == '7') ? 8 :
                    (strstr(model, "950") ? 16 : 12);
        _snprintf_s(req.BrandString, sizeof(req.BrandString), _TRUNCATE,
            "AMD Ryzen %s %s %d-Core Processor        ",
            board->cpuSeries, model, cores);
    } else {
        strncpy_s(req.VendorString, sizeof(req.VendorString), "GenuineIntel", _TRUNCATE);
        if (strcmp(board->socket, "LGA1700") == 0) {
            BOOL is13th = (board->cpuModels[0][0] == '1' && board->cpuModels[0][1] == '3')
                       || (board->cpuModels[0][0] == '1' && board->cpuModels[0][1] == '4');
            req.FakeLeaf1EAX = is13th ? 0x000B0671u : 0x00090672u;
        } else {
            req.FakeLeaf1EAX = 0x000A0652u;
        }
        _snprintf_s(req.BrandString, sizeof(req.BrandString), _TRUNCATE,
            "Intel(R) Core(TM) i%s-%s CPU @ %s        ",
            board->cpuSeries, model, board->cpuFreq);
    }
    req.BrandString[48] = '\0';

    if (!SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_CPUID, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
        LOG_WARN("CPUID spoof failed");
        return FALSE;
    }
    LOG_OK("CPUID spoofed: %s %s, leaf1EAX=0x%08X", req.VendorString, model, req.FakeLeaf1EAX);
    return TRUE;
}

```

`spoofer/usermode/board_ident.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include "driver_comms.h"

struct BoardIdentity {
    const char* mfr;
    const char* sysProd;
    const char* boardProd;
    const char* socket;
    const char* cpuSeries;
    const char* cpuModels[6];
    const char* cpuFreq;
    const char* memMfr;
};

extern const BoardIdentity* g_PickedBoard;
extern ULONG g_PickedCPUModelIdx;
extern char  g_PickedBIOSVendor[64];
extern char  g_PickedBIOSVersion[32];
extern char  g_PickedBIOSDate[32];

const BoardIdentity& PickBoardIdentity(ULONG* seed);
BOOL SpoofSMBIOS(ULONG* seed);
BOOL SpoofCPUID(ULONG* seed);

```

`spoofer/usermode/hardware_spoof.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "hardware_spoof.h"
#include <string.h>
#include <winioctl.h>

static const struct { UCHAR b0, b1, b2; int vendor; } g_OUIMap[] = {
    {0x00, 0x1B, 0x21, 0}, {0x8C, 0xEC, 0x4B, 0}, {0xF4, 0x4D, 0x30, 0},
    {0xA0, 0x36, 0x9F, 0}, {0x48, 0x51, 0xB7, 0},
    {0x34, 0x02, 0x86, 1}, {0xAC, 0x17, 0x02, 1}, {0x00, 0x21, 0x6A, 1},
    {0x00, 0xE0, 0x4C, 2}, {0x10, 0x02, 0xB5, 2}, {0x90, 0x2B, 0x34, 2},
    {0xE0, 0x4F, 0x43, 2}, {0x40, 0xB0, 0x76, 2},
    {0x18, 0xE7, 0x28, 3}, {0x60, 0x67, 0x20, 3}, {0x00, 0x90, 0x4C, 3},
    {0xB8, 0x27, 0xEB, 3},
    {0x00, 0x50, 0x43, 4}, {0x58, 0x47, 0xCA, 4},
    {0x5C, 0xE0, 0xC5, 5}, {0x20, 0xCF, 0x30, 5}, {0x74, 0xD4, 0x35, 5},
    {0x28, 0xD2, 0x44, 5}, {0x00, 0x26, 0xB9, 5},
};

BOOL IsVirtualComponentId(const WCHAR* compId) {
    WCHAR lower[128] = {0};
    wcsncpy_s(lower, 128, compId, _TRUNCATE);
    _wcslwr_s(lower, 128);
    return (lower[0] == 0 ||
            wcsncmp(lower, L"ms_", 3) == 0 ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"pppoe")    != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"l2tp")     != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"pptp")     != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"mupe")     != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"ndisuio")  != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"wifidirect") != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"sstp")     != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"tunnel")   != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"tap-")     != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"wintun")   != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"vmware")   != NULL ||
            wcsstr(lower, L"virtualbox") != NULL);
}

BOOL SpoofNICDriverDesc(void) {
    static const WCHAR* intelDescs[] = {
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection I219-V",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection I219-LM",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection (7) I219-V",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection (11) I219-LM",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection (14) I219-V",
        L"Intel(R) Gigabit Network Connection I211-AT",
        L"Intel(R) Ethernet Connection I217-V",
    };
    static const WCHAR* intelWifiDescs[] = {
        L"Intel(R) Wi-Fi 6 AX200 160MHz",
        L"Intel(R) Wi-Fi 6E AX210 160MHz",
        L"Intel(R) Wireless-AC 9560",
        L"Intel(R) Dual Band Wireless-AC 8265",
        L"Intel(R) Wi-Fi 6 AX201 160MHz",
    };
    static const WCHAR* realtekDescs[] = {
        L"Realtek PCIe GbE Family Controller",
        L"Realtek PCIe 2.5GbE Family Controller",
        L"Realtek PCIe GBE Family Controller",
        L"Realtek USB GbE Family Controller",
    };
    static const WCHAR* broadcomDescs[] = {
        L"Broadcom NetXtreme BCM5762 Gigabit Ethernet PCIe",
        L"Broadcom NetLink BCM57788 Gigabit Ethernet PCIe",
        L"Broadcom NetXtreme BCM57766 Gigabit Ethernet PCIe",
    };
    static const WCHAR* marvellDescs[] = {
        L"Marvell Yukon 88E8056 PCI-E Gigabit Ethernet Controller",
        L"Marvell AVASTAR Wireless-AC Network Controller",
    };
    static const WCHAR* qualcommDescs[] = {
        L"Qualcomm Atheros AR8151 PCI-E Gigabit Ethernet Controller",
        L"Qualcomm Atheros AR8161 PCI-E Gigabit Ethernet Controller",
        L"Killer E2500 Gigabit Ethernet Controller",
        L"Killer E3000 2.5Gb Ethernet Controller",
    };

    HKEY hClass;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class\\{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}",
        0, KEY_READ, &hClass) != ERROR_SUCCESS) return FALSE;

    BOOL ok = TRUE;
    WCHAR sub[16]; DWORD enumIdx = 0; ULONG slot = 0;
    while (RegEnumKeyW(hClass, enumIdx++, sub, 16) == ERROR_SUCCESS && slot < 4) {
        if (wcslen(sub) != 4) continue;
        WCHAR path[256];
        swprintf_s(path, 256,
            L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class\\{4D36E972-E325-11CE-BFC1-08002BE10318}\\%s", sub);
        HKEY hDev;
        if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, path, 0,
            KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &hDev) != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;

        WCHAR compId[128] = {0}; DWORD cs = sizeof(compId);
        RegQueryValueExW(hDev, L"ComponentId", NULL, NULL, (BYTE*)compId, &cs);
        if (IsVirtualComponentId(compId)) { RegCloseKey(hDev); continue; }

        int vendorId = 2;
        WCHAR macStr[24] = {0}; DWORD ms = sizeof(macStr);
        if (RegQueryValueExW(hDev, L"NetworkAddress", NULL, NULL, (BYTE*)macStr, &ms) == ERROR_SUCCESS
            && wcslen(macStr) >= 6) {
            WCHAR hex[3] = {macStr[0], macStr[1], 0};
            UCHAR b0 = (UCHAR)wcstoul(hex, NULL, 16);
            hex[0] = macStr[2]; hex[1] = macStr[3];
            UCHAR b1 = (UCHAR)wcstoul(hex, NULL, 16);
            hex[0] = macStr[4]; hex[1] = macStr[5];
            UCHAR b2 = (UCHAR)wcstoul(hex, NULL, 16);
            for (ULONG i = 0; i < sizeof(g_OUIMap)/sizeof(g_OUIMap[0]); i++) {
                if (b0==g_OUIMap[i].b0 && b1==g_OUIMap[i].b1 && b2==g_OUIMap[i].b2) {
                    vendorId = g_OUIMap[i].vendor; break;
                }
            }
            hex[0] = macStr[6]; hex[1] = macStr[7];
            ULONG r = (ULONG)wcstoul(hex, NULL, 16);
            hex[0] = macStr[8]; hex[1] = macStr[9];
            r = r * 31 + (ULONG)wcstoul(hex, NULL, 16);
            hex[0] = macStr[10]; hex[1] = macStr[11];
            r = r * 31 + (ULONG)wcstoul(hex, NULL, 16);

            const WCHAR* desc;
            switch (vendorId) {
            case 0: desc = intelDescs    [r % (sizeof(intelDescs)/sizeof(intelDescs[0]))]; break;
            case 1: desc = intelWifiDescs[r % (sizeof(intelWifiDescs)/sizeof(intelWifiDescs[0]))]; break;
            case 3: desc = broadcomDescs [r % (sizeof(broadcomDescs)/sizeof(broadcomDescs[0]))]; break;
            case 4: desc = marvellDescs  [r % (sizeof(marvellDescs)/sizeof(marvellDescs[0]))]; break;
            case 5: desc = qualcommDescs [r % (sizeof(qualcommDescs)/sizeof(qualcommDescs[0]))]; break;
            default: desc = realtekDescs [r % (sizeof(realtekDescs)/sizeof(realtekDescs[0]))]; break;
            }
            if (RegSetValueExW(hDev, L"DriverDesc", 0, REG_SZ,
                (BYTE*)desc, (DWORD)((wcslen(desc)+1)*sizeof(WCHAR))) != ERROR_SUCCESS)
                ok = FALSE;
            slot++;
        }
        RegCloseKey(hDev);
    }
    RegCloseKey(hClass);
    return ok;
}

static int QueryDiskSerial(ULONG driveIndex, char* out, int maxLen) {
    char devPath[32];
    _snprintf_s(devPath, sizeof(devPath), _TRUNCATE, "\\\\.\\PhysicalDrive%lu", driveIndex);
    HANDLE h = CreateFileA(devPath, 0, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, NULL, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, NULL);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return 0;

    BYTE buf[4096] = {0};
    STORAGE_PROPERTY_QUERY q = {0};
    q.PropertyId = StorageDeviceProperty;
    q.QueryType  = PropertyStandardQuery;
    DWORD br = 0;
    BOOL ok = DeviceIoControl(h, IOCTL_STORAGE_QUERY_PROPERTY, &q, sizeof(q), buf, sizeof(buf), &br, NULL);
    CloseHandle(h);
    if (!ok || br < sizeof(STORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR)) return 0;

    STORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR* desc = (STORAGE_DEVICE_DESCRIPTOR*)buf;
    if (desc->SerialNumberOffset == 0 || desc->SerialNumberOffset >= br) return 0;

    const char* s = (const char*)buf + desc->SerialNumberOffset;
    int len = 0;
    while (len < maxLen - 1 && s[len] != '\0') len++;
    while (len > 0 && s[len - 1] == ' ') len--;
    if (len == 0) return 0;

    strncpy_s(out, maxLen, s, len);
    out[len] = '\0';
    return len;
}

BOOL SpoofDiskSerials(ULONG* seed) {
    BOOL anySuccess = FALSE;
    for (ULONG i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        char origSerial[64] = {0};
        int len = QueryDiskSerial(i, origSerial, sizeof(origSerial));
        if (len == 0) {
            if (i == 0)
                LOG_WARN("Disk 0 serial empty — no disks or access denied");
            break;
        }

        SPOOF_DISK_REQUEST req = {0};
        req.DriveIndex = i;
        strncpy_s(req.OriginalSerial, sizeof(req.OriginalSerial), origSerial, _TRUNCATE);
        GenerateRandomSerial(req.NewSerial, len, seed);
        req.SerialLength = (ULONG)len;

        if (!SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_DISK_SERIAL, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
            LOG_ERR("Failed to spoof disk %lu serial", i);
        } else {
            LOG_OK("Disk %lu: %s -> %s", i, req.OriginalSerial, req.NewSerial);
            anySuccess = TRUE;
        }
    }
    return anySuccess;
}

BOOL SpoofGPUKernel(ULONG* seed, ULONG* outDeviceId, ULONG* outSubSysId, ULONG* outRevision) {
    SPOOF_GPU_REQUEST req = {0};

    ULONG detectedVendor = 0x10DE;
    {
        HKEY hEnum;
        if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\PCI",
            0, KEY_READ, &hEnum) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            WCHAR kn[64]; DWORD ei = 0;
            while (RegEnumKeyW(hEnum, ei++, kn, 64) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                if (_wcsnicmp(kn, L"VEN_1002", 8) == 0) { detectedVendor = 0x1002; break; }
                if (_wcsnicmp(kn, L"VEN_10DE", 8) == 0) { detectedVendor = 0x10DE; break; }
            }
            RegCloseKey(hEnum);
        }
    }

    static const USHORT nvidiaDIDs[] = {
        0x2684, 0x2704, 0x2782, 0x2786, 0x2705, 0x2803,
        0x2882, 0x2204, 0x2208, 0x2206, 0x2482, 0x2484,
        0x2489, 0x2487, 0x2504, 0x1E84, 0x1E87, 0x1F08,
        0x2182, 0x21C4, 0x2184,
    };
    static const USHORT amdDIDs[] = {
        0x744C, 0x747E, 0x7480, 0x73A1,
        0x73BF, 0x73DF, 0x73FF, 0x731F,
        0x5351, 0x67DF,
    };

    req.VendorId = 0;
    if (detectedVendor == 0x1002) {
        req.DeviceId = amdDIDs[SpoofRand(seed) % (sizeof(amdDIDs)/sizeof(amdDIDs[0]))];
    } else {
        req.DeviceId = nvidiaDIDs[SpoofRand(seed) % (sizeof(nvidiaDIDs)/sizeof(nvidiaDIDs[0]))];
    }
    req.SubSysId = (SpoofRand(seed) & 0xFFFF) | ((ULONG)detectedVendor << 16);
    if (detectedVendor == 0x1002) {
        static const UCHAR amdRevs[] = {0xC1, 0xC5, 0xC1, 0xC1};
        req.Revision = amdRevs[SpoofRand(seed) % 4];
    } else {
        static const UCHAR nvidiaRevs[] = {0xA1, 0xA2, 0xA1, 0xA1};
        req.Revision = nvidiaRevs[SpoofRand(seed) % 4];
    }

    if (!SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_GPU_IDS, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
        LOG_WARN("GPU kernel spoof failed");
        return FALSE;
    }
    LOG_OK("GPU IDs spoofed: DID=%04X SubSys=%08X Rev=%02X", req.DeviceId, req.SubSysId, req.Revision);
    *outDeviceId = req.DeviceId;
    *outSubSysId = req.SubSysId;
    *outRevision = req.Revision;
    return TRUE;
}

void SpoofGPURegistryAndPnP(ULONG newDeviceId, ULONG newSubSysId, ULONG newRevision) {
    if (newDeviceId == 0) return;

    ULONG vendorId = (newSubSysId >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
    WCHAR vendorPrefix[16];
    swprintf_s(vendorPrefix, 16, L"VEN_%04X", vendorId ? vendorId : 0x10DE);

    HKEY hEnum;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\PCI", 0, KEY_READ, &hEnum) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {

        WCHAR keyName[256];
        for (DWORD idx = 0;
             RegEnumKeyW(hEnum, idx, keyName, 256) == ERROR_SUCCESS;
             idx++) {

            if (_wcsnicmp(keyName, vendorPrefix, 8) != 0) continue;

            HKEY hDev;
            if (RegOpenKeyExW(hEnum, keyName, 0, KEY_READ, &hDev) != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;

            WCHAR instName[256];
            for (DWORD idx2 = 0;
                 RegEnumKeyW(hDev, idx2, instName, 256) == ERROR_SUCCESS;
                 idx2++) {

                WCHAR fullPath[512];
                wsprintfW(fullPath,
                    L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\PCI\\%s\\%s", keyName, instName);

                HKEY hInst;
                if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, fullPath, 0, KEY_SET_VALUE, &hInst)
                        != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;

                WCHAR id1[128], id2[128], id3[128];
                wsprintfW(id1, L"PCI\\%s&DEV_%04X&SUBSYS_%08X&REV_%02X", vendorPrefix, newDeviceId, newSubSysId, newRevision);
                wsprintfW(id2, L"PCI\\%s&DEV_%04X&SUBSYS_%08X",          vendorPrefix, newDeviceId, newSubSysId);
                wsprintfW(id3, L"PCI\\%s&DEV_%04X",                       vendorPrefix, newDeviceId);

                WCHAR multi[512] = {0};
                ULONG off = 0;
                wcscpy_s(multi + off, 128, id1); off += (ULONG)wcslen(id1) + 1;
                wcscpy_s(multi + off, 128, id2); off += (ULONG)wcslen(id2) + 1;
                wcscpy_s(multi + off, 128, id3); off += (ULONG)wcslen(id3) + 1;
                off++;

                RegSetValueExW(hInst, L"HardwareID", 0, REG_MULTI_SZ, (BYTE*)multi, off * sizeof(WCHAR));
                RegSetValueExW(hInst, L"CompatibleIDs", 0, REG_MULTI_SZ, (BYTE*)multi, off * sizeof(WCHAR));
                RegCloseKey(hInst);
                LOG_OK("GPU PnP HardwareID: %ls\\%ls", keyName, instName);
            }
            RegCloseKey(hDev);
        }
        RegCloseKey(hEnum);
    }

    HKEY hClass;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Class\\"
        L"{4d36e968-e325-11ce-bfc1-08002be10318}\\0000",
        0, KEY_SET_VALUE, &hClass) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {

        WCHAR matchId[128];
        ULONG matchVen = vendorId ? vendorId : 0x10DE;
        wsprintfW(matchId, L"pci\\ven_%04x&dev_%04x&subsys_%08x", matchVen, newDeviceId, newSubSysId);
        RegSetValueExW(hClass, L"MatchingDeviceId", 0, REG_SZ,
                       (BYTE*)matchId, (ULONG)(wcslen(matchId) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR));
        RegCloseKey(hClass);
        LOG_OK("GPU MatchingDeviceId: %ls", matchId);
    }
}

BOOL SpoofVolumeSerial_UM(ULONG* seed) {
    DWORD drives = GetLogicalDrives();
    BOOL  anyOk  = FALSE;

    for (int bit = 0; bit < 26; bit++) {
        if (!(drives & (1u << bit))) continue;

        WCHAR letter[4];
        letter[0] = (WCHAR)(L'A' + bit);
        letter[1] = L':';
        letter[2] = L'\0';

        WCHAR root[4] = { letter[0], L':', L'\\', L'\0' };
        if (GetDriveTypeW(root) != DRIVE_FIXED) continue;

        WCHAR fsName[32] = {0};
        if (!GetVolumeInformationW(root, NULL, 0, NULL, NULL, NULL, fsName, 32)) continue;
        if (_wcsicmp(fsName, L"NTFS") != 0) continue;

        WCHAR devPath[260] = {0};
        if (!QueryDosDeviceW(letter, devPath, 260)) continue;

        DWORD oldSerial = 0;
        GetVolumeInformationW(root, NULL, 0, &oldSerial, NULL, NULL, NULL, 0);

        SPOOF_VOLUME_REQUEST req = {0};
        wcscpy_s(req.VolumePath, 260, devPath);
        req.NewSerialNumber = SpoofRand(seed);

        if (SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_VOLUME_SERIAL, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
            LOG_OK("%lc: volume serial %08X -> %08X", letter[0], oldSerial, req.NewSerialNumber);
            anyOk = TRUE;
        } else {
            LOG_WARN("%lc: volume serial spoof failed (%ls)", letter[0], devPath);
        }
    }

    if (!anyOk)
        LOG_WARN("No NTFS volumes spoofed");
    return anyOk;
}

void SpoofMonitorEDID_All(ULONG* seed) {
    HKEY hDisplay;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\DISPLAY",
        0, KEY_READ, &hDisplay) != ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_WARN("Monitor EDID: cannot open DISPLAY enum key");
        return;
    }

    WCHAR monitorModel[256];
    for (DWORD idx = 0;
         RegEnumKeyW(hDisplay, idx, monitorModel, 256) == ERROR_SUCCESS;
         idx++) {

        HKEY hMon;
        WCHAR monPath[512];
        wsprintfW(monPath, L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\DISPLAY\\%s", monitorModel);
        if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, monPath, 0, KEY_READ, &hMon) != ERROR_SUCCESS)
            continue;

        WCHAR instanceName[256];
        for (DWORD idx2 = 0;
             RegEnumKeyW(hMon, idx2, instanceName, 256) == ERROR_SUCCESS;
             idx2++) {

            WCHAR edidRegPath[512];
            wsprintfW(edidRegPath,
                L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Enum\\DISPLAY\\%s\\%s\\Device Parameters",
                monitorModel, instanceName);

            HKEY hParams;
            if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, edidRegPath, 0, KEY_READ, &hParams)
                    != ERROR_SUCCESS) continue;

            UCHAR origEDID[128] = {0};
            DWORD edidSize = sizeof(origEDID);
            BOOL  hasEDID  = RegQueryValueExW(hParams, L"EDID", NULL, NULL,
                                              origEDID, &edidSize) == ERROR_SUCCESS;
            RegCloseKey(hParams);

            if (!hasEDID || edidSize < 128) continue;

            UCHAR newEDID[128];
            memcpy(newEDID, origEDID, 128);

            ULONG rnd = SpoofRand(seed);
            newEDID[12] = (UCHAR)(rnd >>  0);
            newEDID[13] = (UCHAR)(rnd >>  8);
            newEDID[14] = (UCHAR)(rnd >> 16);
            newEDID[15] = (UCHAR)(rnd >> 24);

            for (int d = 0; d < 4; d++) {
                UCHAR* desc = newEDID + 54 + d * 18;
                if (desc[0] == 0x00 && desc[1] == 0x00 &&
                    desc[2] == 0x00 && desc[3] == 0xFF) {
                    char rndSerial[14];
                    GenerateRandomSerial(rndSerial, 13, seed);
                    memset(desc, 0, 18);
                    desc[3] = 0xFE;
                    desc[4] = 0x00;
                    memcpy(desc + 5, rndSerial, 13);
                }
            }

            newEDID[127] = 0;
            UCHAR sum = 0;
            for (int b = 0; b < 127; b++) sum += newEDID[b];
            newEDID[127] = (UCHAR)(256 - (sum & 0xFF));

            SPOOF_EDID_REQUEST req = {0};
            wsprintfW(req.MonitorDevicePath, L"%s\\%s", monitorModel, instanceName);
            memcpy(req.OriginalEDID, origEDID, 128);
            memcpy(req.NewEDID,      newEDID,  128);

            if (SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_MONITOR_EDID, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
                LOG_OK("Monitor EDID %ls: %02X%02X -> %02X%02X",
                    req.MonitorDevicePath,
                    origEDID[12], origEDID[13], newEDID[12], newEDID[13]);
            } else {
                HKEY hParamsWrite;
                if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, edidRegPath, 0, KEY_SET_VALUE,
                                  &hParamsWrite) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                    RegSetValueExW(hParamsWrite, L"EDID", 0, REG_BINARY, newEDID, 128);
                    RegCloseKey(hParamsWrite);
                    LOG_OK("Monitor EDID %ls (fallback)", req.MonitorDevicePath);
                } else {
                    LOG_WARN("Monitor EDID spoof failed: %ls", req.MonitorDevicePath);
                }
            }
        }
        RegCloseKey(hMon);
    }
    RegCloseKey(hDisplay);
}

BOOL SpoofBluetooth(void) {
    if (!SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_BLUETOOTH_MAC, NULL, 0, NULL, 0, NULL)) {
        LOG_WARN("Bluetooth MAC spoof failed");
        return FALSE;
    }
    LOG_OK("Bluetooth MAC spoofed");
    return TRUE;
}

        LOG_WARN("EFI variable spoof failed");

    }
    LOG_OK("EFI variables spoofed");

}

}
```

`spoofer/usermode/registry_spoof.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "registry_spoof.h"
#include "board_ident.h"
#include <time.h>
#include <string.h>

BOOL SpoofRegistryString(HKEY root, LPCWSTR subKey, LPCWSTR valueName, LPCWSTR newValue) {
    HKEY hKey;
    LONG result = RegOpenKeyExW(root, subKey, 0, KEY_QUERY_VALUE | KEY_SET_VALUE, &hKey);
    if (result != ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_ERR("Failed to open %ls: %ld", subKey, result);
        return FALSE;
    }
    WCHAR oldVal[256] = {0};
    DWORD oldSz = sizeof(oldVal), oldType = 0;
    if (RegQueryValueExW(hKey, valueName, NULL, &oldType, (BYTE*)oldVal, &oldSz) != ERROR_SUCCESS
            || oldType != REG_SZ)
        wcscpy_s(oldVal, 256, L"(unset)");
    result = RegSetValueExW(hKey, valueName, 0, REG_SZ,
                            (BYTE*)newValue, (DWORD)(wcslen(newValue) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR));
    RegCloseKey(hKey);
    if (result == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_OK("Spoofed %ls: %ls -> %ls", valueName, oldVal, newValue);
        return TRUE;
    }
    LOG_ERR("Failed to set %ls: %ld", valueName, result);
    return FALSE;
}

BOOL SpoofRegistryDWORD(HKEY root, LPCWSTR subKey, LPCWSTR valueName, DWORD newValue) {
    HKEY hKey;
    LONG result = RegOpenKeyExW(root, subKey, 0, KEY_SET_VALUE, &hKey);
    if (result != ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        LOG_ERR("Failed to open %ls for DWORD write: %ld", subKey, result);
        return FALSE;
    }
    result = RegSetValueExW(hKey, valueName, 0, REG_DWORD, (BYTE*)&newValue, sizeof(DWORD));
    RegCloseKey(hKey);
    if (result != ERROR_SUCCESS)
        LOG_ERR("Failed to set DWORD %ls\\%ls: %ld", subKey, valueName, result);
    return result == ERROR_SUCCESS;
}

BOOL SpoofMachineGuid(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newGuid[64];
    GenerateRandomGUID(newGuid, seed);
    return SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Cryptography", L"MachineGuid", newGuid);
}

BOOL SpoofProductId(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newPid[32];
    char serial[6];
    GenerateRandomSerial(serial, 5, seed);
    wsprintfW(newPid, L"00326-%hs-00000-AA%03d", serial, SpoofRand(seed) % 1000);
    return SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion", L"ProductId", newPid);
}

BOOL SpoofInstallDate(ULONG* seed) {
    DWORD now = (DWORD)time(NULL);
    DWORD offset = SpoofRand(seed) % (365 * 2 * 86400);
    return SpoofRegistryDWORD(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion", L"InstallDate", now - offset);
}

BOOL SpoofBuildGUID(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newGuid[64];
    GenerateRandomGUID(newGuid, seed);
    return SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion", L"BuildGUID", newGuid);
}

BOOL SpoofHwProfileGuid(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newGuid[64];
    GenerateRandomGUID(newGuid, seed);
    return SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\IDConfigDB\\Hardware Profiles\\0001",
        L"HwProfileGuid", newGuid);
}

BOOL SpoofHostname(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR oldHostname[MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH + 1] = {0};
    DWORD oldLen = MAX_COMPUTERNAME_LENGTH + 1;
    if (!GetComputerNameW(oldHostname, &oldLen))
        wcscpy_s(oldHostname, _countof(oldHostname), L"(unknown)");

    char suffix[8];
    GenerateRandomSerial(suffix, 7, seed);
    WCHAR newHostname[32];
    wsprintfW(newHostname, L"DESKTOP-%hs", suffix);

    SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\ComputerName\\ComputerName",
        L"ComputerName", newHostname);
    SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\ComputerName\\ActiveComputerName",
        L"ComputerName", newHostname);
    SetComputerNameW(newHostname);
    LOG_OK("Hostname: %ls -> %ls", oldHostname, newHostname);
    return TRUE;
}

BOOL SpoofSusClientId(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newGuid[64];
    GenerateRandomGUID(newGuid, seed);
    return SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\WindowsUpdate",
        L"SusClientId", newGuid);
}

BOOL SpoofSqmMachineId(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newGuid[64];
    GenerateRandomGUID(newGuid, seed);
    return SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\SQMClient", L"MachineId", newGuid);
}

BOOL SpoofExtendedRegistryKernel(ULONG* seed) {
    SPOOF_EXTENDED_REG_REQUEST req = {0};

    strncpy_s(req.NewBIOSVendor,      sizeof(req.NewBIOSVendor),      g_PickedBIOSVendor,  _TRUNCATE);
    strncpy_s(req.NewBIOSReleaseDate, sizeof(req.NewBIOSReleaseDate),  g_PickedBIOSDate,    _TRUNCATE);
    strncpy_s(req.NewBIOSVersion,     sizeof(req.NewBIOSVersion),      g_PickedBIOSVersion, _TRUNCATE);

    {
        const BoardIdentity& board = g_PickedBoard ? *g_PickedBoard : PickBoardIdentity(seed);
        SpoofRand(seed);
        ULONG pick = g_PickedCPUModelIdx;
        BOOL isAMD = (strcmp(board.socket, "AM4") == 0 || strcmp(board.socket, "AM5") == 0);

        strncpy_s(req.NewSystemManufacturer, sizeof(req.NewSystemManufacturer), board.mfr,     _TRUNCATE);
        strncpy_s(req.NewSystemProductName,  sizeof(req.NewSystemProductName),  board.sysProd, _TRUNCATE);
        MultiByteToWideChar(CP_ACP, 0, board.boardProd, -1, req.NewComputerHardwareId, 128);

        if (isAMD) {
            _snprintf_s(req.NewProcessorNameString, sizeof(req.NewProcessorNameString), _TRUNCATE,
                        "AMD Ryzen %s %s                 ",
                        board.cpuSeries, board.cpuModels[pick]);
        } else {
            _snprintf_s(req.NewProcessorNameString, sizeof(req.NewProcessorNameString), _TRUNCATE,
                        "Intel(R) Core(TM) i%s-%s CPU @ %s",
                        board.cpuSeries, board.cpuModels[pick], board.cpuFreq);
        }
    }

    {
        UCHAR uuid[16];
        GenerateRandomUUID(uuid, seed);
        swprintf_s(req.NewSusClientId, 48,
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            uuid[0],uuid[1],uuid[2],uuid[3],uuid[4],uuid[5],uuid[6],uuid[7],
            uuid[8],uuid[9],uuid[10],uuid[11],uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);
        GenerateRandomUUID(uuid, seed);
        swprintf_s(req.NewSqmMachineId, 64,
            L"{%02x%02x%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x-%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x%02x}",
            uuid[0],uuid[1],uuid[2],uuid[3],uuid[4],uuid[5],uuid[6],uuid[7],
            uuid[8],uuid[9],uuid[10],uuid[11],uuid[12],uuid[13],uuid[14],uuid[15]);
    }

    if (!SendDriverIOCTL(IOCTL_SPOOF_EXTENDED_REG, &req, sizeof(req), NULL, 0, NULL)) {
        LOG_WARN("Extended registry spoof failed");
        return FALSE;
    }
    LOG_OK("Extended registry spoofed (BIOS, SystemInformation, CentralProcessor, IDs)");
    return TRUE;
}

void SpoofMRTGuid(ULONG* seed) {
    WCHAR newGuid[64];
    GenerateRandomGUID(newGuid, seed);
    SpoofRegistryString(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\RemovalTools\\MRT", L"GUID", newGuid);
}

void CleanSusClientIdBinding(void) {
    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegOpenKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\WindowsUpdate",
        0, KEY_SET_VALUE, &hKey) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        if (RegDeleteValueW(hKey, L"SusClientIdValidation") == ERROR_SUCCESS)
            LOG_OK("SusClientIdValidation removed");
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
    }
}

void SpoofDeliveryOptHWID(ULONG* seed) {
    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegCreateKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\DeliveryOptimization\\Config",
        0, NULL, 0, KEY_SET_VALUE, NULL, &hKey, NULL) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        ULONG val = SpoofRand(seed);
        RegSetValueExW(hKey, L"DOH", 0, REG_DWORD, (BYTE*)&val, sizeof(val));
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
        LOG_OK("DeliveryOptimization HWID randomised");
    }
}

void SpoofSPPClientMachineId(ULONG* seed) {
    HKEY hKey;
    if (RegCreateKeyExW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE,
        L"SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion\\SoftwareProtectionPlatform",
        0, NULL, 0, KEY_SET_VALUE, NULL, &hKey, NULL) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        WCHAR guid[64];
        GenerateRandomGUID(guid, seed);
        RegSetValueExW(hKey, L"ClientMachineId", 0, REG_SZ,
            (BYTE*)guid, (DWORD)((wcslen(guid) + 1) * sizeof(WCHAR)));
        RegCloseKey(hKey);
        LOG_OK("SPP ClientMachineId randomised: %ls", guid);
    }
}
```
